The Green Forest Sect and Tang family glared madly at the Mount Hua guys, but Namgung Do-Wei’s gaze, who had asked the question, was fixed on Chung Myung.

“So... what you mean is...”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung nodded.

“Even if people try to run together, they should at least keep the same pace. Frankly, when the high priest appeared in the Southern Lands this time, how many could actually fight there?”

Chung Myung’s eyes fixed on the Tang family.

“At best, one from the Tang family.”

The Tang family members bowed their heads deeply.

The Sichuan Tang family is never belittled for their skill, but this Southern lands incident is different. Considering what happened there, among the Tang family, only Tang Gunak is deemed capable of performing in Hangzhou.

This time, Chung Myung’s gaze fixed on Im So-Byeong.

“One from the Green Forest Sect.”

Unlike the Tang family, Im So-Byeong proudly lifted his chin.

“That’s how bandits are! What more do you want?”

Chung Myung, looking at Im So-Byeong with a displeased face, suddenly turned his head to Namgung Do-Wei.

“One from the Namgung Family.”

“......”

“And that too is a shaky half-step.”

Namgung Do-Wei bowed his head deeply.

Before Plum Blossom Island, when Namgung Hwang and the elders were alive, the Namgung Family would be different, but now they couldn’t deny that.

It was a grievance worth feeling, but Namgung Do-Wei knew that grievance couldn’t make up for the lack of skill.

“But, what? Heavenly Friends Alliance? Heaavenly Frieeends Alliaaance? Frieeends?”

Chung Myung’s eyes gleamed.

“Friends are friends who freeze and die together. If the Demonic Cult bastards invade now, you’ll probably say, ‘We’ll cheer from behind, so Mount Hua, you go solve it,’ and just clap from behind. You bastards!”

“......”

“Do you think I’ll stand for that?”

Chung Myung’s madness reached its peak and raged.

“This time, no one dies alone! If we die, we die together; if we live, we live together! When Mount Hua goes to die, you all go to die with them. That’s friendship!”

“Ah, shut up, sahyung!”

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul glanced at each other for a moment.

“Did you open your mouth?”

“No?”

“......”

“Anyway!”

Chung Myung shouted angrily and glared.

“Tang family!”

The Tang family members flinched and shrank their necks.

“Namgung family!”

Namgung Do-Wei bowed his head deeply.

“Bandit bastards!”

“...No. Don’t discriminate, at least call us the Green Forest Sect...”

Im So-Byeong protested, but Chung Myung didn’t hear that part.

“Before those Four Sects Alliance bastards or Demonic Cult bastards invade, I’ll at least make you earn your keep, so just shut up and train as you’re told.”

“Um, um...”

At that moment, Tang Pae, the young master of the Sichuan Tang family, timidly raised his hand.

“What?”

“Well... the taoist might find it hard to understand, but the Sichuan Tang family focuses on poisons and secret techniques, so our training methods differ from typical martial arts. So we prefer to train autonomously...”

Upon hearing that, the Tang family members looked at Tang Pae with hopeful eyes.

But Chung Myung sharply crushed that hope.

“It’s fine.”

“...Yes?”

“I said it’s fine.”

“...Ah, no. That...”

“The Tang family lord agreed to this from the start, so just be quiet.”

Tang Pae’s head drooped. No wonder he hadn’t been seen since dawn; he must have already sold off his retainers.

“No need to worry.”

Chung Myung looked at everyone with eyes full of determination.

“It takes me some time to find problems, but I’m very skilled at solving them. So... half a year. Before that half year passes, I’ll make every one of you into human beings without exception.”

“......”

“Only then will we.”

A very gentle smile spread across Chung Myung’s lips.

“Truly become friends.”

At that moment, everyone had only one question: could the word ‘friend’ really be used like that...?

“Now, then let’s begin...”

At that moment, Im So-Byeong raised his hand high. His eyes flashed with poison.

“What? No objections...”

“No, that’s not it!”

As Chung Myung tilted his head in confusion, Im So-Byeong shouted with a voice full of malice.

“When are the Beast Palace and Ice Palace troops coming?”

“......”

“We have to be friends. They’re part of the Heavenly Friends Alliance too!”

Hearing that resolute voice that no one dies alone, Chung Myung looked at Im So-Byeong with genuine admiration.

“...As expected of an Evil Sect bastard. You hold different talents.”

Everyone secretly agreed with that.

“Well, I’ll take care of that.”

Chung Myung smirked and stood up.

“Now, let’s start. The process of becoming true friends. Hehehehe.”

Those who saw that smile seriously considered leaving the alliance. Very seriously.

“Aaaaargh!”

A desperate scream came from outside the door. Startled, Tang Gunak peeked outside to see. It seemed as if a familiar voice was mixed in with the scream he just heard.

But he soon returned his head to its original position.

“...Are you alright?”

When Hyun Jong asked gently, Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

“I’m fine.”

“Still, it can’t be an easy task to accept.”

“It’s something I’ve long hoped for.”

Tang Gunak reached out and picked up the teacup before him. Confirming the tea had cooled slightly, he gently drew up his internal qi. The cooled tea soon began to emit white steam curling upward.

Savoring the aroma, Tang Gunak smiled faintly.

“As I always feel, plum blossom tea has a truly wonderful fragrance.”

“Thank you.”

Tang Gunak took a sip of the tea and set the cup down on the table, then spoke.

“In truth, this is something I’ve been thinking about for quite some time. At this rate, a day will inevitably come when we can no longer keep up with Mount Hua.”

“You are too humble. Who would dare compare the Sichuan Tang Family to Mount Hua?”

Tang Gunak gave a wry smile.

Surely, the world might still place the Tang family slightly above Mount Hua. Even when the Heavenly Friends Alliance was formed, except for a few who knew the intimate details, most believed the Sichuan Tang family had put Mount Hua at the forefront and held the real power, didn’t they?

But once their deeds in the south spread, even that mistaken perception will change.

‘Above all...’

What matters is not perception. Isn’t it how Tang Gunak views the current situation?

Mount Hua grows stronger day by day, but the Tang family remains in the same place. Until now, the Tang family was the one waiting for the day Mount Hua would stand beside them. But from this incident onward, that position has reversed.

“Alliance Leader. No... Sect leader.”

Hyun Jong looked at Tang Gunak with deep eyes at the changed title.

“If things continue like this, the gap between Mount Hua and the Tang family will widen.”

“...Tang family lord.”

“Refusing to acknowledge that is neither pride nor anything else; it’s merely the stubbornness of a petty person ignoring reality.”

Hyun Jong quietly met Tang Gunak’s gaze.

Though Tang Gunak spoke calmly, entrusting the training of the disciples to others is no easy matter. In fact, even Hyun Jong himself doubted he could do it.

Seeing Tang Gunak speak so calmly about it naturally inspired respect.

“Tang family lord, if you had the will, wouldn’t it have been possible?”

Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

“I appreciate your high regard... but if it had been possible, there would have been no reason for me not to have strengthened the Tang family’s forces until now.”

“...I have been rude.”

“Not at all.”

Tang Gunak calmly shook his head.

“I also learned from observing the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint that exceptional martial arts skill and the ability to teach others are entirely separate matters.”

This was Tang Gunak’s sincere truth, without addition or subtraction.

Of course, from the first time he saw Chung Myung, he suspected he was a talent who would elevate Mount Hua. At that time, Chung Myung displayed martial prowess and a chilling mindset beyond his years. But...

‘Even that underestimated the Mount Hua’s Sword Saints; true skill.’

Even Tang Gunak never imagined Mount Hua would become this strong.

Watching Chung Myung develop Mount Hua, even Tang Gunak, who held only goodwill toward him, sometimes felt inferiority and self-doubt.

An ordinary person might express that inferiority as hostility toward the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, but Tang Gunak was no fool. He knew the best way for him and for the Tang family.

“If it were something I could do, I would have done it. But if I step in now and push the disciples, it will only sound like the Tang Family Lord’s is pressuring the Tang family members out of fear of Mount Hua’s growth, both internally and externally.”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong was silent. Upon reflection, it made perfect sense.

“So I believe this is the right approach.”

“But lord...”

“At least outwardly.”

Tang Gunak smiled slyly.

“Inwardly, it’s a bit different. If I personally step in and push the kids but no results come, won’t I be the only one embarrassed?”

“...”

“Face and pride are a useless thing, but sometimes it must be kept. Outwardly, it looks like I am commanding the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint as an instructor, so for me, it couldn’t be better.”

Seeing Tang Gunak’s sly smile, Hyun Jong sighed.

“...That kid makes things difficult for many people.”

“It’s quite meaningful coming from the Alliance Leader.”

Hyun Jong gave a wry smile at Tang Gunak’s words.

That remark implied that Hyun Jong himself might be the one having the hardest time.

“It’s hard to deny.”

Chung Myung’s existence was like a blessing to Hyun Jong. But at the same time, it was like a dagger pointed at his throat.

An outstanding subordinate is always like that. Someone as excessively talented as Chung Myung always stabs those above. Therefore, those in power must always reflect on themselves and question whether they are thinking correctly.

Overcoming that burden was certainly no ordinary matter.

“Thank you, family lord.”

Lost in thought, Hyun Jong bowed his head toward Tang Gunak.

“Ah, no. Why are you doing this all of a sudden?”

Tang Gunak waved his hand in embarrassment. Then Hyun Jong raised his head and looked at Tang Gunak with deep eyes.

“Though it was an incredibly bold request, you accepted it without anger... As the Sect leader of Mount Hua, I cannot do anything but thank you.”

“I think there is some misunderstanding, Alliance Leader.”

“...You say misunderstanding?”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak smiled faintly.

“It wasn’t that the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint asked me to let him teach the Tang family. I asked the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint to take charge of the Tang family a little.”

“...I see.”

But Hyun Jong’s expression did not change at all.

“Still, my gratitude remains. That means you, Tang family lord, understood the hidden meaning in Chung Myung’s words and stepped forward so that the boy wouldn’t have to speak unpleasantly, right?”

“...Alliance Leader, you are flattering me.”

Tang Gunak smiled bitterly.

“You don’t have to, Alliance Leader.”

Then he glanced toward the door.

“In truth, I lack courage. Unlike the Green Forest King, I don’t have the guts to do exactly as the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint commands, so here I am, just drinking tea with you, Alliance Leader.”

“That is... somewhat inappropriate...”

“Not really different. The truly remarkable one is the Green Forest King. If he asserts his authority, even Mount Hua cannot help but acknowledge him. Moreover, didn’t he perform greatly in Hangzhou this time?”

“Indeed.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

Setting aside Im So-Byeong’s character, the title of Green Forest King alone grants him enough respect.

By steadily taking over the Green Forest inherited from his predecessor, he had already proven his qualifications as Green Forest King. Naturally, Im So-Byeong was respected everywhere as the leader of a world-dominating sect.

Yet such a person was rolling in the dirt like that.

“There shouldn’t be a need to go that far...”

“That’s to prevent any backlash.”

Tang Gunak smiled faintly. He had been entangled with Im So-Byeong in the Yangtze for the past three years, so he had come to understand what kind of person Im So-Byeong was.

“Though the Evil Sect is a place where everything is decided by the logic of the strong surviving, that mostly applies within the Evil Sect itself.”

“Hmm.”

“No matter how strong the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is, after all, he is just a disciple, he is not even the sect leader of Mount Hua. No one would be pleased to follow such an order, even if he is no ordinary disciple.”

Hyun Jong nodded. From the perspective of the Green Forest sect followers, it was only natural.

“But if the Green Forest King Im So-Byeong trains at the forefront without complaint, there will be no room for dissatisfaction. The Green Forest King is probably aiming for that.”

“...He is a thoughtful person.”

“Unlike how he appears on the surface.”

Tang Gunak’s eyes sank deeply.

“Of course, the Green Forest King also intends to strengthen the power of the Green Forest to solidify his control over it. At the same time, he is indebting the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

Tang Gunak spoke gently, then smiled bitterly and sighed lightly.

“However, even with such intentions, it is certainly not an easy task.”

“Yes. I think he is an extraordinary person.”

Hyun Jong’s expression was somewhat complex and subtle.

The more you get to know him, the more you watch, the more different sides you see in Im So-Byeong. Isn’t it clear how much the evaluation has changed since he first joined Mount Hua?

“Extraordinary...”

It was then.

“I’m a patient, you damn bastard!”

“Patient? Patient? Oh yeah, you Evil Sect bastard! I’ll show you what a real patient is! Come here, you punk!”

“Eeeek!”

Tang Gunak and Hyun Jong looked toward the door with sullen faces as if by agreement. After silently listening to the screams for a while, Tang Gunak quietly finished his words.

“...He is an extraordinary person.”

“Yes...”

Strangely, his voice lacked strength.

“Ahem. Anyway.”

Clearing his throat, Tang Gunak spoke again.

“Since the Green Forest King is working so hard, I cannot just sit and watch. The Tang Family intends to cooperate fully with the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s plans this time.”

“Thank you, family lord.”

Tang Gunak gave a peculiar smile.

“I’ve realized anew that people have their capacities. Setting aside whether something is realistically possible or difficult, the mere fact that the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint can conceive such plans means they are no ordinary people.”

“Hmm... In many ways.”

“Yes. In many ways.”

A subtle expression passed over both their faces.

It would be nice if they were only extraordinary in good ways...

“That’s a grateful story.”

“...Yes?”

“On the surface, it just means that other sects should share the responsibility for Mount Hua’s defeat. But isn’t the meaning contained within entirely different?”

Hyun Jong nodded with a heavy expression.

“That’s right.”

“Not just anyone, but the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint...”

Tang Gunak hesitated briefly, then continued.

“Didn’t the other sects belonging to the Heavenly Friends Alliance also declare that they would bear their share?”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes and nodded.

“When the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint first formed the Heavenly Friends Alliance, the logic they presented to me was ‘Those who benefit each other can become friends.’ But... this time, the word ‘benefit’ was omitted.”

“You knew that.”

“That’s why it’s remarkable.”

Tang Gunak laughed as if he couldn’t handle it.

“Who in the world would dare say they will embrace all beyond profit and logic? Only... yes. Only the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint can say such a thing.”

“...”

“So how could one not respect them?”

Outside the door, the voice of a young friend was heard repeatedly. Within that sharp voice was a subtle affection that was hard to detect without careful listening.

“I always thought he didn’t fit with the Taoists, but it seems I was mistaken. The great Tao is invisible to ordinary eyes, and if you call the Tao the Tao, it ceases to be the Tao...”

Tang Gunak finished his words with a gentle smile.

“Only now do I truly understand.”

Hearing those words, Hyun Jong quietly closed his eyes.

“I think so too.”

Sometimes, one is surprised by people.

When seeing someone think thoughts that ordinary people would not, or think in a direction different from ordinary people.

But the greater surprise is when you see with your own eyes someone who sees farther than ordinary people.

“We feared the Demonic cult. Along with that, we worried about the unification of the Evil Sect and the division of the Nine Great Sects. Therefore, we were flustered and hurried. What we thought then was how we should deal with them from now on.”

At Tang Gunak’s words, Hyun Jong nodded.

“That is an accurate statement.”

“But only the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was seeing something different. Not how to deal with the demonic cult, but how we ourselves must change. Similar, yet a completely different story, isn’t it?”

Tang Gunak, who was speaking, sighed.

“Truly strange......”

“Hmm.”

Though it was an unexpected remark, only Hyun Jong nodded as if he understood its meaning.

“Sometimes when I look at the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint I wonder how a person can think that way. How can someone who has walked such a perfect path lack faith in themselves so much?”

“...Sect leader.”

Tang Gunak’s gaze shifted slightly upward. It seemed as if he was looking somewhere beyond the ceiling.

“For the Heavenly Friends Alliance, it is a blessed matter. From Mount Hua’s perspective, it is certainly welcome. The existence of such a person leads everyone. But......”

Tang Gunak’s eyes darkened as he hesitated briefly.

“Even from the standpoint of the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint... and the individual named Chung Myung, whether that is truly welcome......”

His voice carried a clear sense of regret.

“I do not know well, Alliance Leader. Perhaps it is... a sad matter.”

Hyun Jong slowly closed his eyes.

Tang Gunak’s words seemed to pierce the core of the individual named Chung Myung.

Of course, on the surface, Chung Myung appears as the embodiment of self-confidence. Without doubt that he might be wrong, he always presents the right answer and moves forward without hesitation. Moreover, he is a superhuman who leads not only himself but others as well.

But what lies deep within that outward appearance?

It is doubt and anxiety bordering on self-contempt, and... fear.

Hyun Jong still cannot forget the shock he felt when he first glimpsed Chung Myung’s inner self. Even though Chung Myung’s arrogance was sky-high and no one could blame him for being excessive, Hyun Jong discovered that Chung Myung regarded himself as less than a worm. Anyone other than Hyun Jong would have been surprised.

Among the many reasons Hyun Jong refrains from delving into Chung Myung’s past, this was the greatest.

No matter what Chung Myung has seen or experienced, even if he committed unforgivable sins... he regrets and suffers so deeply.

Not only as a sect leader but as a single Taoist, how could one pry open the wounds of someone who has suffered so long and deeply?

A long silence passed. Both wore thoughtful expressions.

Tang Gunak slowly resumed speaking.

“Shaolin prioritized its own gains above all else and even split an already established alliance.”

“......”

“And the Ten Thousand People Clan is subjugating even the allied sects under its command. It may seem right for now... but thinking in reverse, they are following the footsteps of the great Evil Sect that once flaunted its power but eventually vanished into history.”

Hyun Jong nodded heavily.

“On the other hand, the method chosen by the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is a completely different path. He does not build walls between sects, share what all he has, and no one stands in a superior position... Yes, that is friendship.”

Tang Gunak smiled.

Until now, Chung Myung always favored other sects, but deep inside, Mount Hua was always foremost. Mount Hua’s harsh training while neglecting the strengthening of other sects’ forces is proof of that.

When threatened by the rise of the Four Sects Alliance, the first thing he did was to seal Mount Hua disciples and strengthen them, leaving other sects alone. Doesn’t that clearly show his inner thoughts?

But now Chung Myung has begun reaching out not only to Mount Hua but also to other sects within the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

To some, it may seem like overstepping or excessive interference in matters. But considering it, it is a huge sacrifice from Chung Myung’s perspective. He is someone who objectively evaluates his own abilities more than anyone.

Then Hyun Jong smiled slightly and said.

“Come to think of it, the young lord of the Namgung Family... might have awakened Chung Myung’s thoughts a bit.”

“I don’t think there was no influence.”

It was probably the first time for Chung Myung as well. The experience of a sect as significant as the Namgung Family approaching humbly to grow.

So Chung Myung, who had respected each sect’s independence, has also slightly expanded the scope of what he can do.

“Family lord......”

Hyun Jong carefully chose his words as if pondering. Then he asked.

“What do you think will be the outcome of what that child is trying to do?”

“Well.”

Tang Gunak smiled subtly.

“To be honest, I don’t know. No one in the murim world has ever attempted this.”

“Hmm.”

He then looked at Hyun Jong with clear eyes and said.

“But the situation we face is also unprecedented. The Demonic Cult may erupt and invade the Central Plains at any time, the Nine Great Sects, which should be at the center of the response, are divided and weak, and the Four Sects Alliance, which is hard to expect cooperation from, grows stronger day by day.”

“......”

“In this situation, the probability that the Heavenly Friends Alliance will overcome everything without sacrifice is low. Honestly, even with sacrifice, resolving this situation will be a difficult task.”

“...That’s right.”

Tang Gunak smiled.

“Then, even if it might be like grasping at straws, shouldn’t we try everything we can? Even if it has never been attempted before.”

Hyun Jong also smiled back.

“That is right. All I can do is silently support what that child wants to do.”

“Don’t say it like that. At least for the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, there is nothing more powerful than that silent support.”

A faint embarrassed expression appeared on Hyun Jong’s face.

“There is much to be done.”

“Yes. First, we should inform the Southern Beast Palace and Northern Ice Palace of this and request their joining. In fact... it is true that we have somewhat neglected them unconsciously.”

A sigh escaped from Tang Gunak’s mouth.

It was never intentional avoidance. But since the Beast Palace and Ice Palace are located far from the Central Plains, it was difficult to ask for help rashly.

But from the perspective of the two palaces, that so-called consideration might have made them feel that most of the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s actions were led by Mount Hua and the Dang family.

Now that it has been declared to embrace everyone, they must never feel alienated.

“The Beast Palace Lord might find it a bit bothersome...”

At Hyun Jong’s words, Tang Gunak smiled bitterly.

“That might be so. But it is something for all to endure. Authority comes with responsibility. He would understand that much.”

Hyun Jong nodded and sighed.

Whenever Chung Myung faces a major event, upheaval occurs, small in Mount Hua, large in the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

But one must not blame him. Chung Myung has repeatedly overturned and rebuilt himself and the Heavenly Friends Alliance to reach this point.

What Hyun Jong must do is not to scold that fact but to allow Chung Myung to freely express his will.

“However......”

Tang Gunak opened his mouth with a slightly different expression than before.

“There are concerns. Internal issues are internal issues, but the greatest worry is probably external.”

“...You mean the Nine Great Sects and the Four Sects Alliance?”

“Yes.”

Tang Gunak’s eyes narrowed slightly.

“The truth of this Hangzhou Demon Fire will soon spread throughout the world. If that happens, the status of Mount Hua and the Four Sects Alliance that participated will rise immeasurably. Then, the ones most uncomfortable with that fact will be......”

“The Nine Great Sects, I suppose.”

“Yes. And it might be beyond what you imagine, Alliance Leader.”

When Hyun Jong looked with questioning eyes, Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

“What Shaolin never wanted to lose was the idea of ‘Shaolin protecting the murim world.’ But this incident has taken that idea away to Mount Hua.”

“Ah......”

Hyun Jong nodded with a slightly complicated expression. At that moment, Tang Gunak, who was about to say something, covered his mouth with his wide sleeve. His shoulders began to tremble faintly. Hyun Jong widened his eyes and asked.

“Why all of a sudden?”

“Ah, sorry. I know I shouldn’t, but thinking about how that guy, the abbot is boiling mad makes me keep laughing.”

It was an unbelievably frivolous remark coming from Tang Gunak.

But Hyun Jong fully understood Tang Gunak’s mood. He too found it hard to contain a smile imagining the abbot raging wildly.

He composed his expression and said.

“A pitiful man.”

“Indeed. He is not inherently foolish. He just knows his own excellence and has a strong pride.”

In this light, the abbot stands opposite Chung Myung.

The abbot is also extremely talented. Among the millennium Shaolin talents, he stood out and ultimately became the sect leader, so he is by no means lacking.

‘That is why.’

The abbot never thinks he could be wrong. He never abandons his conviction that he is right. Isn’t that a perfect contrast to Chung Myung, who lives with the dagger of doubt that he might be wrong?

“So we must be careful. We don’t know what the abbot, wounded in pride, might do.”

Hyun Jong nodded with a heavy heart.

It is frustrating, but the Nine Great Sects nearby are a more immediate threat than the Demonic Cult aiming to destroy the Central Plains or the Four Sects Alliance eyeing the North.

Hyun Jong spoke with a firmer and more resolute voice than before.

“No matter what they think.”

At the sudden change in tone, Tang Gunak’s eyes across from him also brightened.

“No one will be able to lay a finger on those children. That is what I must do as the sect leader of Mount Hua and the leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Those words clearly conveyed his resolve. Tang Gunak smiled and said.

“I will also do my best to help, Alliance Leader.”

Perhaps even this movement of the Heavenly Friends Alliance is just a small change. Indeed, considering the turbulent martial world situation, it is a minor change.

But Tang Gunak repeatedly affirmed that the end of this change will never be insignificant.

“Ughhhhh...”

“S-spar... me...”

“No... just kill me...”

The members of the Tang family and the bandits of The Green Forest lie face down on the ground, gasping for breath.

Where else could one see the green-clad retainers of the Tang family and the similarly grass-colored elite The Green Forest warriors all together, dying like this?

Chung Myung smiled contentedly.

“This is the true scene of sect harmony.”

Namgung Do-Wei silently squeezed his eyes shut at those words.

No matter how many times he saw it, he was always amazed at how this was even possible.

The Sichuan Tang family are a prestigious family known throughout the murim world. Naturally, their training is extremely harsh.

The Green Forest is the same. Those present here are the carefully selected elite among the many Green Forest. Even those unfamiliar with the martial world could guess how fiercely the Green Forest King has trained them.

Yet, after only a few hours of rolling under Chung Myung’s command, these survivors of harsh training are sprawled around, beaten to the point of barely clinging to life.

That man seems to have spent his whole life studying how to torment those with the nature of ‘warriors.’

Perhaps due to some experience(?), Namgung Do-Wei, who avoided joining them in collapse, looked at the fallen with eyes full of pity.

Both he and the Namgung Family, when first faced with this sight, had curses spill out like a machine gun.

But unlike the helpless Namgung Do-Wei, there were those who laughed at this pitiful scene.

“They’re all dead.”

“Hey, they’re not quite dead yet.”

“They shouldn’t be panting this much already.”

Shouldn’t they all be called as demons?

Namgung Do-Wei glanced narrowly at the Five Swords, who were chuckling. Seeing those Taoist demons laughing as if others’ misfortune was their happiness, he thought of the Grand Elder... Huh? Monk? ...Why is the monk laughing there too...?

Namgung Do-Wei abruptly cut off his thoughts and tightly shut his eyes.

This place is a den of corruption that knows no distinction between Taoism and Buddhism.

The only relief was that even in this den of corruption, there was one true Taoist who embraced the Way.

Someone, cautious among the demons, quietly spoke.

“Um... sasuk. They’re struggling so hard after intense training, but to openly enjoy this like this is a bit...”

Tears welled up in Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes.

‘Taoist Yoon Jong!’

Indeed, the Taoist vessel of Mount Hua! Even in this demon-infested Mount Hua, there was hope.

Baek Cheon, hearing Yoon Jong’s words, spoke with a strange expression.

“Yoon Jong.”

“Yes, sasuk.”

“You’re definitely not wrong. I think so too.”

“...Then why...”

“But try to see it from another perspective. Until now, they were the ones watching us train, weren’t they?”

“...”

“Thinking about how much fun they must have had watching us do the same, I can’t help but laugh.”

“...”

“Remember how they looked at us when we trained.”

“No, that was...”

Yoon Jong, lost in thought for a moment, turned his gaze to the sprawled figures and murmured.

“Life... is ultimately about what you get back.”

“That’s right. You’ve realized it.”

It was the moment the Taoist vessel of Mount Hua fell.

Namgung Do-Wei silently screamed, ‘Don’t realize those things,’ but Yoon Jong and Baek Cheon had no way of knowing his feelings.

“Looks like the Green Forest King is dead?”

“Oh dear, he was a frail man.”

“He’s really going to the grave this time?”

“Nah, he won’t die from that. The worst part of the training he orders is that you feel like dying but don’t actually die. If you died, it would be easier.”

“Right. He still has a long way to go.”

“Get healthy already.”

At the demons’ chuckling, Im So-Byeong lifted his head and glared at the Five Swords. His bloodshot eyes leaked a poisonous glare.

“These... bastards worse than the Evil Sect...”

“Hehehehehe.”

“Heeheeheeheehee!”

Namgung Do-Wei, watching the horrific scene, finally looked away.

‘Where is the Heavenly Friends Alliance headed?’

It felt like they had gone too far to turn back.

And then.

“No!”

Chung Myung slammed the bench with a face full of dissatisfaction and stood up.

“What, did everyone just eat porridge? How much did you roll around to already be stuck to the floor writhing?”

“...”

“...”

“And yet you still held your heads high as Tang family or Green Forest? With this skill? This stamina? Oh my fate. I was fighting in front of these!”

Those who had sent poisonous glares to the Five Swords immediately lowered their eyes prettily when Chung Myung stepped forward. Just listing those who rashly challenged him and got hurt would be enough to cross the Yangtze River easily.

So for now, it’s best to think you’re dead and avoid eye contact.

“Tsk tsk tsk.”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue as if displeased watching that sight.

“You really want to face the Demonic Cult bastards with this skill? If you don’t pee yourself and run away, consider yourself lucky.”

Those who tried to raise their heads to protest out of frustration saw Chung Myung’s face and bowed their heads deeply again.

‘Just close your eyes tight.’

‘That man is not the Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon we knew, but the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.’

‘Now even the family lord has to concede a point.’

His personality hasn’t changed, but the status difference between the Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon they knew and the current Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is like heaven and earth.

Even without considering that he is a disciple of Mount Hua and has ties with the Tang family lord, one must just close their eyes and listen to the lecture from someone with such skill.

The position of the Green Forest is obvious. The Green Forest King himself is rolling here with others.

Chung Myung glanced over everyone disdainfully and said.

“From tomorrow, we’ll do exactly twice as much as today, so prepare yourself. Anyone who will roll around without spirit like today should just escape tonight. It’s easier for me than getting my hands bloody.”

“...Um, excuse me.”

At that moment, Tang Pae, the young master of the Tang family, raised his hand with an intrigued expression.

“C-can we escape? Is there any retaliation or...?

“Why would I retaliate against the Tang family or Green Forest? Of course not.”

“R-really...”

Tang Pae’s face brightened, though it quickly darkened after Chung Myung’s next words.

“But you have to handle the Tang family lord’s wrath yourself.”

“......”

Imagining Tang Gunak running at him with twin blades and a blank face, Tang Pae lowered his arm helplessly.

That is hell in its own right. Or perhaps compared to that, this place might be heaven.

“Anyway.”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue shortly as if displeased.

‘No wonder only Mount Hua suffered.’

The old Mount Hua only focused on increasing and efficiently using its own power, neglecting those who support from the rear.

Thus, Chung Myung flashed here and there, slicing Demonic Cult bastards everywhere, building a heroic record, but...

‘No, thinking about it, I was the only one rolling to death.’

“This guy?”

Chung Myung glared and looked up at the sky.

Lately, his respect for the heavens was gradually eroding. Thinking back, this guy was just shouting day and night, ‘Chung Myung! A Demonic Cult bastard appeared here! Go handle it!’

Even Hyun Jong sweats trying to stop Chung Myung from rushing out when his eyes go wild, fearing he might get hurt.

“...They say there’s no one to trust in this world.”

“Yes?”

“Ahem. Enough!”

Chung Myung waved his hand sharply and glared.

“The Nine Great Sects bastards are red-eyed thinking only about how to screw us in future, and the Evil Sect bastards only think about stabbing knives into our backs, and you want to face both at the same time with this kind of skill?”

Im So-Byeong suddenly raised his head.

“No, when did I say I’d face them both at the same time, I said...”

Thwack!

At that moment, a flying shoe hit Im So-Byeong’s face.

Thud.

Im So-Byeong stiffened and collapsed flat on the ground.

“You’re speaking to your elders, be respectful!”

Yoon Jong, watching the situation, whispered softly.

“Sasuk, isn’t that guy the youngest here?”

“Let’s just think of him as the older brother from now. It’s easier for all of us that way.”

“...”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue watching Im So-Byeong convulsing straight on the ground.

“That guy is the Green Forest King? Ugh.”

“...”

“Anyway, training continues tomorrow. From tomorrow, come out thinking you’re all dead.”

“...”

“By any means, I will make you all useful. Even for my own survival.”

Chung Myung, eyes gleaming, turned sharply and walked away.

Baek Cheon shook his head after confirming the man had gone far away.

“He’s full of poison.”

“Looks like he’s serious about this.”

“At this point, it’s really a bit pitiful.”

Jo Gul glanced at Chung Myung’s staggering back, holding a bottle of liquor in one hand, and said.

“But why is that guy suddenly like that?”

“Huh?”

“Well... since we train alongside them, it’s easier and better than usual...”

Hearing that, the Tang family members and Green Forest glared at Jo Gul with wide eyes.

Easy? This? Really?

“Considering that guy’s temper, I thought as soon as he caught his breath he’d go crazy saying we did nothing in Hangzhou... but strangely, that arrow seems to be pointing the other way.”

“What else could it be?”

Baek Cheon shrugged.

“Whether it’s the Evil Sect or the Demonic Cult, they’re opponents too difficult for Mount Hua alone now, right?”

“That’s true.”

Honestly, even Mount Hua’s disciples must admit this. No matter how much skill they have, the numbers are just too different. No matter how strong Mount Hua becomes, in sheer numbers, it’s just a small sect.

Even if every disciple of Mount Hua had the strength of a hundred men, the numbers of the Demonic Cult and Evil Sect would exceed Mount Hua by a hundredfold. So there’s no answer.

“So to oppose them, you have no choice but to cultivate other sects. Like Mount Hua.”

“...Like where?”

“Like Mount Hua.”

“...”

Baek Cheon shrugged and said.

“So what Chung Myung ultimately aims for is the Transformation of the entire Heavenly Friends Alliance. Beyond that, it can be called the Transformation of all Central Plains.”

“The... Mount Hua’s form of Transformation of all Central Plains?”

Everyone trembled with shocked faces.

“That’s the only way to stand against those Demonic Cult bastards and the Heavenly Demon, isn’t it?”

“Y-yeah, that’s true but...”

“That sounds truly terrifying.”

“That’s hell itself.”

“At that point, shouldn’t we compare which is more terrible, that or the Demonic Cult’s plan to annihilate all Central Plains?”

“...Guys. You are Mount Hua disciples.”

“I know, but...”

Jo Gul shook his head.

“If that happens, Jang Ilso will be truly terrified.”

“No.”

“Yes?”

Baek Cheon chuckled.

“Isn’t there someone who would find that situation even more terrifying than Jang Ilso?”

“Who... Ah!”

At that moment, all of Five Swords’s gazes turned to one person, precisely to a shiny head.

The shiny head instantly turned bright red.

Knock. Knock. Knock. Knock.

The sound of a wooden fish being struck rhythmically and the chanting of Buddhist scriptures filled the small room.

Each time the wooden fish was struck, the lantern lighting the room flickered slightly.

It was a sublime scene. The old monk striking the wooden fish and chanting the sutras radiated solemnity, tranquility, and the unique nobility of Buddhism.

Knock. Knock. Knock.

But at some moment, the rhythmically ringing wooden fish sound began to falter. Accordingly, the gently burning lantern flickered.

It was then that the chanting from the old monk’s mouth stopped. Opening his closed eyes, the monk quietly looked down at the wooden fish in his hand.

‘I’ve struck this all my life.’

He had held this wooden motak for over seventy years. Now, even with eyes closed or ears blocked, he could strike it without the slightest tremor.

But at this moment, the wooden fish in his hand failed to produce a proper sound.

‘The mirror of the mind...’

The monk, the abbot, looked down at the wooden fish with solemn eyes and slowly put it down. No matter how much one engraves and masters it in the body, if the mind wavers, all is in vain.

Ultimately, everything depends on where the mind goes.

The abbot knew this very well. It was the Buddhist teaching he had strived to realize all his life.

But...

‘Everything is within the sutras.’

The sages had already shown him the way. Yet the reason followers cannot easily reach the Dharma is because knowing all does not mean one can practice it.

“...Amitabha Buddha.”

A small Buddhist chant escaped his lips.

How much more must one seek and seek again to truly enter the ranks of the Buddha? Is it really possible to reach that state while wearing this sinful and greedy flesh?

“...That too is attachment.”

It’s a funny thing.

Attachment is the root of all suffering. To truly master the Dharma, all attachments must be abandoned. But if you think about it, the desire to obtain the Dharma and reach Buddha’s state is itself a kind of attachment, isn’t it?

Those who seek cannot obtain it; only those who have let go can obtain it. The Buddhist path is such, so how can a person walk it?

‘It is futile.’

The abbot slowly shook his head.

He knew. His body was already unfit to become a Buddha.

‘Rather than becoming a Buddha alone, I will make the whole world Buddhas. Even if my own body falls into hell.’

To do so, everyone under heaven must understand the nobility of the Dharma. They must realize that accepting and seeking the Dharma is the only true way to escape this painful life.

But sadly, those struggling just to live each day pay no attention to the lofty Buddhist teachings. Therefore, to lead them to the Pure Land, dignity rather than teaching is needed.

A dazzling dignity so radiant that no one can look away. A dignity that naturally inspires the desire to walk that path oneself.

Compared to that, even the name Shaolin is small. Everything he does is ultimately to preach Buddha’s teachings in the Central Plains and lead even one more person to the Pure Land.

It is not Buddha’s teachings that need Shaolin’s name to be elevated. Rather, Shaolin’s reputation is needed to widely spread Buddha’s teachings.

Isn’t everything just for the suffering sentient beings?

“But why is that path so arduous?”

The abbot’s eyes closed tightly.

The world is too chaotic to spread Buddha’s teachings. And his strength is too weak to overcome that chaos.

“No one shows the way. No one...”

The abbot stretched out his wrinkled hand and grasped the wooden motak again. Even if it trembles and falters, not stopping is the path of a practitioner.

But then.

“Abbot, are you inside?”

Beop Gye’s voice was heard outside the door.

The abbot gently put down the wooden motak in his hand and sighed deeply. The life he chose did not even allow him ample time to calm his own mind.

“Come in.”

“Yes, Abbot.”

The door opened cautiously and Beop Gye entered.

The abbot, Beop Jong looked at him and unconsciously frowned slightly. Seeing Beop Gye’s extremely haggard complexion, he felt he knew how he himself must look.

“Abbot.”

Beop Gye carefully sat before the abbot. His eyes repeatedly examined the abbot’s expression.

“News from Hangzhou has arrived.”

The abbot closed his eyes silently at the news.

He too was curious about the news. But he seemed to already know the outcome. Beop Gye’s expression said it all.

“What happened?”

“...Hangzhou suffered near annihilation, but for now... the wicked Demonic Cult forces that invaded Hangzhou can no longer cause chaos and have left the Central Plains.”

This much was what the abbot had expected.

“What about the Mount Hua swordsmen who went to Hangzhou along with those Evil Sect forces?”

“That is...”

Beop Gye glanced at the abbot, who had his eyes closed, and cautiously began to speak.

“Two days ago... they returned to where Mount Hua resides without casualties.”

The abbot was suddenly engulfed by indescribable emotion. Countless feelings swirled inside him.

The quiet silence lengthened, and Beop Gye sitting before him could barely breathe deeply. He just kept his mouth shut, endlessly waiting for the abbot’s reaction.

Finally, the abbot slowly opened his eyes and asked.

“Two days ago...?”

“...”

“Did you say two days ago?”

“A-Abbot.”

At the icy aura flowing from his eyes, Beop Gye involuntarily shrugged his shoulders. The old abbot never showed such coldness even when hearing upsetting words.

But not anymore. Now, unlike before, he sometimes showed such coldness that even Beop Gye trembled in fear.

But now, the abbot’s aura was different from before.

“If they returned two days ago, the Beggars’ Union would naturally have tracked their movements.”

“...”

“Then how am I to interpret that this news has only just reached you?”

“That... that I also...”

As Beop Gye trailed off and looked around nervously, a faint twitch appeared at the corner of the abbot’s eye.

“Not a single thing...”

His hand, which lightly held the wooden motak, clenched the folds of his robe on his thigh tightly. Blue veins bulged on the back of his hand.

“Not a single thing is going right! Not a single thing!”

“Abbot...”

“Yes.”

The abbot bit his lip tightly and glared at Beop Gye.

“Continue.”

“...”

“I said continue!”

“Y-Yes! Abbot!”

Beop Gye flinched, bowed his head, and quickly continued speaking.

“According to news from the Beggars’ Union... those who appeared in Hangzhou were the high priest of the Demonic Cult and his followers. The disciples of Mount Hua who headed south killed the Evil Sect members along with the Ten Thousand People Clan, Black Ghost Treasure, and Green Forest...”

Beop Gye instinctively glanced at the abbot again after mentioning the Demonic Cult group and continued.

“And the Beggars’ Union’s Jang Ilso and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint Chung Myung jointly struck the high priest’s neck.”

“...”

“The remaining Demonic Cult members fled. The Beggars’ Union tried to track them, but their trail vanished without a sound, and their whereabouts are unknown.”

Beop Gye swallowed dryly and spoke with a hoarse voice.

“That is all.”

The abbot’s face was as cold as a north wind’s blade. Seeing that, Beop Gye involuntarily closed his eyes tightly.

“What...”

He had spent his whole life with the abbot.

He was his great sahyung and also his sahyung. He admired and respected the abbot’s noble character all his life.

But why recently does the abbot show sides unknown to him? Even though the abbot’s heart for Shaolin and all under heaven has not changed, why?

“The Hegemony Lord and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint struck the high priest’s neck?”

“The Beggars’ Union says so...”

“So.”

The abbot cut off Beop Gye’s words and spoke coldly.

“Those who were desperate to kill each other joined hands and fought together to defeat the enemy?”

“...”

“And the Hegemony Lord, after defeating the Demonic Cult, willingly let the isolated Mount Hua’s Sword Saint in the southern land go?”

Beop Gye could not even answer and only nodded.

He too found it hard to believe, but there was no other way. At least in the south, their eyes do not exist. So they had to rely on the Beggars’ Union’s information.

“Ha... haha.”

The abbot let out a hollow laugh.

“I misjudged Mount Hua.”

“...Abbot?”

His face had become horribly distorted.

“Though their intentions differ, I believed they were striving for the right path. So I secretly had some compassion!”

“...”

“If they collude with those wicked Evil Sect forces for their goals, how are they different from the Evil Sect?”

“Abbot... the situation is...”

“Situation?”

The abbot glared at Beop Gye with angry eyes.

“What situation are you talking about?”

“...”

“Were they the only ones heartbroken by the news of innocent civilians dying there? I too lost sleep over it!”

Bang!

The abbot’s fist slammed down on the low table.

“But is that truly right? Is taking an unjust path to achieve desired results the way those who claim righteousness should choose?”

Beop Gye bowed his head deeply. The abbot flushed and continued.

“I do not blame their altruism! But do they truly not know that this ultimately strengthens the Evil Sect’s control over the south?”

“Th-That is...”

The abbot clenched his fist tightly.

“Those fools have made Jang Ilso a hero. Now, Jang Ilso will be the hero who saved the south from the Demonic Cult’s grasp, further solidifying the Evil Sect’s rule in there. Why don’t they realize this means more people will suffer longer?”

Beop Gye looked at the abbot without answering. He looked like someone lost for words.

“Everything is wrong! All wrong! Hangzhou? Of course, those who died in Hangzhou are tragic. But what did those who rushed to the south achieve? Did they save the people of Hangzhou?”

“They did not...”

“When such a disaster occurs, people have no choice but to flee. Naturally, the damage would be minimized, and ultimately those wicked Evil Sect scoundrels would have to fight the Demonic Cult’s southern advance. Just by enduring a little, a clash between the two evils, the Evil Sect and the Demonic Cult, was possible! Why did they recklessly jump into that situation and plunge the southern civilians into misery?”

“...”

“Foolish! Foolish and foolish again!”

The abbot ground his teeth fiercely.

“Those who cannot see deeply will praise them and criticize us! Again! Not even knowing what they have done!”

“A-Abb...”

“Hey!”

Kwahhhang!

Finally, the abbot slammed his fist down, shattering the low table before him.

Startled, Beop Gye stared blankly at the abbot with his mouth agape.

“Mount Hua... utterly despicable...”

A piercing blue murderous aura flowed from the abbot’s eyes.

“Abbot...”

Beop Gye swallowed dryly and cautiously opened his mouth.

“I do not say that I do not understand what you are saying, abbot...”

Abbot’s gaze, sharp as a blade, pierced through Beop Gye.

For a moment, Beop Gye felt the urge to shut his mouth, biting his lips tightly, but he opened his mouth again. Fear should not delay honest advice. Otherwise, everything would truly go awry.

“Abbot... if you truly were concerned about that situation, wouldn’t it have been right to seriously discuss it when Mount Hua’s sect leader came to us? To blame them after everything is over...”

“Discussion?”

The cold voice flowing from the Abbot’s mouth interrupted Beop Gye’s words.

“With whom do you say I should discuss?”

“With Hyun Jong of Mount Hua...”

“That scarecrow?”

Beop Gye tightly closed his eyes.

No matter what happens, one should not call another sect’s leader a scarecrow. It would be a great humiliation even to a small minor sect, let alone a sect as large as Mount Hua.

Abbot is not ignorant of this fact. As the exalted abbot of Shaolin, he has been extremely careful with his words and actions.

However, recently, Abbot has often lost his composure regarding matters related to Mount Hua.

‘No...’

A strange light flickered in Beop Gye’s eyes.

Would the Abbot he knows really use such words against someone just out of simple enmity? ...Really?

‘What am I thinking!’

Beop Gye quickly erased the suspicion that had arisen in his mind. It was unimaginable. Would Abbot, the Shaolin’s abbot, feel rivalry toward Hyun Jong, merely the sect leader of Mount Hua?

At that moment, the Abbot shouted.

“What meaning is there in discussing with Hyun Jong? Do you not know who truly decides the crucial matters in Mount Hua?”

“...That is...”

“Hyun Jong might not know. If he were a good person, he might not guess what Mount Hua’s move to the south means, or how that cunning the Hegemony Lord would exploit them. But!”

Abbot ground his teeth.

“That Mount Hua’s Sword Saint! Do you think he went to the south without predicting all this?”

Beop Gye closed his mouth like a clam. Abbot openly scoffed.

“No way. That guy, who seems to have ten snakes in his belly, wouldn’t be unaware of this situation. He went knowing full well! Knowing!”

Beop Gye dared not refute this.

Considering the abilities of the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint he had observed, it would be stranger if they did not predict all this.

“Those who endlessly talk about cooperation and civilians with their mouths could not endure their petty vanity and ultimately drove all the southern civilians into hell! This was the chance to overthrow the Four Sects Alliance and liberate the south! Such a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity wasted like this!”

Abbot’s face flushed bright red. The refined old monk who pursued Buddhist law moments ago was nowhere to be found.

“Despicable! Despicable! Their intentions are clear to me! If only they endured a little disgrace, bore a little criticism, and waited, they could bring peace to the world! But they ruin it like this! Is this Mount Hua’s way?”

It was a frost-like fury.

“Moreover!”

Abbot’s hand trembling as he gripped his robe.

“You mean to say they conspired with those Evil Sect bastards to do this!”

“...”

“If Mount Hua had handled all this alone without joining hands with Jang Ilso, I might have called them foolish but not doubted their sincerity! But how am I to accept that those who claim justice fought alongside the Evil Sect’s lackeys? The spirits of those who died at Black Dragon Pirates and the Namgung souls at Plum Blossom Island still wander the heavens! And they allied with the Evil Sect Alliance?”

Abbot spoke as if spitting fire. Beop Gye’s body trembled involuntarily at the intense rage.

But what followed was not a shout filled with wrath, but a hollow laugh.

“Huh... hahaha...

Abbot suddenly laughed hollowly and shook his head.

“It’s exhausting... just exhausting.”

“...abbot.”

“Those who know nothing will now praise Mount Hua. And also praise Jang Ilso. Saying they defended the Central Plains against that terrible Demonic Cult. And they will blame us, asking where we were.”

Beop Gye closed his mouth.

Judging by the revealed circumstances, it is not wrong. Shaolin did nothing during the Hangzhou rebellion, did they?

No, to be frank, Beop Gye was confused about what role Shaolin played in the recent series of events shaking the murim world.

Always stepping back, waiting for the situation to ripen, only to have the Four Sects Alliance or Mount Hua take the initiative repeatedly.

Beop Gye spoke in a grave tone.

“... Abbot. In my opinion... considering the gravity of the Demonic cult’s name, perhaps we should not have waited like this.”

Abbot silently stared at Beop Gye.

After a long heavy silence pressuring Beop Gye, a voice like a lament escaped Abbot’s mouth.

“Even you think like that?”

“That’s not...”

Abbot cut off his words with a deep, sinking voice.

“That is why we had to wait even more.”

“...Yes? That is...”

“Don’t you understand?”

Abbot said, staring piercingly at Beop Gye.

“If Mount Hua truly feared the Demonic Cult, if they were alert, they should not have rushed to the south rashly but naturally tried to join hands with us. No, to put it bluntly, they should have bowed to us!”

“A-abbot... the Heavenly Friends Alliance is no longer a small coalition.”

“What does that mean?”

Abbot gritted his teeth.

“Who exactly are those in the Heavenly Friends Alliance you mentioned?”

“That is...”

“The Four Heavenly Friends sects, rejected even by the Justice faction as heretics. The Namgung Family, having lost their power and nowhere to go! And the sects from the western regions not even considered Central Plains people, and Mount Hua, expelled from the Nine Great Sects!”

“...”

“Among them, is there truly a sect with great influence in the murim world?”

“...No, there isn’t.”

“Everyone only sees the facade! Everyone!”

Bang!

Abbot slammed the floor in frustration.

“Just because Mount Hua defeated Southern Edge, did the Southern Edge bow to Mount Hua?”

“...”

“Just because Mount Hua defeated Wudang, did Wudang give up their position to Mount Hua?”

“No, abbot.”

“That is the limit of Mount Hua as a sect, and the limit of the Heavenly Friends Alliance as a coalition. No matter how much they perform and expand their influence, would the proud prestigious sects of the Nine Great Sects submit under Mount Hua?”

Beop Gye could not answer this.

Can you imagine Shaolin bowing to Mount Hua and joining the Heavenly Friends Alliance?

No. Such a thing can never happen.

Probably the other Nine Great Sects think similarly. Unless it’s the Beggars’ Union, who don’t care about face or pride.

“...It’s not because I am Shaolin’s abbot that we must unite around Shaolin. Do you know why Shaolin has always been at the center when the martial world faces turmoil?”

“That is...”

“Because only when dealing with Shaolin do they step back! Simply put, if Namgung Hwang were alive, would he have given up the leadership of the Five Great Families to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“...”

“The Heavenly Friends Alliance cannot unify the murim world. Therefore, they gather only those they can easily control to expand their power. Even knowing this causes clear division in the Central Plains!”

Abbot’s shoulders trembled with rage.

“Do you understand what I mean?”

“...Yes, abbot.”

Beop Gye nodded unconsciously.

There was nothing wrong with Abbot’s words.

No matter how much one thinks about it, the scene of the Heavenly Friends Alliance subjugating the prestigious sects of the Nine Great Sects, the existing powers of the Central Plains, does not come to mind. Ultimately, the stronger the Heavenly Friends Alliance becomes, the more the North is clearly divided into two forces: the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Nine Great Sects.

Moreover, these two forces have already accumulated deep animosity toward each other.

Even without mentioning Shaolin, Mount Hua and the Nine Great Sects have never had good relations. Leaving unresolved issues until now has caused wounds to fester and rot.

“Do you think they don’t know this?”

“...”

“Is there truly a place for the Nine Great Sects in the Heavenly Friends Alliance they envision?”

“That is...”

“But how long must I show mercy to them? How long must I think of them as those to be embraced? Even Buddha did not show mercy to the Devil. So how long must I, who cannot reach that profound meaning, endure?”

Killing intent flowed from the Abbot’s eyes.

The Abbot was the first to recognize Mount Hua’s potential and support them. And Mount Hua exceeded his expectations. Therefore, he sent Hae Yeon, naturally timid and with weaknesses, to them to learn their spirit.

Though sometimes displeased with their actions, those with ability deserve some freedom. Thus, he treated them with patience rather than condemnation.

But...

‘I can no longer tolerate their recklessness.’

If Buddhism tried to solve everything with mercy, there would be no disciplinary tools like the execution blade punishing those who break rules.

Rotten sprouts must be cut off.

Otherwise, other healthy sprouts will rot too. The rotten sprout called Mount Hua has already caused many evils. If division accelerates like this, not only the Demonic Cult but even the Four Sects Alliance might become uncontrollable.

“...Not even realizing they are being played.”

The Abbot bit his lips tightly. Eventually, thick blood began to seep from his lips.

“I should have heard those words back then.”

“Abbot?”

“No.”

Abbot shook his head.

“If we leave things like this, it will become uncontrollable. Call the Beggars’ Union. We must interrogate the Heavenly Friends Alliance about their alliance with the Four Sects Alliance.”

“I-Is this the time to say that?”

When Beop Gye asked in surprise, Abbot silently glared coldly. Eventually, Beop Gye stood and bowed his head.

“I will do so.”

“Hurry.”

“Yes!”

Beop Gye ran out.

Left alone, Abbot slowly turned his head and stared at the flickering lamp. Though no breeze stirred in the room, the lamp flickered as if revealing his inner turmoil.

“Mount Hua... Mount Hua’s Sword Saint Chung Myung.”

Crunch.

Abbot clenched his fist tightly as if it would burst, then exhaled deeply. Trying to calm himself, he reached out and lightly grabbed the wooden beads that had fallen to the floor.

Crack.

At that moment, his worn wooden beads cracked open.

Abbot stared blankly at the cracked wooden beads. Unable to look away for a long time, a hollow laugh escaped his mouth.

“Huh... haha.”

The lamp flickered even more violently.

“Hahahahaha. Hahahahahahaha!”

In the room where no one approached, only the feeble laughter of the old monk echoed endlessly.

Applying heat doesn’t make water boil instantly. But if heat is applied continuously, the water will inevitably boil over someday.

The current situation was exactly like that.

Even with news that the Demonic Cult had appeared in the south of the river, the people in the north showed little reaction. After all, the south was too far away for them.

For those who lived day by day tilling fields, the calamity happening in the south, a place they might never visit in their lifetime, did not resonate with them.

But what made them truly realize the gravity was a piece of news that came across the river.

The Demonic Cult that invaded Hangzhou annihilated all its people, leaving not even a single rat alive.

Hangzhou, once the most splendid city in the world, became a city of death in just a few days.

Those who heard the news could only doubt their ears and be horrified.

What kind of place was Hangzhou? Even if not the largest city, it was undeniably the most prosperous city in the world. Even those who had never been there knew of it.

How could one not be shocked that such a place was destroyed overnight?

Such things were not unheard of. Sometimes when northern invaders attacked, a city would be completely destroyed and refugees would flee in droves.

But this incident happened not in the distant north, but in the southern region that was rarely invaded. The shock was incomparable.

“Is, is this really true?”

“Don’t you keep hearing the same thing over and over? It must be true!”

“No... No matter how elusive the Demonic Cult is, does it make sense for them to suddenly appear in the south? They didn’t just fall from the sky. And... how big is Hangzhou? How could it fall? I just can’t believe it.”

“Hey you! Didn’t all the merchant traffic heading south stop?”

“Me-merchant traffic?”

There’s a joke that even in war, merchant traffic doesn’t stop. Merchants are those who would cross battlefields if there’s money to be made.

Even when the Yangtze River was completely controlled by Pirates and crossing it meant risking one’s life, the merchants of Central Plains risked danger to continue their trade.

That such people were not heading south was clear evidence that something serious had happened beyond the river.

“Th-then what happens now?”

“What happens? It’ll be just like a hundred years ago.”

“N-no way...!”

People were instantly engulfed in fear. And they all began to remember.

The story from a hundred years ago that had been forgotten. The tales passed down from generation to generation about the Demonic Cult.

Specifically, how ruthless those Demonic Cult people were.

During that terrible war, blood flowed like rivers and corpses piled like mountains. Just hearing about it sent chills down one’s spine. The thought that such a war might happen again was enough to make even those indifferent to the southern events tremble.

“Shouldn’t we stop it?”

“How? What can we do?”

“No, didn’t Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects stand up to stop the Demonic Cult before? Then they have to do something this time too!”

“Shaolin? That Shaolin is just watching the fire from across the Yangtze River right now.”

“No, why?”

“Didn’t the Four Sects Alliance control the south? There’s a fire there, but Shaolin wouldn’t risk themselves to jump in.”

“What nonsense is that! Does the Demonic Cult care about Justice faction or Evil Sects? Didn’t they fight desperately against the Demonic Cult regardless before? Shaolin was the most active in that war!”

“Maybe back then. But now Shaolin seems incapable. Or maybe they don’t want to join hands with the Evil Sect even if it means death.”

“Th-this stupidity...”

People disdain the Evil Sect but do not fear their existence. The Evil Sect is already very familiar to them.

If unlucky, one might suffer great harm or lose life, but ordinary people rarely get involved with the Evil Sect in their lifetime.

To them, the small-time thugs in back alleys were far more threatening than the massive Evil Sect alliance. So Shaolin ignoring the south out of pride was not welcome.

“Then when will Shaolin move?”

“I don’t know. They’ll fight when the Demonic Cult heads north.”

“Then what about the south? Are there no people there? Are those living under the Evil Sect alliance not worth saving? They just leave them to the Evil Sect?”

“Have you ever seen those noble gentlemen care about such things? When Pirates ran rampant and civilians near the Yangtze were massacred, they were just meditating on mountains. It was the Sichuan Tang family that saved people then, not Shaolin.”

“Those damn bastards! Always talking about righteousness and peace in Central Plains, but when trouble comes, they just step back and watch. If they care so much about position, how are they different from those scoundrels in the Evil Sect?”

Once water starts boiling, it doesn’t cool easily.

Fear of the Demonic Cult, worry about when they might flood north, and the behavior shown so far by Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects all mixed and began to boil.

Those called the Justice faction in Central Plains receive privileges.

They can carry swords openly in daylight without interference, and even if they fight, the authorities do not stop them. Many people deliberately use their businesses more and even directly support them.

All this is tolerated because commoners believe they will step in to help when injustice occurs.

The government is strict and generally indifferent to commoners’ lives. If taxes aren’t paid properly, they intimidate and punish people.

In contrast, the Justice faction helps without taking from them. Thus, commoners trust the Justice faction more than the government.

But at this moment, the commoners who had endured began to doubt their sincerity. How many lived in Hangzhou? With so many dying, how could they trust those who sat idle?

“Then what should we do? Should we flee...?”

“Hey, fleeing isn’t easy. How will you survive if you leave here?”

“It is better than dying. Will a spider web grow in a living mouth?”

“How is that different from dying? Will you make your family beg?”

“That is...”

Unable to do anything, days passed with simmering frustration.

Just as the boiling public sentiment was about to explode, news began to spread rapidly from the south.

The rampant Demonic Cult was annihilated.

The elite of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, led by Mount Hua, were dispatched to the south and beheaded the Evil Sect leader.

The Heavenly Friends Alliance temporarily allied with the Four Sects alliance to defeat the Demonic Cult.

The commoners who heard these simultaneous news cheered.

“Mount Hua! It’s Mount Hua again this time!”

“If it’s Mount Hua, I knew they’d step up! They’re the ones who solve problems!”

“Hey! Why only talk about Mount Hua? Didn’t the Heavenly Friends Alliance move together?”

“Isn’t that the same thing? Mount Hua leads the Heavenly Friends Alliance. The Alliance is Mount Hua, and Mount Hua is the Alliance!”

“That’s right, that’s right!”

For those trembling in anxiety, this news was like a sip of sweet nectar.

“They beheaded the Demonic Cult person? Does that mean the Demonic Cult is completely eradicated now?”

“We don’t know. We have to see if this is Demonic Cult’s main force or not.”

“Still, it means all those who appeared this time were dealt with, right?”

“Seems so. After all, Mount Hua is the only one to trust!”

Most who raised their voices didn’t even know Mount Hua existed a few years ago. But now, they praised Mount Hua without hesitation.

Evaluation ultimately comes from achievements and deeds.

Even things not highly regarded at the time become strength when repeated continuously.

From when Pirates ran rampant on the Yangtze, or even earlier during the Green Forest chaos, Mount Hua was the first to rush and save civilians, and those deeds remained clearly in everyone’s memory.

“While those stupid Shaolin monks just watched, Mount Hua risked the danger and rushed south.”

“Is that surprising? Wasn’t Mount Hua always like that?”

“Mount Hua does all the hard work, but those swindlers puff themselves up! They’re worse than the Evil Sect.”

“The Nine Great Sects are completely rotten. Tch! The rice I gave to beggars before was wasted!”

People’s reactions were more intense than expected.

Many had viewed Mount Hua’s deeds kindly, but no one thought their power surpassed Shaolin.

But Mount Hua going south and defeating the Demonic Cult was proof their power was no less than Shaolin’s.

No, even if lacking, it’s the same. No matter how sharp a sword is, if not drawn from its sheath, it’s just a stick. People began to favor the dagger in front of them rather than waiting for the unsheathed sword.

“I feel a bit uneasy.”

“Hmm? What do you mean?”

“No matter what, allying with the Evil Sect is a bit...”

“What nonsense is this fool saying? Did the Evil Sect alliance massacre Hangzhou people because of that?”

“No, not really...”

“If you watch people die just to avoid allying with the Evil Sect, that’s putting the cart before the horse! Should the so-called prestigious Justice faction defeat the Evil Sect, or protect the commoners? What’s more important? Huh?”

“...Well.”

“Tsk tsk tsk. Didn’t you know there would be people like you among Mount Hua? Honestly, even if those Shaolin bastards just stood by, it would have been fine. Yet Mount Hua risked disgrace and danger to save people in the south, and you can’t even praise them! I misjudged you badly!”

“I-I was short-sighted. Oh, please don’t be angry.”

“Never say such things again! If you spout nonsense and next time Mount Hua doesn’t move, only people like us will die! Got it?”

“Ah, I got it.”

Water doesn’t boil all at once.

And the same went for public opinion about Mount Hua. The deeds of Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance over years had gradually heated the world. When the Demonic Cult reached its boiling point, it finally began to boil explosively.

Even Chung Myung and the abbot did not anticipate this level.

“Ugh.”

Hyun Yonug pulled back the blanket and sat up. Habitually turning his head to look at the window, he slightly frowned.

‘Has the sun risen?’

Today, he had overslept a little.

Normally, oversleeping was impossible, but while his disciples went to the Southern Lands, he hadn’t slept a wink for several days and nights, so the accumulated fatigue seemed to hit him all at once.

“Oh, my joints.”

He patted his shoulder. Though he had mastered martial arts and kept his body in good shape, age was something he couldn’t overcome.

After fully removing the blanket, he sat cross-legged right there. Only after finishing his habitual breathing exercise did he get up and change clothes.

“Hmm.”

Before stepping out the door, he closed his eyes briefly.

Mount Hua has been achieving success after success. But precisely because of that, he must be more cautious.

Fortune always comes with misfortune. If one becomes arrogant or intoxicated by success, they inevitably pay the price.

‘I must be careful.’

These are things young disciples easily overlook. He, being older by even a year, must first be vigilant and soothe them.

Hyun Young wore a slightly wicked smile.

“Then shall I find some fault today?”

Determined to be a strict spirit for his disciples today, Hyun Young firmly opened the door and stepped outside. But his resolve collapsed neatly in less than a short while.

“...What is all this...?”

Standing at the entrance of the estate, Hyun Young muttered with a dazed expression.

Piles of luggage stacked like mountains blocked his view. Not only inside the estate but also at the entrance and beyond, unknown things were piled up in a chaotic layered mess.

“...Did the Demonic Cult invade here? Are they evacuating people?”

“Ah, no. That’s not it...”

Baek Sang wiped the sweat flowing down his forehead and answered.

“Since early dawn, people have been coming in crowds, thanking us for driving away the Demonic Cult...”

“Th-this?”

“Yes.”

“All of this?”

Hyun Young’s pupils trembled wildly as if an earthquake had struck.

As he raised his head following the piled luggage, he grabbed the back of his neck before even seeing the top.

“Oh, my neck...!”

“Are you alright?”

“...Is this all?”

“There’s no more space inside, so the disciples are receiving and stacking goods outside the estate now.”

“What?”

Flames suddenly lit up in Hyun Young’s eyes.

“Is this guy sane or not? You accepted all that gladly? You took without thinking that these are things brought by commoners who struggle to survive day by day while others rake in money?”

Baek Sang was shocked and waved his hand.

“Oh, Elder. That can’t be. We naturally refused politely. But when they insistently give it, what can I do?”

“...Insistently?”

“They say it’s nothing, why don’t you accept even this, is what they said? They say our life is worth only that much. What can I say? No matter how much I block, they just throw it over and run away...”

“...Ha, haha.”

Hyun Young couldn’t help but laugh in disbelief.

It’s not new for things sent from various places to pile up at Mount Hua. Hyun Young had seen it many times. But this is different.

The gifts that came to Mount Hua so far were from powerful figures in Shaanxi to gain favor. Even at best, they were hardly sent with pure hearts; to put it bluntly, they were bribes.

But the things piled here have a completely different meaning, don’t they?

Mount Hua will soon leave the Yangtze River region and go far to Shaanxi. What good would it do for the locals to curry favor with them?

That means all these are truly goods given by the local commoners as pure tokens of gratitude.

“Hehehe...”

Hyun Young, smiling with an unreadable meaning, approached the piled luggage as if entranced and unpacked one bundle from the bottom.

Inside the roughly tied bundle was grain, not particularly precious, packed tightly. Hyun Young quietly looked at it and gently touched it with his fingertips.

“...This precious thing...”

Though gifts from powerful figures might be expensive, they were not ‘precious’ to Hyun Young. How could things sent by the wealthy just to show off have great meaning?

But this grain was different.

The people living in the Yangtze River region had just barely escaped the Pirates’ grip. They struggled daily just to eat. These people took some of the grain they needed for winter and brought it as a token of gratitude.

How could this grain be considered as less valuable than such treasures?

“There are many small things, mostly food.”

“...”

“But the quantity is too large to store easily. We need to find suitable merchants to sell them...”

“What, you fool?”

Hyun Young’s eyes blazed as he sharply turned his head, glaring at Baek Sang with fierce anger.

“Sell what?”

“Th-the grain...”

“Are you crazy?”

Hyun Young’s eyes were bloodshot with fury.

“What is this! What kind of goods are these that you talk about selling? Are these things you can trade for money? The leftovers here are parasites who eat like oxen; feed them all! Why would you sell this!”

“E-Elder, grain can be stored and eaten later, but fresh meat or vegetables brought in now will spoil soon...”

“Eat it all!”

Hyun Young spat fire from his mouth, and Baek Sang quietly shut his mouth.

“Don’t leave a single grain; eat it all! If you don’t, you all will die!”

Baek Sang’s face turned pitch black.

Of course, those here eat five or six times more than an ordinary man, causing Hyun Jong stomach pain. But even so, how could they possibly eat enough to fill ten huge granaries in just a few days?

“Ah, no...”

“Shut up! Start feeding them from lunch today! Make them eat it all!”

“...”

“Do you understand?”

“Yes, yes...”

“Tsk.”

Hyun Young clicked his tongue in displeasure and looked at the piled gifts with a renewed gaze.

Indeed... young disciples would find it hard to understand.

For some, chivalry is a matter of choice, but for others, it was a desperate hope too dazzling to even look at.

When Mount Hua was once on the brink of collapse, Hyun Young couldn’t even dream of chivalry. For some, it might be a task they can skip if lazy despite ability, but for Hyun Young and his generation, chivalry was something dazzling.

What kept them going through that harsh life was the vain hope that someday they too would fight as proud warriors for the suffering commoners and make Mount Hua’s name known far and wide.

As his hair gradually turned white in longing, and as he realized youth doesn’t last forever, he slowly let go of that hope.

Hyun Young carefully put down the grain he had been gently touching.

“...I was not wrong.”

“Yes?”

“No.”

Hyun Young answered curtly and turned his head. He didn’t want to show his face to the disciples now.

After taking some time to compose his expression, Hyun Young looked again and asked.

“So, are there still visitors outside?”

“Yes. The disciples are receiving the goods now.”

“Let’s go see.”

“Yes!”

Baek Sang quickly stepped forward. Following him, they soon saw people lined up carrying bundles tightly wrapped.

After waiting, a large man reached the disciples, snorting, and suddenly thrust the items in his hands forward.

“I don’t know if the masters eat meat, but I couldn’t think of anything else, so I brought this.”

“Thank you. We do eat meat.”

“That’s a relief.”

The gruff man almost threw the bundle into the disciples’ arms.

“...It looks like a lot.”

“It’s not much for someone like me!”

“Th-thank you.”

The disciples wiped sweat from their foreheads and accepted the goods despite his gruff attitude.

“Where do you live...”

“Enough. What’s the use in knowing that?”

“...Ah, yes.”

The man turned as if without regret but glanced at the Mount Hua disciple.

“Thank you.”

Chung Myung, holding the bundle, looked at the man with questioning eyes.

“I have an elderly mother. If we had to evacuate, she wouldn’t have survived the harsh outside winds, so she would have died here helplessly.”

“...”

“Thanks to you, she lived. Thank you.”

Chung Myung stared blankly at him for a moment, then smiled warmly.

“I just did what I had to do.”

“Ahem.”

The man nodded, then turned and shuffled away.

Hyun Young stared at the man’s back, then turned his gaze aside. The bottom of the bundle was damp, perhaps from freshly butchered meat.

“...Baek Sang.”

“Yes, Elder.”

“Go and bring more disciples. The weather is cold, and the line is too long. If they can’t refuse, at least reduce their waiting time.”

“Yes? But Chung Myung is currently beating... no, training the kids, so he won’t let them go.”

“Tell him I called for them and bring them out. No, bring Chung Myung and all the trainees to receive the goods first.”

“That training is...”

“Hurry.”

“Yes! Elder.”

Baek Sang hurried inside. Even though it was Chung Myung, no one would argue with the sect leader and Elder Hyun Young’s orders, so there should be no problem.

Hyun Young silently watched those lined up.

From the shaggy-haired strong men to the elderly bent old women, and the women holding children’s hands with rough hands, all held something in their hands and looked at Mount Hua’s disciples with admiring eyes.

“That...”

Hyun Young opened his mouth as if to say something but closed it again. As he turned his head, a voice brushed past his ear.

“Isn’t it a good sight?”

Hyun Jong glanced back and said gruffly.

“When did you arrive?”

“Just a moment ago.”

Hyun Jong was smiling warmly at him.

“The sect leader is quite free. What is there to see that he comes all the way here?”

“No matter how busy, there are things one must see.”

“Then why not lend a hand?”

Hyun Young’s words made Hyun Jong smile quietly.

“We are not the ones who should welcome them, right?”

Hyun Young silently nodded at those words.

“Chivalry should not be performed expecting reward. The moment you expect reward, it ceases to be chivalry and becomes a transaction.”

“...”

“But...”

Hyun Jong said.

“If the reward is like this... yes, it’s worth receiving.”

Hyun Young just pouted his lips. He felt that if he spoke, nonsense would come out, so he couldn’t bring himself to open his mouth.

At that moment, disciples came running breathlessly from afar.

“Work!”

“Damn! We get to work!”

“I’ll do it! Please just let me!”
