They were happily celebrating, but their faces told a different story. They were swollen all over from the fierce struggle.

“Idiots. Fighting desperately against greenhouse flowers and barely winning, yet acting so proud.”

“B-but a win is a win...”

“...What?”

Im So-Byeong’s eyes narrowed, and Beon Chung quickly shrank his thick neck.

“Tsk tsk.”

Im So-Byeong showed his discomfort openly and clicked his tongue.

‘We almost lost.’

The reason they won today was simple: the Namgung Family’s strength was less than half of what it was before, and Im So-Byeong himself commanded Green Forest.

Conversely, if Im So-Byeong hadn’t led, they might have lost even against the half-dead Namgung Family.

‘This is a problem.’

Im So-Byeong groaned.

Green Forest was never a sect led by a few strong individuals. Their power came from overwhelming numbers. So depending on perspective, this result might not be disappointing.

But Im So-Byeong knew this was just an excuse. And this was why Green Forest, despite being the oldest Evil Sect historically, had never had a leader of the Evil Sect.

“As it stands, it’s hard for us to handle even the Namgung Family. That means against other sects of the Four Sects Alliance, we’d be utterly destroyed.”

“What nonsense! When all our brothers in the world gather, there’s no sect we can’t face!”

“...Gather?”

Im So-Byeong glared at Beon Chung as if to say, “What nonsense?”

“So have they ever gathered in one place?”

“...Well...”

“How can you gather those spread all over Central Plains? It would take over a month just for the message to reach them.”

Beon Chung closed his mouth.

He was right. Green Forest influenced mountains across the land, but being so widespread, coordinated movement was nearly impossible for them

“And even if the order is delivered, would they really abandon their mountain bases and all rush here on my command?”

“Of course! It’s the Green Forest King’s order!”

“Really?”

“...”

“Really?”

“...Well... leaving the mountain bases too empty could cause problems.”

“Eh.”

Im So-Byeong sighed deeply.

Before joining the Heavenly Friends Alliance, he had no capacity to manage all this. Just hiding and engaging in guerrilla tactics against Jang Ilso, who was constantly hunting him, drained all his energy. No matter how brilliant his strategy, there were limits.

Now that he had just solved one problem by joining the Heavenly Friends Alliance, another huge problem emerged.

“First... realistically speaking, Green Forest is by no means weak.”

“Of course.”

“But at the same time, we are incredibly weak. They have overwhelming forces, but the actual usable strength is ridiculously weak.”

“...”

“And even if gathered, it’s just a large number of people, useless in local battles. In war, all they can do is use their numbers for supply lines.”

Im So-Byeong muttered and looked around at everyone.

“What do you think?”

“What?”

“If there’s a problem, it must be solved. Right?”

“...That’s true, but...”

“Fortunately, I know how to solve this. Not implementing the solution despite knowing it would be a fool’s act.”

“Um...”

Beon Chung quietly asked.

“Sorry, but may I ask what that solution is?”

At that, Im So-Byeong nodded as if pleased they asked.

“It’s simple. First, strengthen the core forces. If Green Forest Bandits grows stronger, its influence will increase, and so will my influence over Green Forest. Eventually, the entire Green Forest will become more agile.”

Beon Chung asked with a puzzled face.

“Isn’t that what we’ve been doing till now? That’s why the Green Forest King...”

“No.”

Im So-Byeong shook his fingers left and right.

“I thought we were, but we weren’t. Being born in the Evil Sect, I assumed training was something everyone did on their own.”

“...”

“But seeing how Mount Hua does it, I realized this is a new thing. They do it like that, so why haven’t we tried it?”

“Th-that’s we have our way...”

“Ah, no need to worry. What I decide is our way. Isn’t that right?”

“...”

“And in my view... Mount Hua’s method is a bit weak.”

“What?”

The Green Forest warriors gaped and stared blankly at Im So-Byeong, their eyes full of disbelief.

“If you want to train like real combat, you have to do it like real combat. The Justice faction pretends because their warriors are weak and afraid someone might die.”

“...”

“If a person is truly determined, they must accept sacrifice. Isn’t that right?”

“...”

“Hehehe. I never thought I’d compete in strategy with Mount Hua’s Sword Saint like this. I’ll show you properly what the Evil Sect’s way is.”

The Green Forest warriors’ faces began to darken.

The next morning.

“Sigh.”

Hyun Jong let out a deep sigh.

Considering his situation, it would be strange if he had no worries, but even so, Hyun Jong’s expression was unusually dark today.

“It shouldn’t have come to this...”

In fact, they did not intend to settle at the Yangtze River.

Originally, they planned to rescue the refugees and then return to Shaanxi.

But suddenly, the Demonic Cult appeared, complicating matters and forcing them to stay here.

Of course, Hyun Jong did not view this situation entirely negatively.

Since the Heavenly Friends Alliance, including the alliance, stayed together, he thought it was the best opportunity to build camaraderie.

Yes. For camaraderie... for camaraderie.

However.

“How on earth...”

Instead of building camaraderie, the rift between them seemed to deepen.

From Hyun Jong’s perspective, leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, it was a situation so frustrating it felt like his insides were rotting.

“Ugh.”

Hyun Jong shook his head hard, recalling Chung Myung’s chuckling face.

That guy wouldn’t act without thought, but surely this ongoing situation was not good.

“This won’t do.”

He had to intervene at the right moment before the guy rampaged further.

Deciding to personally observe today’s training, Hyun Jong quickly moved toward the training ground.

Since Chung Myung hadn’t appeared yet, if he took a seat first and kept an eye during training, the situation might improve a bit.

But Hyun Jong’s expectations shattered the moment he arrived at the training ground.

“Aaaaaah!”

“Hey, you Evil Sect bastard!”

“You damn poisoners!”

“You’re the biggest problem! You Mount Hua demons!”

“...”

Hyun Jong stared blankly at the chaotic scene of the training ground.

There were no longer allies or enemies.

Mount Hua. Tang Family. Namgung Family. Green Forest.

Each sect, powerful enough to represent the world, was tangled up and fighting recklessly.

“Uh...”

Hyun Jong’s mouth dropped open.

“Dieeeee!”

Seeing Tang Soso’s graceful Hwa Swift Kick strike her brother’s jaw away, Hyun Jong involuntarily closed his eyes tightly.

This was the endless hell. This place.

“Wow. They’ve already started on their own.”

“...”

Hyun Jong slowly turned his head and looked at Chung Myung strolling over.

“I didn’t even order it. But well done. Hoo. This is the reward of teaching! Reward!”

“...”

It seemed.

The Heavenly Friends Alliance was ruined.

The way a person looks at another person is generally consistent.

The gaze of a mother looking at her grown child, a father looking at his beloved daughter, or a master looking at a proud disciple is mostly similar in most situations.

But the looks those here now gave Chung Myung were incredibly complex.

“Why?”

And Chung Myung seemed completely unable to understand why they were looking at him like that.

“Why are you looking at a man with such eyes?”

Finally, a groan escaped from Hyun Jong’s lips.

Seeing Chung Myung looking at him innocently with bright eyes as if he had done nothing wrong made Hyun Jong feel a surge of anger inside.

But Hyun Jong is a Taoist. So he spoke as calmly as possible.

“...Chung Myung.”

“Yes?”

“It seems there might be some problems in the Alliance right now.”

“What? Problems?”

“...”

“Here?”

Chung Myung tilted his head as if he had no idea.

Unconsciously looking at Chung Myung’s expression again, Hyun Jong lamented his own foolishness. Why look at that face and upset himself even more?

“Ugh...”

When Hyun Jong was too angry to continue, Hyun Sang gave a bitter smile and spoke. Isn’t it the elder’s role to say what needs to be said at times like this?

“The Sect leader is worried about what’s happening in the Heavenly Friends Alliance right now.”

“Ah, that?”

Chung Myung nodded as if he understood.

“It’s definitely concerning. I understand what the Sect leader is saying.”

“You understand?”

Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung suspiciously. This guy isn’t usually so quick to understand normal stuff...

And as expected, Chung Myung did not betray Hyun Jong’s expectations this time either.

“You mean that we’ve lacked basic training because we’ve only been through real combat. I’ve been thinking about that myself.”

“...”

“Ha. This is really not being easy on me. Originally, both should be done in parallel. You can’t be too biased to one side... Should I tell them to sleep less?”

“If they do that, they’ll all die...”

“Eh, do people really die from that much? No, they don’t, they don’t.”

Hyun Jong pounded his chest while looking at Chung Myung, who waved his hand as if that was impossible.

“Hey, you! Do you know how the Alliance members treat each other these days?”

“What?”

“It’s lucky if they only fight in the training ground! In three days, there have been three knife fights in the dining hall! And twice they’ve had brawls with the neighboring pavilion users while sleeping time was still in due!”

“...”

“And! If they’re going to fight, at least fight nicely! They break walls in the rented manor! They tear off roofs! They even set fires?”

“Wow, the fire was a bit much.”

The Mount Hua disciples already had the experience of losing their pavilion to a fire set by those damned bastards when they invaded, so just hearing fire makes them wake up sweating cold.

Ah, so that’s why it was set?

It must be Im So-Byeong. This is definitely Im So-Byeong. He is truly not to be underestimated...

“What on earth is this nonsense! The money paid in damages is going to be more than the rent for the manor!”

“Eh, we made a lot of money anyway.”

“Is money the problem here? Money?”

“Exactly!”

At that moment, Hyun Young shouted loudly and came to help Hyun Jong.

Startled, Hyun Jong quickly turned his head to look at Hyun Young. This guy isn’t usually on his side?

“Hey, you! No matter how much money you have, if you spend it recklessly like that, you’ll end up a beggar! The more you have, the more you should save! That’s how you have a comfortable old age!”

...So it was you?

Hyun Jong sighed deeply with a ‘that figures’ expression.

“Chung Myung.”

“Yes?”

“I’m really worried.”

Hyun Jong’s face became serious, but Chung Myung’s face remained endlessly bright.

“Fights break out almost every day.”

“Kids naturally grow up by fighting.”

“Injuries are not uncommon either.”

“Kids get hurt when they fight.”

“Their feelings toward each other are worsening day by day.”

“Kids are naturally petty and get offended easily, but the next day they reconcile as if nothing happened...”

“Listen seriously!”

Hearing Chung Myung’s ear-piercing answers, Hyun Jong finally shouted in frustration and grabbed the stiff back of his neck.

“Ugh!”

“Oh dear, Sect leader!”

“You’re old, so don’t get so worked up! Why do you keep doing that!”

“Ugh...”

Hyun Jong took deep breaths and looked at Chung Myung. But no matter what Hyun Jong did and said, Chung Myung still wore the most innocent expression as if asking, ‘Did I do something wrong?’

‘That detestable...’

At times like this, he really wanted to kick him. Hyun Jong’s greatest misfortune was that the one who brought him great fortune was also the one who gave him heartache.

“Hey, you!”

“Yes?”

“What kind of place is the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

Hyun Jong spoke half in admonishment, half in anger.

“Didn’t you yourself say it’s a place where everyone becomes friends with one heart!”

“Did I...?”

“Hey!”

“Ah, I remember. I do.”

“Ugh...”

Hyun Jong took a deep breath as if his anger was about to explode and continued deliberately.

“But all you’ve done after gathering everyone is to make them fight! To split them into factions and make them throw punches! Isn’t this just causing the Alliance to split!”

“Split?”

Hearing that, Chung Myung’s eyes widened.

“Yes! Isn’t it splitting!”

“No, Sect leader. What do you mean?”

Chung Myung asked as if he had never heard such an absurd thing in his life.

“They were never truly united in the first place, so how can it be splitting? You have to give birth, raise, and marry off a child first.”

“...What kind of Taoist uses such a metaphor?”

“It’s actually true though.”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“It’s called the Heavenly Friends Alliance, but when have we ever been close with the Green Forest or Namgung family? We haven’t even been close with Sichuan Tang family either.”

“...But we are close.”

“The Sect leader and the family lord might be close.”

“No, even the kids...”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung grinned and looked at Hyun Jong.

“Oh dear. Those so-called close kids grab each other’s collars and fight like they’re going to kill each other just because of a little poke. Wow! The chivalry of the Central Plains has hit rock bottom. There’s no need to curse the Evil Sect bastards.”

Hyun Jong was left speechless and stared blankly at Chung Myung like a mute who had honey stuck in his mouth. Actually, that wasn’t entirely wrong. If the Heavenly Friends Alliance really got along well, this situation wouldn’t have happened.

In fact, the Heavenly Friends Alliance is inherently a place where they can’t get along.

No matter how much the sect leader decides the sect’s affairs, hearts don’t always follow. Even if the sect leaders agree to socialize, the disciples don’t necessarily tear down their emotional walls.

But that didn’t mean he agreed with Chung Myung’s words.

“So all the more, shouldn’t such things not happen?”

“Why?”

“If they’re not close, shouldn’t they be made close? But if they keep fighting every day, won’t their relationship only worsen?”

“What do you mean?”

“Huh?”

This time, Chung Myung truly tilted his head as if he didn’t understand, not joking.

“Is there a better way for people to become close than fighting?”

“...Huh?”

“Usually, after exchanging some punches, people become very close.”

Hyun Jong, staring blankly at Chung Myung, suddenly asked as if he understood something.

“Could it be... Chung Myung?”

“Yes.”

“That... the closeness you mentioned... does it mean the other person stops arguing or suddenly acts friendly...?”

“Exactly. Also offering drinks.”

“...”

“And meals.”

“...”

Hyun Jong closed his eyes tightly. Moisture gathered at the corner of his eyes.

What kind of life did this guy live before entering Mount Hua? What kind of endless hell did he crawl through to have such a mindset?

“That’s not becoming close, that’s surrendering, isn’t it?”

“That’s what I mean.”

“How can those be the same thing!”

At that moment, Chung Myung chuckled.

“Sect leader. What do you think is the fundamental reason those guys are fighting now?”

“Because you’re sowing discord.”

“...”

“Isn’t it?”

“Well... to some extent, a very small part is true, but that’s not the fundamental reason.”

Chung Myung said bluntly.

“Sect leader. We are Taoists, but before that, we are warriors.”

“What do you mean by that?”

“Warriors are a breed who inevitably live with the pride of wanting to test who is stronger.”

Hyun Jong closed his mouth. Seeing that expression, Chung Myung grinned.

“If you gather hot-blooded kids and just tell them to respect each other, will that simply work? If it’s a problem that will explode someday, it’s better to let it explode early and clear it up.”

Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung with a face of disbelief.

“So you mean... they’re having a hierarchy fight now?”

“You could say that.”

“No, they’re not neighborhood dogs, what hierarchy...”

“Eh. It’s the opposite.”

Chung Myung waved his hand.

“Even neighborhood dogs do it, how can people not? It’s only natural.”

“...”

“The phrase ‘our side is stronger’ never disappears across East and West and all times. Rather than forcibly suppressing it, it’s better to let them fight it out.”

Hyun Jong’s mouth dropped open.

“Ah, no, this is...”

This sounds somewhat consistent with Taoist logic.

Taoism does not forcibly resist the natural flow, whether it be the flow of the world or the human heart. Forcibly blocking it only makes things worse.

“If someone else said this, I’d praise them for being excellent...”

The problem is that this guy treats the sacred Taoist philosophy as a mere excuse to strengthen his sophistry.

“The words... yes, the words are right. Ahem!”

But Hyun Jong, the Sect leader of the Taoist sect, found it too difficult to refute this. After carefully choosing his words several times, he finally spoke.

“But... yes. Even so, isn’t it better to get along well? Ideally, shouldn’t it be in harmony with Heaven’s will?”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung made a strange expression.

“So you mean, no matter what you think inside, outwardly pretend to be close and laugh and joke?”

“Well, not quite that much.”

“That’s good. Of course, that’s not bad either.”

“Huh?”

Hyun Jong tilted his head at the unexpected answer. What is this guy trying to say again?

Sure enough, a wicked smile appeared on Chung Myung’s lips.

“But that... place where they keep up appearances, pat themselves on the back thinking they’re doing great things, and outwardly laugh and pretend to be close.”

“...”

“I know such a place very well. Have you heard of the Nine Great Sects?”

Hyun Jong couldn’t control his bursting anger and covered his face.

“Oh dear, if the Sect leader wants it to be like the Nine Great Sects, what can I do as your disciple? I have no choice but to step into that world of hypocrisy and pretense with tears in my eyes...”

“Cut it out, you brat!”

“Hehehehe.”

Chung Myung, who had thoroughly upset Hyun Jong, suddenly became serious and spoke.

“Sect leader, you really don’t want that, do you?”

Hyun Jong had no choice but to nod.
.

You say it’s like the Nine Great Sects.

That statement was utterly unbearable. How many times had Hyun Jong been disappointed by the hypocrisy of the Nine Great Sects?

But upon reflection, the words he uttered were not so different from the behavior of the Nine Great Sects. They hid their true feelings and repeatedly put on a good face, and what accumulated and festered was exactly what the Nine Great Sects have become now.

Chung Myung looked at Hyun Jong squarely and said.

“It’s strange if there are no problems between people in the first place, so there’s no way sects can harmonize without any issues.”

“...”

“Of course there are problems. The important thing is how we solve those problems, isn’t it?”

“That’s true, but...”

The problem is that the way to solve them is quite unreliable, isn’t it?

“Removing hypocrisy is a good thing. But if the result leaves only bad feelings between each other, isn’t that worse than just pretending?”

The Nine Great Sects don’t pretend out of desire. Fundamentally, society is a place where it’s hard to reveal one’s innermost feelings openly.

The phrase “friendly competition” sounds nice, but in reality, it’s like dragons or unicorns—words exist but the substance does not. When people compete, it’s natural to feel jealousy.

Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders.

“You might think that way, but ignoring problems because they’re difficult isn’t the solution, right?”

“Hmm...”

Hyun Jong suddenly felt this situation was quite strange.

Usually, he spoke the sound argument while Chung Myung often spouted sophistry. But now, the words coming from Chung Myung were closer to the sound argument.

The kind of sound argument that’s hard to uphold and practice.

“I can’t help but be worried.”

“Sect leader.”

Chung Myung looked at Hyun Jong with serious eyes.

“If they weren’t Tang Gunak, Green Forest, or Namgung, what would you think?”

“Hmm?”

A firm voice came from Chung Myung’s mouth.

“If there was a rift between the Baek disciples and the Chung disciples of Mount Hua, would you really tell them to just cover it up and pretend to be close like now?”

Hyun Jong pressed his lips tightly.

‘Surely...’

If the Baek disciples and Chung disciples hid ill feelings and acted hypocritically, Hyun Jong would have tried to resolve it by any means.

Yes, by any means. Because that would never be good for Mount Hua as a whole.

“...Are you saying I am putting up walls against them?”

“It’s not exactly a wall. But... you probably don’t think of them as truly one huge sect.”

Hyun Jong furrowed his brows. Is that really so? He wanted to deny it, but his mouth wouldn’t easily open contrary to his thoughts.

Chung Myung shrugged and said.

“There’s no need to be so serious. It’s not as easy as it sounds, right?”

“...”

“I was the same. I thought this was enough. But... it wasn’t.”

Seeing Hyun Jong silent, Chung Myung smiled bitterly.

“If we talk about friends and such, we should consider them as one sect first. So far, what we’ve done is no different from saying our kids must train hard and suffer, but others’ kids should be pampered and cherished.”

“...”

“It looks like favoring others’ kids on the surface, but in reality, it’s just loving only our own. Shouldn’t we stop that now?”

Hyun Jong gave a hollow laugh.

The Chung Myung he knew was fanatically devoted to Mount Hua. Sometimes, he felt Chung Myung cared for Mount Hua several times more than even the sect leader himself.

Such a person was saying not to discriminate between Mount Hua and other sects.

“...Chung Myung.”

“Yes, Sect leader.”

“Is that the right thing to do?”

“Yes. I think so.”

“Even if problems arise because of that?”

“Yes. All problems cease to be problems once solved. The danger is not that problems arise, but that we blindly cover them up fearing problems might arise.”

A sigh escaped from Hyun Jong’s mouth.

“And.”

“Hmm?”

Chung Myung grinned.

“If they fight and clash like that, they might not get along happily like before, but at least... they will be able to trust the one standing behind them on the battlefield.”

“...”

“Isn’t that enough?”

Hyun Jong shook his head.

“I thought you wanted a close friend. But now I see you want not a friend, but a comrade-in-arms.”

“No. What I want is a true friend.”

“...”

“Not just pretending to be close on the surface, but someone who insults and criticizes each other, yet runs to help first in times of crisis.”

Tang Gunak, who had been quietly listening to their conversation, spoke up.

“So far, Mount Hua has fulfilled that role well.”

“Yes, that’s right. It happened that way somehow. But what I hope for is not that. I want everyone in the Heavenly Friends Alliance to have that kind of relationship.”

“That’s a difficult task.”

“Yes, it is difficult.”

Chung Myung said calmly.

“Of course, sustaining it will be difficult. But... if we try and make an effort, wouldn’t it be possible for a while?”

“Until we face the Four Sects Alliance and the Demonic Cult?”

Chung Myung did not answer that. He just smiled slyly. Seeing that, Tang Gunak shook his head.

“It’s closer to delusion than ideal... but anyway, I have no complaints about that.”

“Yes?”

“Just the chance to reorganize Tang Family is enough to satisfy me.”

After finishing, Tang Gunak muttered gloomily, “The final winner is Tang Family, damn Mount Hua bastards.” Seeing this, Hyun Jong quickly gave Chung Myung a warning look, as if to say, “How can you say that while he’s like that?”

But Chung Myung kept grinning. Hyun Jong finally sighed.

‘When hasn’t he been like this...’

Hyun Jong knew well that Chung Myung never acted without thought. This time, he was just more worried because it involved not only Mount Hua but other sects as well.

“Chung Myung.”

“Ah, don’t worry, Sect leader.”

“...”

“There will be no problem.”

Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung with solemn, deep-set eyes.

‘Still the same as ever.’

In truth, Hyun Jong did not want to protect Tang Gunak or other sects. He was worried that if serious problems arose from Chung Myung’s actions, the responsibility would fall on Chung Myung himself.

But Chung Myung seemed to have already guessed Hyun Jong’s feelings.

“Alright. I understand. If that is truly your will...”

Hyun Jong nodded heavily. In the end, he had no choice but to support Chung Myung.

“But Chung Myung, that’s not the only problem.”

“...What else is there?”

“I fully understand your thought that it’s better to foster... no, to let this situation continue. But whether they can endure this situation is a separate matter, isn’t it?”

“What do you mean by that...”

At that moment, Hyun Sang flipped through the ledger in his hand and spoke.

“Up to today, Namgung has fifteen injured and twenty lightly wounded. The Green Forest has twenty-eight injured, and the lightly wounded... are unaccounted for. Tang Family also has ten injured.”

“We didn’t bother counting the lightly wounded. Tang Family can treat those on their own.”

“...So you say.”

After Hyun Sang and Tang Gunak finished speaking, Hyun Jong frowned.

“Do you understand?”

“...”

“Recently, the frequency of injuries has sharply increased. Moreover, the severity of injuries is gradually worsening. To me, this seems less about intense training and more about bodies not being able to endure it.”

“Uh...”

Hyun Jong smiled knowingly, as if there was no escape this time.

“No matter how good the training is, if the body can’t endure it, it’s useless, isn’t it? You usually consider this, but it seems you missed it this time.”

“No, but there’s Tang Family...”

Chung Myung glanced at Tang Gunak, who quietly smiled.

“We’re doing our best, but it’s not easy.”

“You’re really doing your best, right? Really?”

“Haha. That’s obvious. Do you think I discriminate against those who eat the same food because of petty pride?”

He didn’t used to be like that. But lately, I’ve had some doubts...

“I swear I’m not.”

“Ugh.”

Of course, Tang Gunak believes him.

But I can’t trust the Tang Gunak blood flowing in him. Because in that man’s body flows the blood of a person who once calmly swindled with the most wronged face in the world.

“Tsk.”

There is suspicion but no evidence. Chung Myung glanced suspiciously at Tang Gunak, then turned his gaze back to Hyun Jong.

“So it’s better to patch things up moderately here. After all, if they become sickly and can’t fight properly after finally uniting, it would be worse than not uniting.”

“Ah, you don’t have to worry about that.”

“Yes, good thought... Huh? What did you say?”

“I said you don’t have to worry.”

“...Ah, no. Chung Myung.”

“So you mean there must be a way for the kids to endure while maintaining the current situation.”

“Is that... so?”

“Ehhh, Sect leader. Who am I? I’ve got all that prepared!”

“...Huh?”

Is there really a way for this?

“Well, what are you planning to do?”

The most practical way is to reduce training intensity, but that’s practically impossible. This training isn’t commanded by Chung Myung.

“How about the Mount Hua kids?”

“Huh? Mount Hua?”

Hyun Jong looked at Hyun Sang for an answer. Hyun Sang replied with a somewhat reluctant expression.

“Mount Hua... has about twenty lightly wounded and no injured.”

“No, why?”

“...Well, maybe because they’re unnecessarily sturdy?”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“The kids aren’t sturdy just because they received Mount Hua’s cultivation. That’s all the result of continuous physical conditioning, isn’t it?”

“...”

“Can’t endure? Then make them endure. Don’t make excuses to escape because you’re weak.”

“Chung Myung, what exactly have you prepared?”

“They’ll probably arrive soon.”

“What?”

“What... both what and who. The Beast Palace and Ice Palace will come soon. I’ve sent word through the Eunha Merchant Guild. They’ve been told to bring everything they’ve gathered over the past three years.”

“...”

“If we shove a Heavenly Violet Pill into their mouths, they won’t be able to rest even if they want to. At the same time, we should mix in the Beast Palace and Ice Palace people too. Those fellows have been living comfortably using the excuse of being far away, but we can’t keep watching that.”

“Uh... well...”

Chung Myung, you say you’ll take care of the Ice Palace and Beast Palace too in such a strange way. It’s easy for listeners to misunderstand...

“Hehehehehe!”

Chung Myung grabbed his belly and laughed heartily.

“We’ve been the only ones bleeding so hard all this time, we can’t keep watching that. Now, if we die, we all die together! No one can ever say later that we didn’t get anything and backed out! Uhehehehe!”

Ah... so it wasn’t a misunderstanding.

Yes. Yes.......

“Uryaaaaaah!”

Baek Cheon swiftly struck down the flying poisoned blade.

The poisoned blade, coated with venom so strong it could put a person into a coma just by grazing them, could not overcome the power contained in the sword and was flung high into the sky.

“...I roughly understand it.”

“Indeed.”

Baek Cheon gripped his sword tightly and looked at those standing before him.

“So this training... isn’t just about adapting?”

He realized this while fighting Tang Gunak, Namgung, and the Green Forest. Despite fighting so much, their real combat experience was still lacking.

To be precise, they have abundant experience in one-on-one battles but are extremely lacking in group combat experience. Especially when multiple groups with different tendencies fight together, they cannot even demonstrate half their true strength.

Probably Chung Myung saw this clearly. That’s why he made them undergo this training.

And once again, Chung Myung’s scheme showed definite effect. The more they fought, the faster they understood how to handle this chaotic battle. This is what it means when the body understands and moves before the mind does.

Yes, good. All good. The problem is...

‘It’s not just us experiencing this.’

Those gentlemen are absorbing not only group combat experience but also real combat experience through this training alone. They grow stronger and sharper day by day.

So much so that Baek Cheon, who faces them, is repeatedly surprised.

‘Have people watching us felt like this all along?’

Watching those visibly growing right in front of him is a very unique experience. Especially when clashing swords and feeling that growth physically, emotions multiply.

Watching someone grow is both fulfilling and evokes a sense of crisis to your position. The will to never be caught up ignited naturally.

This was definitely a good thing.

...If only it was just their ‘skill’ that was growing.

“But... this is a bit...”

Baek Cheon looked displeased at those slowly approaching him.

Tang Pae, holding poisoned blades in both hands with a sinister smile, was understandable. After all, for Tang Family, this training started with the purpose to defeat Mount Hua.

But...

“Hmmm.”

Namgung Do-Wei, who was creeping closer with a slightly awkward but resigned expression...

“Hehehehe...”

And Im So-Byeong, approaching with a blatant ‘you’ve been caught’ expression, was a bit different, wasn’t he?

“Um...”

Baek Cheon quietly raised his hand and gently spoke to those approaching.

“It seems... the meaning of the training is somewhat fading...?”

“Huh?”

“...Wasn’t this supposed to be a chaotic battle?”

“Hahaha.”

The one who responded was none other than Im So-Byeong.

“Oh dear, it seems like taoist Baek Cheon is under some misunderstanding.”

“Yes?”

“...You still think this looks like training? Huh?”

Baek Cheon shivered as he saw Im So-Byeong growling openly.

“Well, if you think about it... it is training, isn’t it?”

But Tang Pae seemed to have a different opinion from that lowly Evil Sect guy. Indeed, the young lord of the prestigious Sichuan Tang Family...

“Training is training. But it’s training where only Mount Hua enjoys the fun alone.”

“...”

No, no. Tang Pae was colder. Well... thinking about it, Tang Pae probably harbored more ill feelings.

“We want to enjoy that fun too... but you’re really narrow-minded, Taoist Baek Cheon.”

“...So you poisoned the food we ate yesterday?”

“Hahaha. Isn’t it Mount Hua who taught us not to spare any means or methods when facing enemies?”

“Still, that’s a bit...”

Namgung Do-Wei, who had been silently listening, spoke up.

“Please don’t misunderstand, Taoist Baek Cheon. There is no ill will in this whatsoever.”

“...”

“This is merely a strategic decision. If we just mix and fight wildly, Mount Hua keeps winning, right? There’s nothing more foolish than repeating losing wars. Isn’t that so?”

That’s right, Young Lord of Namgung. Of course.

But to make that argument convincing, you might want to move that twitching sword aside first... The sword keeps twitching as if it wants to stab my throat—is that just my imagination?

“Until now, it must have been fun.”

“The feeling of winning and returning must have been very refreshing.”

“It’s not bad to start tasting the bitter taste of defeat. We’ve tasted it enough to become stronger. This is all done with Mount Hua in mind.”

“You are salivati...”

“Hahaha. Just kidding.”

Namgung Do-Wei joked and cut off Baek Cheon’s words.

“Mount Hua should have no problem facing the three of us simultaneously.”

“There is a problem.”

“Ahh, that must be so. Because it’s Mount Hua.”

“...I said there is a problem! Hey?”

Baek Cheon broke out in sweat and stepped back hesitantly. The other Mount Hua disciples guarding him also tightly gripped their swords and slowly retreated.

But they were surrounded on three sides, and the pavilion blocked the rear. Where could they possibly go? This was a battlefield where strategic retreat did not exist.

“I finally understand the profound teachings of Taoist Chung Myung.”

“So he means to win, we must first strike the head?”

“The head here is Mount Hua?”

Madness gleamed in the eyes of the three leading each sect. Though they spoke politely, their eyes showed no mercy. Their determination to crush Mount Hua burned like wildfire.

Baek Cheon let out a bitter laugh.

‘...Bastards.’

It’s truly good that their skills improve. Mount Hua can also ignite its will by watching them. But...

“Acting this dirty and cheap crosses the line!”

“Crush them!”

“Smash them!”

“You picked the wrong day! Mount Hua bastards!”

The alliance of Tang Pae, Namgung Do-Wei, and the Green Forest suddenly attacked Mount Hua. The Mount Hua disciples’ faces turned pale seeing the overwhelming wave.

“They’re coming!”

“Damn bastards!”

“Hey! Block them, block them!”

“Aah! Don’t let them spray poison first!”

Mount Hua resisted fiercely, but the three sects, determined to completely crush Mount Hua this time, charged without hesitation.

“Trample them!”

“This is revenge for comrades who died by your swords!”

“We didn’t kill them, you bastard!”

“Who cares!”

Amid the chaos, a trace of sorrow filled Baek Cheon’s heart.

Chung Myung... They say it’s the Mount Hua fire over all of Central Plains, but isn’t this the Chung Myung fire of all Central Plains? I’m not sure if this is really okay. Hey? Chung Myung...

Meanwhile, at one side of the training ground.

Chung Myung, watching the training ground that starkly showed the ruthless reality of the murim world, popped a bean into his mouth.

“They’re having so much fun playing.”

Kids grow by fighting, after all.

“Are you okay with this?”

“With what?”

“It looks like Mount Hua is getting beaten up?”

Chung Myung chuckled at Tang Gunak’s words.

“Those guys have been a bit arrogant lately; it’s not bad to let them lose some air this time.”

“Haha.”

Tang Gunak laughed heartily and looked at Chung Myung.

“Haha. Funny joke...”

But he couldn’t finish his sentence. Chung Myung wasn’t laughing at all.

“...Is it not a joke though?”

“...”

“Ahem.”

Tang Gunak cleared his throat softly. For a moment, he underestimated Chung Myung.

Chung Myung said indifferently.

“These rookies nowadays think they’re strong.”

“They think they’re strong...”

Tang Gunak smiled bitterly.

Right now, Mount Hua is facing Tang Family, Namgung, and Green Forest simultaneously. Of course, they’re being pushed back, and those three forces aren’t at full strength, but the fact they are facing them is important.

If even they are called rookies, then who in the Central Plains isn’t a rookie?

“You seem to have excessively high standards.”

“The Central Plains’ standards are excessively low.”

“Maybe so.”

Having said all he wanted, Tang Gunak looked back at the training ground and quietly spoke without turning his gaze.

“Are you sure this is okay?”

Though the same question, this time the meaning was different. Chung Myung nodded as if understanding what was meant.

“It’s not something to be stopped.”

“But Heavenly Violet Pill is not a spiritual pill to be wasted easily. If used by Mount Hua, it might have greater effect.”

“If eating spiritual pills made everyone stronger, the emperor would be the strongest person in the world. Or maybe the merchants would be.”

“That’s true, but...”

The important thing is balance.

There is a limit to the benefits gained from spiritual pills. Generally, without taking spiritual pills, the level of martial skill is higher than internal qi. Therefore, taking spiritual pills dramatically increases martial ability.

However, after possessing a certain level of internal qi, spiritual pills become less useful. If not, each sect would not have concentrated spiritual pills on their top warrior instead of young disciples.

“Mount Hua guys have been consuming so much that it no longer matters.”

“Of course, that’s true. Yes, it is. But...”

Tang Gunak turned his gaze and looked Chung Myung straight in the eyes.

“Those who will become Mount Hua disciples aren’t the last to get, are they?”

Chung Myung fell silent at those words. Tang Gunak sighed softly and spoke as if determined.

“You know too, but ice crystals and purple grass herbs don’t grow infinitely. In the North Sea, ice crystal production has drastically dropped. They’ve been mined harshly, so from now on, only tiny amounts can be obtained. Otherwise, they have to use low-quality ice crystals.”

“Hmm...”

“Purple grass herb is the same. It wasn’t hard to find in the past. But now, even in Yunnan, it only grows in specific places. It’s impossible to cultivate, and its natural habitats are shrinking day by day.”

“Ugh...”

Chung Myung scratched his head. This wasn’t wrong. Considering the situations in the North Sea and Yunnan, the Heavenly Violet Pill’s usefulness would soon end.

“Perhaps the Heavenly Violet Pill you make this time might be the last batch. But are you going to give that precious pill to them? Not to Mount Hua’s successors?”

“...”

“The sect leader can’t be unaware of this. And the disciples will soon know. This isn’t a matter to decide lightly.”

Tang Gunak continued with a stern face.

“If the Heavenly Violet Pill is used well, Mount Hua might firmly hold the position as the world’s top swordsman sect not only in the next generation but perhaps beyond that. But if you use the pill like this, there will be no more spiritual pills to bring glory to Mount Hua for generations. Are you truly confident you won’t regret that decision?”

While listening, Chung Myung said nothing. He only wore an expression that was impossible to read.

“Hmmm.”

Chung Myung sighed deeply and faced Tang Gunak. Seeing his seemingly deep worries, Tang Gunak asked.

“Have your thoughts changed a little?”

“No, it’s not that.”

Tang Gunak looked at him with a puzzled expression. Then Chung Myung smirked and continued.

“I realized this is the kind of person who becomes the sect leader.”

“...Suddenly, what do you mean by that...?”

Chung Myung rested his chin on his fist.

“Well, Elder Hyun Young seems to think that way. Though he says no now, eventually I will become the sect leader.”

“...”

“It seems the sect leader also sometimes thinks that way.”

Tang Gunak gave a bitter smile.

In fact, to him, not having such thoughts would be stranger. Chung Myung’s influence in Mount Hua has even surpassed Hyun Jong. Even if Baek Cheon or Yoon Jong became sect leaders, if Chung Myung wished, making them puppets would be easy.

Whether Chung Myung has the desire or not is not important. What matters is that he can do it. Considering how big a problem this could be for the sect.

Even Tang Gunak himself had trouble dealing with the elders who refused to relinquish power.

“I think I understand what you’re thinking.”

Rather than splitting power in two, it might be better to concentrate it in Chung Myung alone. Not like the old Chung Myung; the current one quietly cares for people.

“But that’s a useless thought.”

“Hmm? Why is that?”

“Because I will never become the sect leader.”

Tang Gunak showed a completely baffled expression.

Of course, Chung Myung might think he has no desire for power. But Tang Gunak sees him as someone who throws a fit if things don’t go his way and breaks down obstacles to turn the tide.

Can someone who cannot be satisfied without directly leading and obtaining what he wants be said to lack ambition? He might mistakenly think it’s acceptable now...

Lost in thought, Tang Gunak calmly asked.

“If in the future, Taoist Yoon Jong, who becomes sect leader, acts completely opposite to your thoughts, what will you do?”

“What...?”

Tang Gunak pressed Chung Myung further into a corner, seeming a bit flustered.

“If you see that the then sect leader intends to execute a clearly disadvantageous action under his authority?”

“...”

“What will you do then?”

“I would persuade him.”

“And if he refuses to be persuaded?”

Tang Gunak’s voice was flat, devoid of emotion. Yet, that made it even clearer.

“Will you force him to submit? Or will you just let him go down the wrong path?”

Chung Myung hesitated to answer. Tang Gunak spoke now with a slightly reproachful tone.

“If you retreat, you must relinquish power. If you don’t, you shouldn’t retreat. If you act ambiguously, everyone else will suffer.”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung pressed his hand against his chin.

‘It isn’t like I didn’t know this.’

But knowing doesn’t always mean acting. People tend to postpone things which are not for immediate resolution in front of them, even knowing that the delay will bring greater trouble later.

“So rather...”

“No, I think there’s some misunderstanding here.”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung looked at Tang Gunak. His expression was indifferent, not matching the serious conversation.

“If such a situation happens, I’ll just leave it be.”

“...What, you?”

“Yes. What’s strange about that?”

“Isn’t it obvious? The you I know wouldn’t tolerate disciples going down the wrong path.”

“That’s true.”

“Then it’s a contradiction.”

“No. Your question is based on a wrong premise.”

Tang Gunak showed a puzzled look.

“The premise is wrong?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung said with an unwavering face.

“If such a situation occurs, there’s no need to worry. I would be wrong, and Sahyung Yoon Jong would be right.”

“...”

“If I’ve persuaded enough and he still doesn’t change his mind, then I’m simply wrong. Yoon Jong sahyung isn’t someone who would go down the wrong path or fail to recognize his mistakes from others’ words.”

Tang Gunak showed an incredulous expression.

“Does that make sense? Everyone makes mistakes and misjudgments.”

“Yes, that’s true. But then, Sahyung is not just a person but a Taoist, not just a disciple but the sect leader.”

“...”

“People make mistakes, but the sect leader does not. I will make it so.”

Tang Gunak was left speechless.

‘What on earth is this...’

How can a person trust another to this extent?

“Is it the same with Taoist Baek Cheon?”

“Ah... the sasuk is a bit different. That person is reliable in battle but tends to bluff in daily life.”

“...”

“If he does something foolish, I’ll immediately remove him and replace him with Yoon Jong sahyung. Of course, before that, Yiseol Sago will stab him with her sword and remove him.”

“Huh...”

Seeing Tang Gunak unable to hide his absurdity, Chung Myung smirked.

“You understand now why I shouldn’t become the sect leader.”

“...”

“Today, after hearing what the family lord said, I realized: Ah, the sect leader is this kind of person, and the sect leader must think about these things. Then I definitely shouldn’t become the sect leader.”

“Then shouldn’t you just become like one?”

“I’m not someone who can do that.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“You asked if it’s a waste, right?”

“Yes.”

This conversation started with that question: Isn’t it a waste to give the Heavenly Violet Pill to another sect instead of Mount Hua’s descendants? Here’s the answer.

“Not a bit.”

“...Really?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“That’s why I say, I don’t know Mount Hua’s future. I’ve tried to understand, but honestly, I still don’t. And no matter how much I understand, I don’t think I can truly care for it.”

“Can’t you just do it?”

“There are things done with the head and things done with the heart.”

Tang Gunak was momentarily speechless. Chung Myung waved his hand.

“So rather than trying to care for descendants I don’t even know or when they’ll come, I prefer to release the Heavenly Violet Pill to save even one more person now. To me, that’s a hundred times better.”

“I don’t understand. No matter how important the current disciples are, what about Mount Hua’s glory through generations...”

“It’s not even comparable.”

Tang Gunak flinched. Chung Myung’s voice cutting in carried an inexplicable chill.

But looking again, Chung Myung was still smirking.

“Or, well, we can make it that way. So that each surviving disciple becomes worth more than a hundred Heavenly Violet Pills. Someone who can pass on even more to the descendants.”

“...”

“Wouldn’t that be enough?”

Tang Gunak finally shook his head.

“I don’t know, at all.”

“That’s why I say, that’s not what I’m good at. Do you know what I realized this time?”

“What?”

“I don’t have to do everything. Everyone should do what they do best.”

Chung Myung turned his head and looked at the training ground.

“Baek Cheon sasuk will lead and develop Mount Hua. No one can do it better than him. Yoon Jong sahyung will achieve the Dao that Mount Hua currently lacks. Though Baek Cheon sasuk may have more fame, Yoon Jong sahyung will have a greater influence on future generations.”

“...”

“Yiseol Sago will become the manual of Mount Hua’s swordsmanship. Her existence alone will change how descendants approach the sword. Jo Gul sahyung... well, he might cause trouble, but the sect needs people like him.”

“Hmm...”

“And the family lord’s daughter will nurture the lacking female disciples of Mount Hua. She might not be as good as Sago, but she communicates well and will be a channel to convey things that sasuk or sahyung can’t say directly.”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“The rest are the same. Baek Sang sasuk will enrich Mount Hua’s finances, and other disciples will vividly pass on their experiences to future generations. Everyone has their role. There’s no one expendable here.”

Tang Gunak looked at Chung Myung again with a strangely renewed feeling.

He hadn’t thought Chung Myung looked at each Mount Hua disciple so carefully.

Then another question arose.

“Then, what do you exist for?”

“Me? That’s obvious.”

Chung Myung smiled showing his teeth.

“I’m the sword that slays Mount Hua’s enemies.”

“...”

“I’ll do anything to cut down those threatening Mount Hua now. Heavenly Violet Pill? It’s not a tad bit precious. To save even one disciple in Mount Hua, I wouldn’t hesitate to use a hundred or even a thousand pills. What’s so great about them anyway?”

A deep sigh escaped Tang Gunak’s lips.

“I thought I knew you well... but I still don’t you fully.”

“You only need to know one thing.”

“...What is that?”

“That I sincerely need the Heavenly Violet Pill that much.”

Tang Gunak looked at Chung Myung with a slightly sulky face. But seeing his still grinning face, he couldn’t help but smile.

“If you’re going to make me work, then take good care of the Heavenly Violet Pill.”

“Of course. Naturally.”

Tang Gunak, who had been looking at Chung Myung with a strange gaze, stood up and started walking.

“Are you leaving?”

“Because watching an obvious match isn’t fun.”

“It’s somewhat interesting. Well, see you later.”

Tang Gunak turned around and spoke.

“And... let me correct one thing.”

“Yes?”

“You said you’re the sword that slays Mount Hua’s enemies?”

“Yes.”

“I wouldn’t call a sword which slays someone but says they wouldn’t trade even one disciple for a thousand precious pills.”

“...”

“That’s a sword that protects. The sword that protects Mount Hua.”

With those words, Tang Gunak silently walked away, feeling Chung Myung’s gaze piercing his back.

‘He didn’t ask.’

Tang Gunak slowly closed his eyes.

There was one thing he truly wanted to ask. But even Tang Gunak didn’t dare to.

‘Everyone grows perfectly and leads Mount Hua in their own place. I understand that’s ideal.’

Surely, such Mount Hua would become an unparalleled great sect, perhaps the greatest in history.

But if Chung Myung’s role is the sword that protects Mount Hua, the sword that slays its enemies... then in a world without enemies, where does Chung Myung belong?

In a world where the sword is no longer needed, what should he live for?

‘Does he really think the sect leader position doesn’t suit him?’

Or...

Tang Gunak soon shook his head.

That’s a story for a far future. Yes, it’s too far. They still have countless mountains to climb; it’s not a topic for them yet.

Tang Gunak ambled away.

And Chung Myung watched his back intently, his eyes sinking into endless darkness.

The handsome man, staggering from poison intoxication, finally knelt on one knee. The numerous gazes that looked at him with disgust were suddenly filled with a base pleasure.

“Th-this...”

The trembling man ultimately could not raise his head and collapsed forward.

“Trashy... bastards...”

Thud.

The moment Baek Cheon, who remained until the end, fell, everyone surrounding him shouted in cheers.

“We have defeated the evil sect scoundrels!”

“We won!”

“How about that, Mount Hua scoundrels! This is justice!”

From the mouths of the Mount Hua disciples lying on the ground came agonized groans.

‘Justice was harsh...’

‘Then are we the evil ones, you bastards!’

The fact that even some rotten bandits were mixed among those shouting made them feel even more wronged.

No, just because they won a bit, they’re driving people like this. People who should know better...

‘This is just too much.’

‘Is it really going to be like this?’

But at that moment, Tang Family and Namgung family completely embraced each other, their eyes moist with tears.

“The moment I can release this grudge has come.”

“Sniff. Namgung hyung, you’ve worked hard.”

“When those bastards knocked us down last time and said, ‘This is not much,’ I really wanted to bite my tongue and die...”

At those words, the fallen Mount Hua disciples exchanged glances quietly.

‘Was that so?’

‘...I think I heard that too.’

“Don’t even mention it. When I heard ‘Ah, a famous family but so weak,’ I wanted to visit my ancestors’ graves and cry.”

The Mount Hua disciples—no, the Mount Hua bastards—flinched and looked at each other again.

‘We went that far?’

‘It was Jo Gul sahyung. I heard it myself.’

‘That bastard, seriously...’

Then, the bandits from the Green Forest, who couldn’t bring themselves to embrace, suddenly shouted with emotion.

“We even heard ‘Shenzhou Five Clans? Not all are the same!’”

“Wow, that’s harsh.”

“That was the worst. And we already have bad relations with the Ten Thousand People Clan.”

The Mount Hua disciples—no, the evil sect bastards of Mount Hua—quietly turned their heads. Everyone clearly remembered hearing this...

‘Was it the sasuk?’

‘It was Baek Cheon sahyung.’

‘That man, when drunk, is even worse than Jo Gul.’

Of course, it wasn’t only Baek Cheon who was drunk. If only one or two had done such things, it would have been obvious immediately, but the fact that no one knew until now means many did similar things.

“Justice prevails, you trash!”

“How about that! Now you understand our rage!”

“Hahahahaha!”

Watching the triumphant three-faction alliance revel in victory, the Mount Hua disciples gritted their teeth. Especially Jo Gul, who couldn’t endure this situation, flared his eyes and sharply raised his head.

“Th-these filthy bastards... Have no pride! A joint attack!”

“Heh heh heh. That’s understandable, Taoist Jo Gul.”

Im So-Byeong gently fanned his fan. Of course, one of his eyes was bruised blue, so it didn’t look good at all.

“I never expected Taoist Jo Gul to understand the strategy. Heh heh heh. What can we do? We live in different worlds.”

“Ugh...”

Jo Gul, bursting with rage, spat blood and collapsed.

In the training ground, the cheers of those who had completely crushed the Mount Hua scoundrels for the first time echoed for a long time.

“Kuhuhuhu.”

“...”

“Hyung, did you see that bastard Taoist Jo Gul’s face?”

“...”

“It feels like a ten-year-old chest congestion has suddenly cleared. Oh, I’m full even without eating today.”

“Is it really that good?”

“I know what you’re going to say, hyung.”

Tang Zhan placed both hands on his waist and wore a solemn expression.

“Is it something to be so happy about that three sects combined barely defeated one sect!”

“...”

“You’re about to say that, right?”

“Haha.”

Tang Pae laughed and shook his head. Tang Zhan spoke with a face that couldn’t stop smiling.

“I know it’s embarrassing, but today I just want to openly rejoice. So don’t stop me.”

“I won’t stop you, so enjoy it fully.”

Tang Pae chuckled. But Tang Zhan soon slumped awkwardly into his chair.

“Honestly, I want to rejoice all day, but I don’t have the strength left...”

Tang Zhan leaned his head back, his body weary like wet cotton.

“...I thought winning wouldn’t be tiring at all... but winning means fighting until the very end. It would be less exhausting to fall early.”

“I see.”

“Those Mount Hua bastards... they’re really something. How do they manage this every time...”

Tang Zhan didn’t finish his sentence and just stopped. Tang Pae turned his head to look at him. He had already fallen asleep as if fainted.

“...It must have been hard.”

In fact, their bodies had already reached their limits long ago. Sparring is different from training. It’s like a magic spell drains your stamina instantly.

Not just ordinary sparring, but a chaotic battle where friend and foe were hard to distinguish, fought day and night for days, so complete exhaustion was natural.

‘So training is this intense too.’

Then how much harder must those who fought the Demonic Cult for dozens of days without proper sleep have endured?

Surely the ancestors of the Tang Family fought their best on that battlefield. But did those present now truly honor their efforts?

‘Perhaps we have been too indifferent to our past.’

They say when immersed in glorious triumph, the blood shed to achieve it is buried, and that’s exactly right. What they should have truly focused on were not the achievements of defeating the Demonic Cult, but those who sacrificed to achieve them...

Tang Pae, who thought he should revisit the hundred-year-old records left in the family when he returned, soon fell into a deep sleep.

In the room where the two slept, only the lamp’s flame burned quietly.

Humans sometimes experience surpassing their own limits.

Those whose stamina was already broken beyond repair could endure only because of one driving force: the will to punch those annoying, filthy Mount Hua bastards in the face.

That intense will ignited their spirit and moved their bodies.

Of course, this would be a valuable experience for those who stained the training ground with blood and screams. Some would find a path to a higher realm, others would deeply question if the path they walk is right.

Even those who didn’t achieve such growth would benefit from the experience of fighting to their limits. Perhaps in the future, when standing on a precipice, this experience might save their lives.

Yes, it will surely help.

...In the distant future, that is.

“Ugh...”

“I feel like dying...”

“P-please kill me...”

But what mattered to them was not the distant future but the present moment. What remained for those who burned their bodies like a candle was melted wax... no, half-broken bodies and intense dull pain that felt like burning even with a touch.

Their lips cracked and eyes hollowed. At a glance, they looked so broken one might say, ‘Are these Beggars’ Union beggars?’ They barely stood on trembling legs.

“...I feel like dying, young lord.”

“...This feels harder than the fight on Plum Blossom Island.”

“Isn’t that a bit too much?”

“No. It really feels that way...”

Even the Namgung Family, who had experienced limits at Plum Blossom Island, couldn’t regain composure. Back then, the threat to life kept them going, but now there was no such support. They had to hold their shattered bodies up purely with willpower.

At least the Sichuan Tang Family and Namgung Family, who tried to stand and endure, were in a better state.

The Green Forest, with no concern for appearances or pride, just plopped down openly in the training ground, wailing.

“Oh... my, my back...”

“Hey, hyung. But lying down like that is a bit much, isn’t it?”

“You try lying down too. It’s so comfortable.”

“...Maybe I should?”

“Look at the boss over there. He’s already lying down.”

“...Really.”

Im So-Byeong had thrown aside his usual fan and was half-lying down. His already pale face now looked almost like a zombie.

“What... was I... seeking wealth and glory for...”

“Green Forest King, please watch your words. I’m afraid you might die soon like that.”

“Ugh...”

The words of someone whose pride for Mount Hua blinded him to his own illness were so tragic.

Normally, Tang Family and Namgung family would click their tongues at such Green Forest, but now they looked at him with endless envy.

‘Must be so comfortable.’

‘I want to lie down too.’

‘The Evil Sect is better. Why was I born in the Tang Family?’

The two sects realized anew that pride and such require at least some leisure to maintain.

At that moment, a group approached them.

“Oh, I feel so stiff.”

“The places I got hit yesterday hurt so much.”

“We have to take revenge today!”

“We can lose once, but not twice!”

They were Mount Hua disciples entering the training ground. Namgung Do-Wei and Tang Pae were dumbfounded watching the scene.

‘What?’

‘Why are they so lively already?’

It was incomprehensible.

Yesterday, didn’t they beat those martial artists... no, Taoist bastards thoroughly? Yet the ones who got beaten were lively while the fans were dying?

They trained together and fought together.

“Well, today... huh?”

Baek Cheon, who was about to shout loudly at the front, tilted his head.

“Why are these guys in this condition?”

“...”

“Did you guys fight among yourselves again yesterday?”

A sigh escaped from Namgung Do-Wei’s mouth.

‘Are they even human?’

This was not a matter of grit or experience difference. At this level, it’s safe to say they’re different species.

“...Those guys would die even without being hit?”

“Hmm... doesn’t it look like that?”

“Shall we just go back today?”

“Yoon Jong.”

“Yes?”

“I can’t help but be disappointed in you.”

“...Why?”

Baek Cheon scolded with a stern face.

“Even if tired, they are the scions of noble families! Don’t you know that such consideration is actually disrespect and rudeness?”

“Ah...”

“At times like this, it’s proper to do your best to beat... no, to face them!”

Taoist Baek Cheon... such manners are fine.

“But sasuk. We are still the Justice faction...”

“Huh? Us?”

“...Yes?”

“Yesterday, those people called us the Evil Sect?”

“...”

Baek Cheon grinned and approached those who were dying.

“If you’re treated like villains, you should act like proper villains, right?”

“Heh heh heh. That’s right.”

“I’ll kill you.”

“Zhan, come here. While I’m being nice. Come here. Or I’ll kill you. I’ll kill you whether you come or not.”

Watching Baek Cheon, Jo Gul, Yu Yiseol, and Tang Soso slowly step forward, Yoon Jong shook his head.

Isn’t this what real villains are?

“Blood revenge!”

“Revenge!”

Just as Baek Cheon rolled his eyes and charged forward, someone suddenly appeared from behind and kicked his backside.

“Grow up, you fool!”

“Aah!”

Chung Myung clicked his tongue watching Baek Cheon fall forward.

“I trusted this...”

“Chung Myung, it’s sasuk...”

“I am like this because it’s sasuk! Grow up!”

“That’s a fair point.”

Baek Cheon nodded in agreement and got up. Chung Myung clicked his tongue once and glanced over Namgung family, Tang Family, and Green Forest, who barely endured in zombie-like states. The direct descendants looked a bit better, but the rest were on the verge of death.

Chung Myung shrugged.

“I’d like to keep beating them up longer... but well, I have to consider the situation.”

“...Are we resting today?”

“Rest?”

Chung Myung’s eyes widened.

“Is there such a thing in this world?”

“...Damn...”

“Today, instead of sparring, we’ll do something else.”

“What? What kind of...”

“First, let’s welcome the guests.”

Namgung Do-Wei tilted his head at the sudden remark. Then Chung Myung looked to one side and said.

“They’re coming over there.”

At that moment, a loud roar reached Namgung Do-Wei’s ears. It was the cry of a beast he had never heard in his life.

“W-What is that sound?”

“Sounds like a tiger’s roar?”

“No... it also sounds like a cow’s moo?”

“Why are both cow and tiger sounds coming in here?”

The swordsmen of the Green Forest bandits and the Namgung Family blinked in confusion, unable to understand the situation at all.

However, the disciples of the Sichuan Tang family and Mount Hua seemed to have an inkling, and with a sudden “Ah!” they turned their heads sharply.

“No way?”

Without anyone saying a word, the disciples of Tang Family and Mount Hua rushed toward the door in a crowd. Then the Namgung Family and the Green Forest also caught the mood and began running along.

When Baek Cheon violently flung open the door, his eyes saw the scene he had guessed... no, a sight even more spectacular than he had imagined.

It was a procession. A long line along the riverbank. The problem was that the line was not made up of just people.

“Wow...”

“Amazing.”

“Is that a tiger?”

“That’s a snake though?”

“...Then what on earth is that?”

It was a scene that perfectly fit the word ‘varied.’ Literally, tiger-sized tigers, leopards that looked fierce at a glance, and gigantic snakes that could easily swallow a person whole...

Pwooooooo!

Even a mysterious giant animal with a long, snake-like nose raised high and let out a cry.

“W-What is that?”

“Is it a monster?”

“You idiots. That’s an elephant.”

“Elephant?”

“Yes! An elephant!”

“Whoa? I’ve never seen one in real life. So that’s what an elephant looks like?”

Just the sight of these huge beasts, seen for the first time in life, was astonishing, but adding to the shock were the robust men walking alongside them, seemingly unafraid.

Everyone’s eyes widened at this rare and unparalleled sight. Where else could one see such a scene?

Especially Namgung Do-Wei, seeing this for the first time, could not help but be greatly impressed.

“W-What on earth is this...?”

Tang Pae gave a wry smile and helped Namgung Do-Wei.

“These are the warriors of the Nanman(Barbarian) Beast Palace.”

“Ah...”

Namgung Do-Wei finally nodded as if he understood. He had known that the archers of the Nanman Beast Palace handled wild beasts, but there was a big difference between knowing in theory and seeing it with one’s own eyes.

“So they are our allies.”

“Yes. They are also fierce warriors of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Tang Pae smiled slightly.

“The Beast Palace is a sect that began alongside the Heavenly Friends Alliance, even before the Namgung Family or the Green Forest.”

Except for Mount Hua, Dang Pae is arguably the sect most familiar with the Beast Palace, due to frequent exchanges through Yunnan tea trade. Naturally, their evaluation was favorable.

“But...”

Pwooooooo!

Kuhuhuhuhuh!

“...Do they usually travel around with so many beasts?”

“...”

“They must have met many others on the way here... but wandering around like that in broad daylight is a bit...”

“Well... uh...”

Tang Pae scratched the back of his head awkwardly. Indeed, it would be hard to get used to that sight at first.

“Sometimes they do, sometimes they don’t... Recently, they haven’t been bringing many beasts. But this time, they’ve brought quite a lot with them”

Namgung Do-Wei blinked and watched the beasts approaching among the people. None were ordinary. Though not an expert on animals, he sensed an unusual aura from them.

‘Are they all spiritual beasts?’

Indeed, no matter how different Yunnan is from the Central Plains, such tiger-sized beasts wouldn’t be scattered everywhere. How could people live in such a place?

Even in Yunnan, these beasts were clearly special spiritual creatures.

Sure enough, the tiger leading the group walked proudly and looked at Namgung Do-Wei and his sect members as if waiting for them, baring its terrifying teeth.

Grrrrr.

The tiger’s low growl sent chills down the spines of those who heard it. That cry triggered a primal fear in humans.

Namgung Do-Wei swallowed dryly. Those beside him flinched and stepped back one step.

Grrrrr!

Seeing this, the tiger grew more fierce, slightly lowering its neck, then roared fiercely as if to swallow and crush the entire Yangtze River.

Kuhahahahahah!

A qi wave that seemed to shake the air. Even those who knew what the Beast Palace was, including Namgung, instinctively gripped their sword hilts at the tremendous roar.

Grrrr.

The tiger glared at everyone with bright yellow eyes and was about to roar again.

Dododododo.

With cute, quick footsteps, something darted out swiftly from among Mount Hua’s disciples.

“Huh?”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes flickered.

What darted out was an animal he knew well: the white weasel dressed in Mount Hua’s martial uniform he had seen many times.

The weasel hurried forward, reached right in front of the huge tiger, stood on two legs, and straightened its back.

“That...!”

Momentarily startled, Namgung Do-Wei instinctively reached out his hand.

Of course, he knew the marten was a spiritual beast, but the size difference with the tiger was extreme. The marten was barely big enough to wrap around a person’s neck, while the tiger was clearly much larger than an average cow.

How could he not be startled seeing such a small marten blocking the path of such a huge tiger?

Grrrr.

The tiger showed its terrifying fangs at the marten standing before it, as if ready to bite it in half at any moment.

With clear hostility in its eyes, the tiger crouched low as if starting a hunt. Its stiff fur bristled wildly, making it look more like a demon from the netherworld than a tiger.

Kuhuhuhuhuh!

Just as the tiger let out a roar so huge it shook its insides, Baek Ah, who had been tapping her tail on the ground in discomfort, leapt into the air and swiftly struck the tiger’s jaw with a sharp back paw.

Kwahang!

The tiger hit by Baek Ah’s back paw bounced away like a fired cannonball, skipping across the ground like a stone skipping on water.

Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud! Thud!

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes nearly popped out.

“Whoa...”

Baek Ah, having sent the tiger flying with one strike, spat on the ground and then stomped the floor with its back paw.

‘That...’

That silhouette looked very familiar. That...

Wait, do martens even spit? Is it really right to call that a marten now? Can it be called that?

The tiger that had been slammed to the ground trembled as it got up.

Kiiiiiiiiii!

As Baek Ah let out a piercing cry, the tiger visibly paled even to human eyes. The tiger that had charged like lightning quickly prostrated itself before Baek Ah.

Tap. Tap. Tap. Tap.

Baek Ah clicked her tongue with a trivial sound, then raised her tiny front paw, almost insignificant compared to the tiger, and tapped the tiger’s cheek.

It felt as if a voice saying “Get your act together” was heard.

Namgung Do-Wei was not the only one who heard that hidden voice; the tiger the size of a house hung its head in obvious sulkiness. Even a three-year-old child could tell who was stronger.

Baek Ah, who had instantly subdued the tiger, gleamed its eyes and looked back at the other beasts. Those beasts that had been so majestic moments ago quickly tucked their tails and avoided eye contact.

“Hahaha!”

At that moment, a booming laugh erupted. Shortly after, a man who looked twice as big as an average strongman stepped forward.

“Baek Ah has gotten even more ferocious. I thought sending her to the Taoist sect would improve her temper.”

Namgung Do-Wei flinched momentarily looking at the man. He had never seen anyone so intimidating just by appearance.

“Palace Lord!”

Meanwhile, the disciples of Mount Hua and Tang family brightened immediately upon seeing Beast Palace Lord Meng So.

‘Palace Lord? That person is?’

Tension appeared in Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes. Indeed, the Beast Palace Lord. His presence was no joke.

At that moment, a dry voice came from behind.

“Why did you bring all these creatures here?”

Meng So burst out laughing as he saw Chung Myung walking over with a scowl.

“Understand the situation now, didn’t you say to bring all the useful ones?”

“You mean these are useful?”

“No. The useful ones are others. Even if they’re spiritual beasts, they’re still wild animals. If you bring all the fighters, no one can control them. To avoid problems, we had to bring them along.”

“Ugh.”

“Don’t look at me like that. I didn’t bring them all. I only picked the more ferocious and bad-tempered ones.”

“...Ferocious and bad-tempered?”

“Huh?”

“Those guys?”

Chung Myung chuckled and gestured, and Meng So turned his gaze toward where he pointed.

Click!

Baek Ah raised her small front paw and pointed, and the beasts of the Beast Palace slunk away dejectedly in that direction. They looked utterly deflated.

“...Because every creature has its natural enemy.”

Even Meng So seemed surprised by this and gave a helpless smile.

“It didn’t seem this bad before. What on earth did you do to that one? She looks several times stronger than before.”

He didn’t bother to say that her temper seemed several times more violent too.

Chung Myung said casually.

“I didn’t really do anything. I just made her understand that if she just plays around as a beast cub, she’ll become an expensive scarf.”

“...must have had a hard time.”

“Me?”

“No, that one.”

“...”

Meng So shook his head. Who would have imagined anyone could handle spiritual beasts better than the Beast Palace people?

With a single gesture, Baek Ah herded the huge beasts into the warehouse, then tapped the floor with her back paw and hurried up to Chung Myung. It then placed its arms on its waist and pushed its belly forward.

“What?”

Tap! Tap!

Baek Ah slapped the floor with her tail, and Chung Myung chuckled disdainfully.

“Alright, alright. Good job.”

Kii!

Baek Ah nodded and quickly climbed onto Chung Myung, standing on one of his shoulders. Chung Myung glanced at Baek Ah and tapped her black nose with his index finger.

“But.”

“Kii?”

“If those guys cause trouble in the future, you’re the one who dies.”

“...”

“Let’s do well.”

Baek Ah sulked and lay weakly on Chung Myung’s shoulder.

Everyone watching that absurd scene shook their heads.

‘Is it the humans who are amazing, or the beasts?’

‘Anyway, nothing happening here made any sense till now.’

‘Let’s not think about it. Just accept it and be comfortable.’

Chung Myung chuckled looking at Meng So.

“It must not have been easy to bring all these beasts, but you arrived earlier than expected. Thank you for coming from afar.”

“You say the darndest things.”

Meng So smiled gently, a softness and warmth unimaginable from his intimidating appearance.

“When a friend calls, you naturally have to come.”

Chung Myung grinned at those words.

“Since I’ve met a friend, it’s time for a drink after a long time.”

“Hahaha! I knew you’d say that, so I brought all the Peach Blossom Fragrance wine.”

“Oh? That amazing liquor! Kuu! Time to indulge after a long time!”

The large Meng So and the slightly smaller Chung Myung linked arms and chuckled as they walked inside.

Namgung Do-Wei, standing dazed, asked with a flustered expression.

“Are those two usually that close?”

“...Surprisingly, yes.”

“...”

Namgung Do-Wei thought anew that it was truly remarkable to have such a bad temper yet be so sociable.

“Ah, you brought the Purple Grass Herb, right?”

At Chung Myung’s words, Meng So gave a bitter smile.

“Ah, you mean the Sacred Spiritual Herb. I brought it. But there’s a bit of a problem.”

“Huh? A problem?”

“It seems that supplying the Sacred Spiritual Herb will be difficult for a while.”

At those words, Chung Myung slightly frowned. Meng So sighed.

“Don’t make that face. We’re not saying this because we’re annoyed or have other demands. We’ve searched all of Yunnan but can’t find any more Sacred Spiritual Herb. The ones planted in the last Sacred Pool still need time to grow...”

Meng So scratched his head as if troubled.

“That Sacred Spiritual Herb doesn’t seem to be fully grown either, so we need to watch it for a while.”

After finishing, Meng So glanced at Chung Myung, knowing how precious the Purple Grass Herb was to him. After all, he had come all the way to Yunnan just to get it. To say he couldn’t have more now...

“Well, it can’t be helped.”

“Hmm?”

But surprisingly, Chung Myung shrugged as if it was no big deal.

“It’s not some weed that grows anywhere, and it won’t grow quickly just because we want it. We have to accept that.”

“Hmm... Is that so?”

“Don’t worry. If left alone, the plants will grow again.”

“It might take another ten years until the next harvest?”

“Ten years or a hundred years, if we can get it then, that’s fortunate. If not, then it can’t be helped.”

Chung Myung spoke calmly, then glanced at Meng So and chuckled.

“Why are you making such a grim face as if it’s a big deal?”

“Me? Hahaha!”

Somehow relieved, Meng So burst out laughing heartily.

He had secretly thought so. The reason Chung Myung treated Yunnan well was probably because Yunnan was essential for obtaining the Purple Grass herb and making money through tea trade.

If either of those became difficult, the treatment of Yunnan might change from before.

No matter how different he was, Chung Myung was still a Central Plains native.

But Chung Myung’s attitude was unchanged from before hearing the news, like someone scolding a friend for worrying unnecessarily because he couldn’t bring a gift.

“When you say that, I feel like I’m stingy.”

“Actually, you did have some traits that didn’t match your size.”

“Haha. I’ve never heard that before in my life.”

“Then the people you’ve met so far don’t know the Palace lord well.”

Meng So laughed heartily, as Chung Myung’s words seemed not wrong.

“Anyway, you are a truly fascinating person.”

“Huh? You’re quick to retort?”

“I can’t just take it.”

Meng So smiled. Though he took it as a joke, calling him fascinating was his honest feeling.

Whether Chung Myung guessed Meng So’s feelings and tried to comfort him or spoke from his true heart, that simple phrase reassured Meng So.

He knew the Heavenly Friends Alliance was not just bound by profit. Like Plum Sword Saint who helped Yunnan without expecting anything in return, Mount Hua was also a place that could reach out to them without reason.

But even knowing that, he couldn’t help but worry because the discrimination between Central Plains natives and outsiders was deeply rooted.

“Ah, come to think of it, this is your first time seeing this side, right?”

“Hmm?”

“Hey, Namgung.”

“Yes?”

Chung Myung gestured to call Namgung Do-Wei. Namgung Do-Wei approached awkwardly. Chung Myung introduced him briefly.

“He is the young lord of the Namgung Family. Well... his position is still young lord, but he will soon become the head of the Namgung Family.”

“Ah, is that so?”

Meng So narrowed his eyes slightly as he looked at Namgung Do-Wei.

“Indeed.”

Is there anyone in the world who doesn’t know the reputation of the Namgung Family?

A prestigious family among the prestigious. Among the many families in the Central Plains, the Namgung Family is top-ranked. Perhaps because he was the young lord of such a family, his aura was extraordinary at a glance.

“Nice to meet you. I am Meng So, the lord of the Nanman Beast Palace.”

“Ah, I am...”

At that moment, Chung Myung chuckled and stopped Meng So.

“No need to be so formal. On the contrary, he’s not even the head yet. He’s still a rookie, so just watch over and help him appropriately.”

“...What do you mean? If he’s the young lord of the Namgung Family...”

Just as Meng So was about to retort with a bewildered face, Namgung Do-Wei hurriedly spoke.

“No, Palace lord.”

“Huh?”

Namgung Do-Wei politely bowed to the slightly stunned Meng So.

“Taoist Chung Myung’s words are correct. I still have many shortcomings, so please give me much guidance and advice.”

Meng So blinked his large eyes.

Usually intimidating like a giant lion, Meng So looked more like a gentle ox blinking in confusion.

“Do you... know me well?”

“Not really.”

“Then why ask for guidance and advice?”

At that, Namgung Do-Wei smiled gently.

“I don’t know much about you, Palace lord, but I think I know Taoist Chung Myung to some extent now.”

“...”

“He always speaks half-jokingly, but there’s always a true meaning that shouldn’t be missed. I think Taoist Chung Myung is telling me to learn many things from you, Palace lord.”

Meng So stared at Namgung Do-Wei, then subtly turned his gaze to Chung Myung, who shrugged as if he didn’t know.

“So naturally, you should ask for guidance, shouldn’t you?”

“Listen... I am an outsider. I have nothing to offer the prestigious Namgung Family that symbolizes the Central Plains.”

“What does that matter?”

“...You asked what matters?”

“Yes.”

Namgung Do-Wei glanced aside at someone and frowned.

“We even share meals with those from the Evil Sect.”

“Hey, you really should take care of your eyes. Won’t it hurt if you get stabbed?”

Im So-Byeong joked, making Namgung Do-Wei’s face twist even more.

“...Did you see that?”

“...”

“Now we even eat with those from the Evil Sect. In such a situation, making distinctions about outsiders is ridiculous.”

Namgung Do-Wei chuckled, not expecting to say such things.

“At least within the Heavenly Friends Alliance, such distinctions mean nothing.”

“...But aren’t you the one who will lead the great Namgung Family?”

“I discarded such pretenses long ago.”

“You discarded them?”

Namgung Do-Wei nodded.

“Yes. The Namgung Family is nothing special or different. Please think of it as a request from a student and help me.”

Meng So laughed heartily.

Of course, he was proud of the Nanman Beast Palace. But his pride and the world’s recognition were different matters.

Between the prestigious Central Plains family and the outsider Nanman Beast Palace, there was an unbridgeable gap. But to this young man, that gap seemed invisible.

“Please.”

Namgung Do-Wei bowed deeply once more.

Meng So looked at the bowed Namgung Do-Wei, or rather those standing behind him, who didn’t seem to find it unusual.

‘Do they not feel this is strange?’

Just ten years ago, it was unthinkable for the Namgung Family’s direct line to bow to an outsider barbarian. Now, they just watch this scene indifferently.
