Though the parties involved might not notice, Meng So who joined from outside felt this huge change vividly.

“Geez, that kid’s going to strain his neck.”

“Uh...”

When Chung Myung subtly hinted, Meng So nodded helplessly.

“If I can help, I’ll do my best.”

“Thank you!”

Namgung Do-Wei smiled brightly, a smile without any pretense.

Seeing that face, Meng So couldn’t help but feel this was no mere formality.

Feeling awkward, Meng So turned to look at Chung Myung.

“...You.”

“Yes?”

“What exactly are you making?”

“What do you mean by that?”

“No...”

Meng So laughed as if incredulous.

It’s not hard for one or two people to show some kindness to outsiders. Even when the Central Plains and outsiders were at odds, some showed goodwill.

But for so many gathered not to show hostility to outsiders was rare, even unprecedented. Even those seeing the Nanman Beast Palace people for the first time today.

Strange clothing or bringing beasts around would normally cause frowns from them...

“By the way, why are those guys walking around shirtless?”

“They came from a hot place.”

“Ah, I didn’t think of that.”

“What’s wrong with taking off shirts? They even live with people who skin others alive.”

“...Shall I start by skinning you?”

“Then will your neck be safe?”

Seeing those who quickly lost interest in the Nanman Beast Palace and started bickering, Meng So shook his head. He swore this was the first time he felt so ignored.

‘...The Heavenly Friends Alliance.’

He thought it was like a dream. Meng So joined Chung Myung’s cause simply because it benefited the Nanman Beast Palace, not expecting to change anything big.

The reality outsiders felt and Central Plains natives felt were too different. The Central Plains had no reason to change that reality first.

But here and now, Meng So felt the change clearly. Perhaps... someday those within the Heavenly Friends Alliance will see each other without prejudice.

Meng So smiled bitterly, realizing hope was swelling inside him. Great expectations only bring great disappointment. He decided to be content with this.

But even if he gave up hope, his heart with the Heavenly Friends Alliance could never be the same. He wanted to see how the world would change when they lead it.

“...What exactly did you do?”

“What do you mean?”

“Everyone is so informal with each other now, regardless of status.”

“Ah, well, that’s natural.”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung answered indifferently.

“Whether Namgung or the Green Forest, they’re all kids who can’t be used anyway. There’s no better or worse among them. They should be treated equally.”

“...”

That’s... a bit strange though...

Understanding and confusion overwhelmed him simultaneously. Meng So thought maybe this was better than empty, unattainable ideals.

“Oh? They’re coming too.”

“Huh?”

At Chung Myung’s words, Meng So turned his head. Seeing where Chung Myung pointed, he burst out laughing.

“They arrived right on time. By the way, aren’t they hot? Wearing those thick fur clothes all the way here.”

“The Nanman Beast Palace people here walk around shirtless too, you know?”

“...No comment.”

The two laughed side by side. Meanwhile, people dressed in snow-white clothes were approaching quickly.

They were the arrival of the Northern Sea Ice Palace, who had traveled farther than Yunnan across the world to finally arrive.

Those clad in pure white fur approached with steps as if measured by a ruler.

If the Beast Palace disciples were as hot as a dense jungle grown under the blazing sun, the Northern Sea Ice Palace disciples were as fierce as if they had brought the biting north wind and snow.

Though a person’s temperament might not change simply by where they grow up, at least those people seemed to carry the cold wind of the Northern Sea so naturally it didn’t feel strange.

‘To be this different.’

Namgung Do-Wei felt a strangely fresh sensation.

Namgung was also a place where swords were used, but the temperament exuded by the swordsmen of the Northern Sea Ice Palace was somehow exceptional. He naturally thought, “So this is what they mean by a blade-like spirit.”

Their spirits were completely opposite to those of the Beast Palace, which was endlessly free and unbound, whereas the Ice Palace disciples seemed as if the word “discipline” had been molded into them.

Perhaps because of that, unlike the Beast Palace, they felt difficult to approach easily...

“Taoiiiiist!”

...But then.

Among those exuding blade-like spirits, suddenly a man dashed out and fiercely charged toward Chung Myung.

“Huh?”

“Taoist!”

The man showed no sign of hiding his joy and grabbed Chung Myung’s hand firmly with both hands, almost snatching it.

“How long has it been! Have you been well?”

“...Huh?”

“Taoist, you really haven’t changed! I’m relieved! Though I thought it might be awkward since it’s been so long since we met!”

“...Uh?”

“Still, seeing you again like this makes the long journey worthwhile! Goodness, why is the Central Plains so hot! It feels hotter here than the Northern Sea’s midsummer!”

“Ah, no, wait a moment.”

“Yes?”

“I...”

Chung Myung tilted his head with a completely puzzled expression.

“Who are you?”

“...”

The man—or rather, the young man—looked momentarily stunned. But soon after, he burst into a big laugh.

“Ahahaha! As expected, Taoist. You often joked like this.”

“No, that’s not what I meant...”

“I also need to quickly learn this kind of composure from you, Taoist. Why is it so difficult to do that?”

“No...”

“But if I keep trying every day, someday...”

“Hey! Let me speak too! Who are you, brat!”

At those words, the young man froze. Then he looked at Chung Myung with a truly shocked expression.

“T-taoist, do you really not remember me?”

“No, I feel like I’m meeting you for the first time in my life, so why do you keep acting like you know me?”

“I’m So-Baek!”

“Huh?”

“Seol So-Baek.”

“Who? Seol So-Baek? The Ice Palace Lord?”

“Yes! That’s me!”

Now Chung Myung looked blankly at the young man before him. Handsome, with pale skin and a height well over five feet...

“The So-Baek I know was this small.”

“Yes! That’s me.”

“...You?”

It wasn’t only Chung Myung who reacted to that.

“Eek!”

“Seol Young Palace Lord?”

“Really?”

The Five Swords behind Chung Myung reacted strongly as if they couldn’t believe it.

Chung Myung looked the young man up and down several times. He couldn’t quite believe this handsome youth was Baek Cheon...

“Now that I think about it, they do look alike...”

“I told you.”

“Look closely, the eyes and mouth are exactly the same.”

“Where do you even see that?”

“I didn’t expect Jo Gul sahyung’s sharp eyes to notice it, so even if you don’t see it, just believe it.”

“...No...”

Baek Cheon blinked. Now that he heard it, it really seemed he was Seol So-Baek...

“Heh... hahaha.”

Chung Myung couldn’t hide his astonishment as he looked at the grown young man before him.

“Really you are So-Baek?”

“Yes, Taoist.”

“Your voice is completely different too.”

“I think it’s the same, is that so strange?”

“It’s not that... well... yeah. You’ve grown a lot.”

No. At this level, it’s less about growing a lot and more like being a completely different person.

His height seemed to have nearly doubled, and his once chubby, baby-faced features had transformed into a slender, handsome young man’s face. Rather than the typical stern Northern Sea look, he had a somewhat delicate, refined beauty.

“Come to think of it, the last time I saw you was... let’s see... almost four years ago?”

“That’s right. It’s already been four years.”

“...Then it’s natural you’ve changed.”

Because you were at the age of growth... Of course, the gap between what I thought and what I saw was the problem.

Chung Myung’s gaze shifted to the sword hanging at the young man’s waist—no, Seol So-Baek’s waist. Wearing a pure white martial robe and carrying a sword, he now clearly looked like a true warrior.

“It’s been a while, Palace Lord Seol.”

“A great honor, Beast Palace Lord!”

Surprised, Chung Myung quickly turned his head and asked Meng So.

“Do you recognize him?”

“He looks exactly the same, so why that reaction?”

“Y-you say he looks the same?”

“He’s just grown a bit taller, isn’t he?”

Indeed, Meng So was the kind of person who could distinguish ten tiger cubs that looked exactly alike. If he could recognize animals, there was no reason he couldn’t recognize a person.

Seeing him say this, it seemed certain that this was indeed Seol So-Baek.

Chung Myung forced away the subtle awkwardness and greeted with a smiling face.

“Anyway, thank you for coming. It must have been a long journey.”

“Not at all. Of course, I had to come.”

Seol So-Baek smiled brightly and spoke a little shyly.

“Actually, I was a bit worried during that time.”

“Huh? Worried?”

“There were some opinions within the Ice Palace. They said things about the Heavenly Friends Alliance and so on, but in the end, it was just a scheme to pretend to be close and cheaply acquire Northern Sea goods like ice crystals to make money.”

Thud.

Seol So-Baek’s words pierced Chung Myung’s heart mercilessly like a dagger.

“...Is that so?”

“Hahaha. Sorry, Taoist. Since I know you personally, I never thought that way, but those who don’t know you well seemed quite distrustful. Some thoughtless people even insulted you as a fraud pretending to be a Taoist. Hahaha.”

Thud!

“Ah, don’t worry. I sharply reprimanded those people. But people are truly tricky; even though they thought it impossible, since you rarely visit, they wondered if you might have completely forgotten the Northern Sea. Hahahaha!”

Thud!

“There’s no way that could be true. How could I not know how dearly you cherish the Northern Sea? What interest would Taoists living deep in the mountains have in money to come all the way to the Northern Sea? Haha. It’s really nonsense.”

“...”

“Still, I worried that by some chance you had forgotten us, but since you asked to see us, I feel ashamed. You think of us so kindly, yet I had such shameless thoughts. I feel so guilty.”

“Alright, I was wrong.”

“What? Taoist, what did you just say...?”

“Ah, no. It’s nothing.”

The back of Chung Myung’s head was soaked with sweat.

Of course, he couldn’t say he had completely forgotten, but it was undeniable that he had pushed them aside somewhat.

If he had resented that fact, Chung Myung might have shamelessly brushed it off, but since such unwavering faith was shown, he could only feel smaller and smaller.

“Ah, and!”

“Yes?”

“Here! Bring it here!”

When Seol So-Baek shouted, the Ice Palace disciples responded loudly and pulled something from behind. Something very familiar to Mount Hua disciples.

“A cart?”

“It’s huge.”

“Hahaha. If that was made of iron, it would be just like what Mount Hua uses... Is that iron?”

The Ice Palace disciples pulled the huge carts without any sign of difficulty. The problem was that there was not just one cart.

Rumble! Rumble rumble!

At least five carts moved, kicking up clouds of dust.

“W-What is this?”

“I couldn’t come to see you by being empty-handed after so long. These are gifts brought from the Northern Sea.”

“G-Gifts? We also receive Northern Sea goods through the Eunha Merchant...”

“Those are items for sale. These are precious gifts incomparable to those. They are for you, taoist.”

“...Huh?”

“Now, this is...”

Seol So-Baek removed the cloth covering the first cart.

“Eek! W-What is this?”

“These are precious animal pelts brought from the Northern Sea. Seal pelts, gray wolf pelts, and even rare bear pelts from the Northern Sea.”

“...”

“These are priceless items. I stopped by earlier and thought Mount Hua’s winter would be very cold, so I prepared these for the Taoists.”

“...Was Mount Hua cold for you too?”

“I didn’t particularly feel that way, but the Taoists might be cold, right? These pelts will help you stay warm through winter.”

Chung Myung suddenly burst into tears.

“I’m the one to blame, I am.”

Had he ever been treated like this in his life? A man living in the freezing Northern Sea worries about the cold on the mountain and brings pelts... and yet he had abandoned such people...

Ah, I know. I’m feeling extreme guilt right now!

“And in this cart are rare ingredients even in the Northern Sea, gathered over a season. And this is...”

“...You brought all this? It’s overwhelming for me...”

“Oh, burden? Considering what the Taoist has done for the Northern Sea, this is nothing.”

“...”

“Ah, of course, this isn’t brought as repayment. Please think of it as a small token of my sincerity.”

Seol So-Baek licked his lips regretfully.

“If the distance weren’t so far, I would have brought more pears.”

“S-Stop...”

Though Chung Myung would drink even poison if free, if he ate all this, his stomach would burst. Lately, he was repeatedly realizing that he actually had a conscience.

At that moment, Seol So-Baek’s expression darkened slightly.

“However...”

“Yes?”

“We couldn’t procure much of the ice crystals you mentioned. The yield has decreased drastically... I’m sorry.”

“No need to apologize!”

Chung Myung quickly shook his head passionately and waved his hands.

I already scraped together decades’ worth before; asking for more now would make me a robber.

“We managed to gather some quantity, but the quality is poor. In the past, it would have been embarrassing to even call it as ice crystals...”

“Ah, it’s okay. Really, it’s okay.”

I’m not the one eating it anyway.

Just then, as Chung Myung deeply realized that a well-raised Northern Sea Ice Palace Lord like Seol So-Baek was worth more than ten dragon warriors, a sharp voice was heard from somewhere.

“What? So you caught animals and skinned them for no reason? These are utterly despicable people!”

“Huh?”

The members of the Nanman Beast Palace glared fiercely at Seol So-Baek and the animal pelts laid out before him.

“Is there a problem?”

At the same time, Seol So-Baek’s face turned as cold as ice. As if to prove he was the legitimate heir of the Northern Sea Ice Palace, a chilling pressure began to emanate from his entire body.

“I asked if there was any problem.”

The Beast Palace members flinched at Seol So-Baek’s cold voice.

They were not foolish enough to misunderstand the situation. They could not be unaware that this young man was the North Sea Ice Palace Lord, belonging to the Five Palaces Beyond the Great Wall along with the Beast Palace.

However... the Beast Palace members’ faces remained dark as they glanced at the cart piled high with animal skins. Even if the opponent was the North Sea Ice Palace Lord, shouldn’t they speak their minds?

“Do you really have to...?”

The voice, which had sounded slightly suppressed, gradually regained its original tone.

“Is there a need to kill innocent beasts like that?”

“I don’t understand what you mean.”

Seol So-Baek cut off the conversation as if it wasn’t worth having.

“It’s meaningless to argue whether beasts are guilty or not, and even if it mattered, why should I accept your judgment on my actions?”

“Oh...”

Chung Myung looked at Seol So-Baek with amazement, and the Five Swords, who remembered Seol So-Baek, all opened their mouths in surprise.

‘Is that really the So-Baek I know?’

‘Sasuk. I don’t think that’s right.’

‘Is that the So-Baek we know?’

‘Wow, he was really cute when he was little.’

Chung Myung looked at Seol So-Baek with fresh eyes.

Though he had grown so much he was unrecognizable, in Chung Myung’s mind, Seol So-Baek was still a child. The legitimate heir of the North Sea Ice Palace, yet never properly treated as such.

But the image Seol So-Baek showed now was clearly far from the innocent child living in a hut that he remembered.

Seol So-Baek, glaring coldly at the Beast Palace people, was filled with the dignity befitting the North Sea Ice Palace Lord who leads the Northern Sea.

‘Fascinating.’

Four years is enough time for a small child to become a young man. But it seems too short a time for an innocent child to show proper dignity.

Is it the position that shapes the person? Or is the blood of the North Sea’s Seol family deeper than Chung Myung thought?

“So you think killing innocent beasts is the right thing to do?”

But the Beast Palace members did not back down easily despite Seol So-Baek’s strong stance.

Everyone has a point they cannot yield on. The Beast Palace members, who treat beasts like friends, could not tolerate the Ice Palace’s act of bringing back piles of animal skins as gifts.

“Aren’t you wearing animal skins yourselves?”

“These were not obtained by killing. They were taken from beasts that died naturally. We do not take the lives of healthy beasts just for their skins.”

“Ha ha.”

At that moment, Seol So-Baek laughed as if it was ridiculous.

“Those who learn martial arts that kill people tell us not to kill beasts recklessly? Absurd.”

“People commit sins, but beasts do not.”

At that moment, Seol So-Baek’s eyes grew bleak.

“It seems the place you live is quite prosperous.”

“...What?”

“I see you spout such well-fed words. But what can I do? The North Sea, covered in snow and ice, is a place where you must do whatever it takes to survive. There’s no luxury to consider the plight of mere beasts.”

Seol So-Baek’s voice was bitterly cold.

In fact, the Ice Palace enjoys a fairly wealthy life in the North Sea, but Seol So-Baek is not an ordinary Ice Palace person. Moreover, until he matured, he lived apart from the Ice Palace and experienced firsthand how the people of the North Sea lived.

So he cannot help but know how hard it is to live rooted in that frozen land where not even a single grain can be properly grown.

For such a Seol So-Baek, how hollow must the Beast Palace’s words about not killing innocent beasts sound?

“Are you saying we are wrong now?”

“Well, I don’t think so. But...”

Seol So-Baek slightly turned his head. Through the gap in the warehouse door, large beasts could be seen.

“Did you bring those?”

“Yes, so what?”

“Interesting.”

Seol So-Baek sneered.

“It must take considerable effort to raise beasts that large. As far as I know, Yunnan wasn’t in a good state until recently either.”

“...What do you mean?”

“If you have food to feed such beasts, you should share it with the starving. Even in hardship, you take care of beasts regularly. It seems beasts are more important to you than people.”

“You’ve said enough...”

“And what if I have?”

The atmosphere turned cold. Seol So-Baek continued in a chilling voice.

“I’ve heard the Beast Palace is free-spirited, but this seems more like licentiousness. Even if there are problems here, is it proper for mere members of Palace like you to question me, the Ice Palace Lord, directly?”

“Th-that’s...”

“This is what ‘reckless and arrogant’ means. Thanks to you, I now know what kind of place the Beast Palace is.”

“Y-your words are excessive!”

“Excessive?”

Seol So-Baek bared his teeth. At the same time, a cold chill emanated from him.

“It’s okay for you to disrespect the North Sea Ice Palace, but it’s excessive for me to speak carelessly about the Beast Palace? I didn’t realize the Beast Palace was such a great place. I’m curious—how much more can you disrespect the Ice Palace?”

“...”

“At this point, I think I need to prove that the Ice Palace is not a place to be so disrespected.”

A chilling murderous aura poured out. Seol So-Baek, with eyes as cold as the ice walls of the North Sea, dropped his hands and stepped forward just then.

“Hey, why fight as soon as we see each other? Let’s get along.”

“...Fight?”

At the moment Chung Myung spoke from behind, the chill Seol So-Baek was emitting vanished as if washed away. Instantly changing the atmosphere, Seol So-Baek turned sharply to Chung Myung and gave an embarrassed smile.

“I-I was just having a brief conversation. With the Taoist here, there’s no way I’d do that!”

“...”

“Oh, come on. No way. Hehe.”

The Ice Palace members, moved by the rapidly grown young lord, lost their strength. Their shoulders slumped, heads bowed weakly, their eyes moist with tears.

‘The Mount Hua illness has flared up again.’

‘Why is it that whenever something related to Mount Hua happens...’

‘Why on earth does he act like that!’

Who could understand the feelings of the Ice Palace members?

Even to them, Seol So-Baek had grown remarkably well. At first, some were displeased that a child who had not properly experienced the Ice Palace world would ascend to lordship simply because he inherited the blood of the previous lord.

But now, no one in the North Sea doubts that Seol So-Baek will be a great lord. His past conduct has been too exemplary to harbor doubts.

Unlike previous lords who grew up comfortably in the North Sea Ice Palace and failed to understand the lives of the North Sea people, Seol So-Baek understands their hardships better than anyone. Thus, he is becoming a caretaker of the North Sea people more than any previous lord.

He is growing from someone to be protected into someone who protects them.

He may not be perfect now, but Seol So-Baek, the current Ice Palace Lord, is regarded as someone who might one day become the most perfect person.

Just one thing.

‘If only it weren’t for Mount Hua.’

It was incomprehensible why such a cold, rational, yet warm-hearted person would lose his mind whenever the two words Mount Hua were involved.

Even those animal skins gift were like that.

L-Lord. The Mount Hua people live in the warm Central Plains; do they really need these kinds of skins?

Hmm. So?

Ah, no. It’s just that they’re hard to catch, and everyone is exhausted... They’re quite rare...

So?

Well... it’s not that I don’t want to, but is this really necessary...?

So?

...

So?

...I will do it.

That is not something to be dismissed as just ‘rare.’

How many countless nights did the North Sea Ice Palace members stay up to obtain those skins! Those beasts are mostly nocturnal and cannot be seen during the day!

Can you imagine how insane it is to hunt beasts on stormy, snowy nights in the already cold North Sea?

A person who usually cares if the Ice Palace members have comfortable beds and enough food becomes a madman who drives them to hunt on blizzard-filled freezing nights just because of the two words Mount Hua.

“And also... I think your words are a bit too harsh.”

“Ah, right?”

Seol So-Baek smiled pleasantly at Chung Myung’s subtle admonition. Chung Myung tilted his head at this incomprehensible reaction.

“Why are you laughing?”

“Because I think I did well.”

“Huh?”

“I tried. Usually, one must be warm, but to enemies or opponents, a warrior must not show even an ounce of warmth, right?”

“...Huh?”

Uh... that’s true, but... the Beast Palace isn’t an enemy, is it?

But Seol So-Baek, whether aware of Chung Myung’s feelings or not, smiled even brighter.

“It wasn’t easy, but with steady effort, the day came when I heard such words from the Taoist. Indeed, the Taoist’s saying that effort never betrays is true.”

“W-what effort?”

“Of course, you are the Taoist after all.”

“...Huh? What do you mean...?”

“I tried to imitate the Taoist’s demeanor. When the Taoist fought, he was exactly like this. Fighting those Demonic Cult bastards like this! Like this!”

“...”

“I’m still lacking, but if I try harder, someday I can be like the Taoist, right?”

Chung Myung looked at Seol So-Baek smiling brightly with a dazed expression.

Shifting his gaze slightly, he saw the Ice Palace members bowing their heads as if wanting to bury themselves in the ground, unable to bear watching this. Their flushed faces contrasted sharply with their pure white uniforms.

“Me?”

“Yes!”

“...I acted like this?”

Chung Myung looked back at the Five Swords standing behind him. But the Five Swords averted their gaze awkwardly, avoiding explanation.

“Well, not exactly the same.”

“A little... uh, a little similar.”

“What he tried to imitate... yeah, I get what he was trying to imitate.”

“Of all the people...”

Chung Myung stared blankly at the Five Swords, then turned his gaze back to Seol So-Baek. Seeing him blinking his big eyes and smiling, he was reminded of a large dog wagging its tail fiercely.

A hollow voice escaped from Chung Myung’s lips.

“...Really?”

...No, you fool. I’ve never been like that.

Wow...

Wow... I’m going crazy, really...

Unlike the warm and friendly atmosphere of Mount Hua and Ice Palace, the mood among the Beast Palace members was not very good.

In the end, they had to back down because they could not ignore the authority of the Northern Sea Ice Palace lord, but coldly speaking, being pushed aside by someone barely a generation younger than them meant they could hardly feel good about it.

Moreover, the young man’s attitude toward them was so different from the one he showed to Mount Hua that it also upset them.

Depending on how you look at it, all these actions could easily be seen as disrespect toward the Nanman Beast Palace.

Just because they were grouped under the name of the Five Palaces Beyond the Great Wall, how much bond could there really be between Ice Palace and Beast Palace? The only thing that united them was the discriminatory treatment they received from the Central Plains people.

However, when the Heavenly Friends Alliance resolved those issues to some extent, the previously hidden cultural differences between them began to surface.

“Isn’t that a bit harsh, no matter what?”

“Didn’t they just call us enemies a moment ago?”

“Can those who kill innocent beasts just for unnecessary skins be considered virtuous? They’re people we can’t associate with.”

“The worst.”

The Beast Palace members quietly exchanged opinions among themselves. Their hostility toward Ice Palace only grew stronger.

“He’s still young but sounds too arrogant.”

“He may be the Ice Palace lord, but if he were in the Beast Palace, he wouldn’t even be a young palace lord at his age.”

“He seems lacking in ability too.”

“I don’t like him.”

What they found especially disgraceful was that no matter how they looked, the Ice Palace lord did not seem very strong.

Of course, they did not deny that his spirit was impressive for his age, but anyone could see that those standing behind the Ice Palace lord looked stronger.

In nature, the strong ones become the lord; it was natural. For someone without experience or strength to occupy a high position just by status was incomprehensible to them.

“He doesn’t know how high the sky is just because he wears a hat of authority.”

At that moment, a sharp voice reached their ears.

“What did you just say?”

“Hmm?”

Unlike the noisy Mount Hua, the Ice Palace members quietly observing from behind seemed to have heard their conversation. Some at the front of the Ice Palace approached the Beast Palace members with cold glares.

“I asked what you just said.”

“...But do these guys think we’re easy?”

“Barbarians who don’t even know how to wear clothes properly are talking big with their loose mouths. What? Lacking ability?”

And naturally, the Beast Palace’s gaze was also unacceptable to the Ice Palace.

If the Beast Palace follows the law of the jungle, the Ice Palace fights against nature itself. They believe that not the strong but the wise survive.

And Seol So-Baek is the Palace Lord even recognized by the elders of Ice Palace. Denying the Ice Palace Lord Seol So-Baek was tantamount to denying the entire way of life of the Ice Palace members.

“...Did you just call us barbarians?”

Hostility surged between the two groups.

Until the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s faction split, the two sects were not on bad terms... in fact, they were on good terms, but now they were baring their teeth at each other.

Just as the tension between the two groups reached its peak.

“Enough.”

The Beast Palace Lord, Meng So, who had been quietly observing, spoke loudly to settle the situation.

As soon as the Beast Palace lord stepped forward, the Beast Palace members bowed their heads and stepped back.

The Ice Palace members also looked displeased but had no choice but to step back. They knew that ignoring Meng So’s words now would be tantamount to excusing the Beast Palace members’ earlier pressure on Seol So-Baek.

“Hmm.”

Meng So frowned as he looked at his subordinates.

“This time, you were wrong. Apologize to the Ice Palace lord.”

“P-palace lord! We...”

“Enough.”

Meng So waved his hand dismissively.

“What do the Central Plains people say about us?”

“...”

“Don’t they call us rude, barbaric people who don’t know how to live? What do you say every time?”

“...”

“Answer me.”

One Beast Palace member bowed his head and replied.

“They said that if they came to live at the edge of Yunnan or in the Southern Barbarian lands, they would have to live like us.”

“That’s right. A person’s life inevitably differs depending on where they live. But... you, who were so offended by that view, are doing the same to Ice Palace people. You criticize them without trying to understand, just by your own standards.”

“...”

“If you had been a bit more rude, I would have broken your backs myself.”

“S-sorry.”

“I’m not the one you should apologize to.”

At those words, the Beast Palace members bowed their heads toward Seol So-Baek.

“We apologize, Ice Palace lord.”

“We were rude.”

Seol So-Baek glanced at the Beast Palace members. In the past, he would have accepted the apology immediately, but this time he did not say a word. It was not that he was upset personally, but that the Northern Sea was being disrespected.

“I apologize too, Ice Palace lord. Please understand.”

“Ah, no, Palace lord. Of course, I understand.”

But even that feeling melted away as Meng So slightly bowed his head himself. Seol So-Baek knew well how difficult it was for a Beast Palace lord to bow to others.

Seol So-Baek scratched his nose awkwardly.

“At first... well, I was acting a bit sensitive too.”

“I understand. You must have heard similar things as we did.”

A sigh escaped Seol So-Baek’s lips. Being looked down upon for living differently from the Central Plains was not so different for them either. That was why they were sensitive to such remarks.

“If you think about it, the Central Plains people are the problem.”

“That’s true.”

“There’s no reason for us to fight among ourselves.”

“Yes. Palace lord, you are right.”

Hearing that, Chung Myung frowned and rubbed his ear.

“I don’t think this is something to say in front of a Central Plains guy.”

“Hahaha. You’re different. You don’t discriminate between Central Plains and Outer regions.”

“Yes. That’s right. I completely agree.”

“...Is that an insult?”

“Ahahaha. How could it be? Anyone who values their life wouldn’t dare insult Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Exactly! Who dares insult the Taoist! I’ll personally throw them to the bottom of the Northern Sea lake.”

Chung Mun Sahyung.

I don’t like these guys... It’s uncomfortable for me...

Chung Myung sighed deeply. What could he do? Embracing these kinds of people was the path he had chosen himself.

“So, what shall we do now?”

“Huh?”

“Please give the order! We are ready.”

Chung Myung, feeling burdened by the approaching Seol So-Baek, stepped back sweating nervously.

“Well... your enthusiasm is good, but since you’ve traveled far, wouldn’t it be better to rest first? We should meet the Sect leader first, right?”

“Yes! We should!”

Seol So-Baek nodded vigorously, almost overwhelming. Then he quickly began leading the Ice Palace members into the manor.

“Be careful not to damage the gift cart!”

“Yes!”

Chung Myung wiped the sweat from his forehead. Meng So, standing beside him, spoke quietly.

“...It seems they’ve grown in a strange direction.”

“Y-yeah?”

“I don’t think it’s bad... but it’s a bit odd.”

Meng So gave a wry smile. Chung Myung sighed deeply.

Though the situation was somehow settled, the Ice Palace and Beast Palace members still harbored resentment and cast unfriendly glances at each other even as they retreated.

Watching this, Chung Myung clicked his tongue softly and then spoke seriously.

“Thank you.”

“What are you thanking for?”

“Even the Palace lord probably wasn’t in a good mood.”

Meng So chuckled.

“What’s there to be upset about?”

Of course, though he said that, he couldn’t have been happy. The Beast Palace members’ mindset was not much different from Meng So’s. If the Palace members were displeased, Meng So had to be displeased too.

In fact, he might have been even more upset than the average sect member, since Meng So was the Beast Palace lord.

But he held back those feelings and scolded his own sect members first.

Though they were both leaders of the Five Palaces Beyond the Great Wall, Seol So-Baek was incomparable to him. If he had tried to suppress him with power and authority, it wouldn’t have been difficult, but Meng So apologized first and bowed his head. That was why Chung Myung also rarely expressed gratitude.

“It’s nothing compared to what you do.”

“...Yes?”

“Even someone as hot-tempered as you is restraining himself.”

“No... my personality isn’t as bad as you think...”

“If you were your old self, the moment the Palace members sharpened their blades at each other, you’d have beaten them both without a second thought.”

Huh? Now that I think about it...

“But isn’t it ridiculous that I’m the one stepping in and getting angry?”

“...”

“More than that... it won’t be as easy as I thought.”

“What do you mean?”

Meng So stared at Chung Myung as if to say, “You know the answer, why ask again?”

“Every time trouble breaks out, neither I nor Palace Lord Seol can always run to mediate. If that were possible, I wouldn’t have asked for your help when you came to Yunnan in the past.”

“...True.”

“The palace members acknowledge and worship the strong, but they don’t understand that they must endure stuff. Especially, they find it hard to endure situations they can’t accept.”

“They’re just too free-spirited.”

Meng So wore a bitter expression.

He hadn’t expected such problems to arise immediately upon meeting. He felt sorry for only bringing headaches despite coming to help.

“There might be some problems, is that okay?”

“Ah, what problems?”

“Huh?”

Meng So asked with a puzzled look as Chung Myung’s response was indifferent.

“What do you mean?”

“Well, you’ll find out soon enough.”

Instead of answering, Chung Myung smiled slyly.

“By the way, you took care of the Beast Palace neatly, right?”

“As you said, we’ve taken measures so we won’t return for years. That’s why it took so long.”

“That’s good.”

Chung Myung nodded.

With this, the full power of the Heavenly Friends Alliance is gathered here.

“Looking at it all together, it’s really no joke.”

Mount Hua, Tang family, Namgung and Green Forest, and then Ice Palace and Beast Palace.

Six of the world’s dominant sects are gathered here. Considering that the Nine Great Sects were regarded as rulers of the martial world with only ten sects, this is no small force.

“Still, it’s not enough...”

Crack. Crack.

Chung Myung cracked his neck left and right.

“Well, that’s something we can fill from now on.”

It’s not that difficult. Just like Mount Hua grew big, they can raise theirs too. Of course, since there’s no time to raise them leisurely, it will be a bit rough and painful for those on the receiving end...

“They’re doing it for their own good, so we have to understand. Even if we don’t, what can they do?”

“Huh?”

“No, it’s nothing.”

Chung Myung chuckled as if just thinking about it made him happy.

Meng So felt uneasy seeing him look like a child who just got an amusing toy.

I can’t breathe.

No, I’m afraid to even breathe.

In a vast plaza, where the word “vast” fits better than just “wide,” countless people are lined up.

The strange thing was that the faces of those who clearly possessed extraordinary energy were all pale white. In their noticeably trembling hands, threatening long spears were held.

Of course, everyone experiences moments of trembling in fear in life. Yet, the reason this scene couldn’t be taken for granted was because those trembling like children were the Evil Sect’s bastards, whose very name instills terror throughout Southern Lands.

“A-alliance Lord! P-please! Please spare me...”

Swoosh!

The sword that soared into the sky fiercely fell and cut off a person’s neck. The severed head floated lightly in the air.

Thud.

From the body that collapsed like a bundle of straw, red blood gushed out in waves.

The faces of those who saw the scene turned even paler.

They were all affiliated with the Evil Sect. Naturally, they had seen countless deaths. Seeing someone die now would hardly evoke any particular emotion.

But the scene unfolding before their eyes was clearly different in nature from any “death” they had witnessed before.

“Next.”

With Ho Ga-Myung’s cold voice, a corpse still warm was dragged out like rotten wood. Then another was dragged in like cattle to the slaughter.

The eyes of the one completely pale with fear were bloodshot. A desperate voice burst out like a seizure.

“Alliance, Alliance Lord! It’s not me! I’m not a fool resisting you! Please believe me, Alliance Lord! Please! Pleeease!”

That wail was almost unbearable to listen to.

The one being dragged now was probably also a villain who had taken many lives with his own hands. Yet even he was no different from ordinary people when facing his own death.

“Alliance Lord!”

Dragged into the vast plaza, he lifted his trembling head and looked toward the main hall.

“Huh... Huh...”

At that moment, a deflating sound escaped his mouth. His terrified appearance did not match his accumulated fame and rank at all.

But no one here could scorn that pitiful figure. Anyone witnessing this scene would react the same way.

The main headquarters, newly built after the Evil Sect was formed, had a vast central area paved with luxurious white marble, reflecting Jang Ilso’s taste.

When sunlight shone, the plaza sparkled like freshly fallen snow, symbolizing the Evil Sect itself.

But the dazzling marble was now stained dark red. The blood of those who died here flowed and flowed.

Who could maintain composure before such a sight?

“Ugh...”

The intense smell of blood assaulted the nose. The man’s pants quickly became soaked.

“Alliance Lord! Please spare me! I’ll do anything! Anything! It’s all a conspiracy! I’ve never once opposed you! Please! Please spare me! Alliance Lordddd!”

He let out a scream that was neither a shout nor a scream, looking desperately upward.

His gaze passed over the blood-stained ground toward the towering stairs connected to the plaza.

While the lower part was soaked in blood, the upper stairs gradually revealed white light, culminating in a summit shining with sacred whiteness.

That stark contrast seemed to signify the difference between the one dragged here and the one at the top of the stairs.

A magnificent, huge jade throne sat atop the stairs, and a man sat there slantwise.

“Alli-Alliance Lord...”

A red robe embroidered with golden dragons. Beneath it, a pale white hand lightly grasped a nearby wine cup.

“Hmm.”

The man who lifted the cup, Jang Ilso, looked down at the screaming man with a languid gaze.

“Alliance Lord!”

The man shouted like seeing the last lifeline below a cliff. But Jang Ilso soon lost interest, leaning back fully against the throne’s backrest.

Instead, it was Ho Ga-Myung who spoke.

“Execute him.”

The chilling voice echoed eerily.

Those who flinched at the voice immediately moved, dragging the screaming man to the execution block and pressing him down.

“Hii... Hiiiiiik!”

Swoosh!

The sharp sword swiftly cut the man’s neck in one stroke. The flying head sprayed blood everywhere as it rolled emptily on the ground.

Those watching without moving a finger barely swallowed their dry saliva.

The man just beheaded was Jo Pyo, one of the great masters of the Black Ghost Fort, notorious like a demon in Fujian.

But he was cut down like a worm without even properly resisting.

Who could have imagined such a scene just a month ago?

Those here do not fear death. From the moment they decided to join the Evil Sect, they accepted the fate of becoming corpses swarmed by flies on some battlefield.

But none imagined their last moments would be like this: dragged like criminals, unable to resist, and beheaded in front of many.

Not all deaths are the same.

This worthless feast of death was enough to make even those prepared to become fertilizer on the battlefield tremble in fear.

“So boring.”

And in this eerie atmosphere, there was only one who was unaffected by fear.

Jang Ilso took a small sip of the fine liquor in his cup and spoke.

“How many more are left?”

“There are 362 scheduled for today. Jo Pyo was the 178th.”

“Half remain still remain.”

Jang Ilso sighed and leaned back against the throne.

“If you’re bored, I’ll finish the remaining executions.”

“No, no.”

At Ho Ga-Myung’s words, Jang Ilso slightly shook his head.

“We once shared the same table; isn’t it courteous to watch their final moments? I am a person with that much consideration.”

“...”

“And...”

Jang Ilso slowly lowered his head and looked at those filling the plaza. Seeing their terrified faces, a satisfied smile spread across his lips.

“In such events, the superior should be present. It makes it more meaningful, doesn’t it?”

“Indeed.”

Ho Ga-Myung glanced at Jang Ilso’s expression and asked.

“Then, speeding up the executions is...”

“Tsk tsk tsk. Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance lord.”

“They lived like vermin and are hard-to-spare criminals, but aren’t they still human?”

“...”

“Killing them quickly out of annoyance is not right. Proceed as scheduled.”

“Yes, Alliance Lord.”

Ho Ga-Myung reluctantly nodded.

Well. I don’t know.

The fate of those scheduled for execution today will not change. They also know they will die today.

Continuing the executions leisurely seems to give them a bit more time to breathe. But on the other hand, it only prolongs their time trembling in fear, waiting for death in despair.

‘Pitiful.’

Ho Ga-Myung was not one to feel pity easily, but he could not help but sympathize with those trembling in fear, waiting for their turn.

But that was all. In truth, it was inevitable. From the moment they dared harbor rebellious thoughts against the Hegemony Lord, their fate was sealed.

“Continue the executions.”

“Yes!”

At Ho Ga-Myung’s shout, those in charge of execution began moving busily. Another criminal was dragged out.

Jang Ilso looked down indifferently at the scene and raised his cup.

“A fine sight.”

One life disappears, and another cup of wine is emptied.

One life, and then another.

The executions, linked by blood, continued until the sun hung low in the west, dyeing the world in sunset hues.

Today, the red sunset even stained the white marble where Jang Ilso sat with a blood-like hue.

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso, clad in a white robe, quietly gazed at his alcohol cup.

Looking into the perfectly still cup calmed his slightly excited heart.

Jang Ilso lifted his head and looked toward the helper standing beside the bed.

Suddenly, Jang Ilso’s eyes narrowed slightly.

“Oh my. Why are you trembling so?”

“Alliance Lord...”

The helper’s face, already pale white, had now turned almost blue.

“Not feeling well? Go and rest quickly.”

“Ah, no, Alliance Lord! How dare I...”

“Tsk tsk.”

Jang Ilso clicked his tongue pityingly. Unlike his gaze at the warriors below the stairs, this look was quite gentle.

“Then go and bring a stronger liquor here. The blood scent clinging to you is too strong to sense the fragrance.”

“I-I will.”

“No rush. Take your time.”

“Yes.”

The helper, pale as death, hurried outside, and Jang Ilso chuckled softly.

“I don’t understand why those kids fear me so much.”

In fact, it was understandable.

There are many tyrants who easily kill or torment lowly helpers. But Jang Ilso has never killed a helper who hasn’t mastered martial arts. He never coveted their bodies nor tormented them on whims. In fact, he even paid them generously when they quit.

Yet their gaze toward him remained soaked in fear, which was quite amusing to him.

“What would the lowly know? They probably just find it hard to look.”

“Tsk tsk. There you go again.”

Jang Ilso sighed lightly.

“They’re not lowly. Why do you keep calling the kids doing their jobs well lowly?”

“...”

“The word ‘lowly’ isn’t for kids like them. It’s for pigs who do things beyond their station and sit in places above their rank.”

“I will keep that in mind.”

“Don’t just say it; try to understand, Ga-Myung.”

Jang Ilso scolded him, but Ho Ga-Myung looked at him without changing expression and spoke.

“Alliance Lord.”

“Speak.”

“...Do you plan to continue executions tomorrow as well?”

“There are about two days left, aren’t there?”

“That’s the plan.”

“Then so be it. Ugh. Sitting all day is frustrating, but... this is what being Alliance Lord is like.”

“The losses are too great, Alliance Lord.”

Hearing that, Jang Ilso suddenly smiled deeply.

His face reflected in the flickering lantern was devilishly charming.

“Excessive?”

“Yes.”

Ho Ga-Myung spoke with a stern face.

“Even those executed today cannot be called mere small fry. They were all people who held important positions in the Black Ghost Fort and the Pirates.”

“Is that so?”

Jang Ilso appeared indifferent, as if he had little interest in such matters. Ho Ga-Myung had already expected this reaction. Yet, it was still his duty to speak his mind.

“If we drive out such people like this, ultimately, we cannot avoid weakening our strength.”

“Hmm.”

Jang Ilso glanced briefly toward the door and muttered something unexpected.

“I did tell them to come slowly, but it would be good to bring some alcohol and rest first.”

“...Alliance lord.”

A sigh escaped from Ho Ga-Myung’s lips.

“I do not misunderstand what Alliance Lord thinks. But, as you know, our situation is not so relaxed. The power of those to be executed is regrettable, but what is more regrettable are the administrative gaps that will arise from their absence. It will take quite a long time to replace them.”

Only then did Jang Ilso look directly at Ho Ga-Myung. His pale eyes remained indifferent. Ho Ga-Myung swallowed dryly and continued.

“I do not intend to oppose Alliance Lord’s will. I just wish to ask if it might be possible to reduce the number of those to be executed a little, for the sake of Alliance Lord and the Four Sects Alliance.”

“Hmm.”

“They are already terrified. If the Alliance lord shows mercy, they will never dare to oppose you again.”

“Ga-Myung, Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance Lord.”

“I always feel this way.”

“Yes.”

Jang Ilso looked at Ho Ga-Myung with a strange smile.

“You don’t seem suited for the Evil Sect.”

“...What?”

Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes widened at the absurd remark. If the mind-reading Ho Ga-Myung was told he didn’t fit the Evil Sect, how would others react?

Because it came from the great Jang Ilso, it was hard to refute; if anyone else said it, everyone would have scoffed immediately.

“How...”

“Isn’t that so?”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

“The Evil Sect doesn’t usually talk about showing mercy, nor say they won’t dare oppose again.”

“But that...”

“I know.”

Jang Ilso laughed softly like a sigh and said.

“They’ve seen what fear is once, so they think they won’t dare oppose again.”

“Yes, I...”

“But you see.”

Jang Ilso abruptly stopped speaking and his eyes glowed coldly.

“People are not as wise as you think. Most do not reflect on a lesson once learned; they soon forget it.”

“...”

“And then they think of it in a favorable way. Those who opposed Jang Ilso were eventually released and survived, so maybe they think they can try again once more?”

Ho Ga-Myung pressed his lips tightly.

“Yes. Humans are like that. Especially those of the Evil Sect.”

Jang Ilso waved his hand dismissively, as if the thought alone made him sick.

“Humans easily forget kindness but never forget grudges. But there is something they forget even less than grudges. Do you know what that is?”

“...”

“It is fear.”

Jang Ilso’s eyes darkened chillingly.

“Fear deeply engraved in one’s bones. The fear that resisting will only lead to a fate worse than death. The fear that I can never defeat him.”

“...”

“What moves people is exactly that intense fear.”

“Alliance Lord.”

Ho Ga-Myung quietly looked at Jang Ilso and spoke. He knew asking was pointless, but it was just his genuine curiosity about Jang Ilso.

“Do you not trust your subordinates?”

“You say interesting things.”

Jang Ilso chuckled.

“There are those worth trusting in this world. But do you mean to trust those vermin? I’d rather trust those Justice faction guys. At least they are not this fickle. They are as steady as oxen.”

A sigh escaped Ho Ga-Myung’s lips. That answer felt unsurprising, making him feel frustrated.

“But, Alliance lord, I did not advise showing them mercy. Even those vermin must be used if necessary, right?”

“Of course, that is true. If only decent people remain, you would be the only one left in this Evil Sect.”

Jang Ilso wore a strange smile.

“How about it? Shall we try again, just the two of us like before? Like those harsh times when we had nothing?”

At the mention of old times from Jang Ilso, a rare smile appeared on Ho Ga-Myung’s lips.

“To be honest, it doesn’t seem so bad, but we can’t go back, can we?”

“I see. It’s a pity. Ga-Myung, we have come too far.”

“But isn’t that what the Alliance lord wanted?”

“Of course.”

Jang Ilso does not fall into regret. He does not long for the past. His gaze is always toward tomorrow, not the past, and toward higher places, not here.

It is a strange thing.

Jang Ilso has obtained many things. What once felt like a dream, no, rather a delusion, he made real and forcibly crushed beneath his feet.

But Jang Ilso’s life is not much different from the past.

From a worthless wanderer to the leader of the Ten Thousand People Clan, and eventually the Alliance Lord of the Evil Sect, the only changes for Jang Ilso are a slightly more luxurious room and more expensive alcohol. These are things anyone could achieve without being the Alliance Lord of the Evil Sect.

Yet Jang Ilso devours everything greedily, like a voracious fish craving desire itself.

“So shouldn’t we find a way to utilize even such people? A rusty sword is better than none.”

Jang Ilso quietly looked at the white sake cup at eye level and spoke.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance lord.”

“Isn’t power like a sword?”

“...A sword?”

“Yes, that’s right. Do you know what is important when making a fine sword?”

“...Using good steel.”

“Yes, that’s correct.”

“But if all you have is scrap metal, isn’t it basic to use even that scrap?”

“Of course. But surprisingly, the most important thing in making a fine sword is not about the materials.”

“What is it?”

“Not being greedy.”

“...”

Ho Ga-Myung furrowed his brows slightly. It was not easy to understand what Jang Ilso was saying.

“Suppose you have one lump of steel to make a sword and a little extra steel. How would you make the sword?”

“Well...”

Ho Ga-Myung answered very logically.

“Make the blade with one lump of steel and use the remaining steel for the handle. Or mix it into the sheath...”

“Yes, that makes sense.”

Jang Ilso nodded as if agreeing, but what he said next was somewhat different from Ho Ga-Myung’s expectation.

“Then you ruin the sword.”

“...What?”

Jang Ilso laughed softly as if amused.

“If steel goes into the handle, the sword’s weight changes. It deviates from the ideal weight. If steel is mixed into the sheath, which is meant to protect the sword, it becomes unnecessarily hard. Then the blade gets damaged.”

“...”

“The best way is to make the sword with one lump of steel and discard the extra. Or you can sell it.”

Jang Ilso turned the liquor bottle upside down. Frowning at a drop falling, he sighed and continued.

“But most people cannot discard that extra steel. Those who use it for the sheath or handle are better off. But do you know what most do?”

“...I don’t know.”

“They put all the steel into the blade.”

“...”

“That makes it an expensive but worthless sword, worse than a cheap iron sword. Yet people wield it as if it were a great blade. They don’t realize that using such a sword diminishes their true skill. Or maybe they know but can’t give up their greed.”

A sigh escaped from Ho Ga-Myung’s lips.

“Are they the extra steel?”

“Yes, you understand it well.”

Jang Ilso slowly stood up.

“That’s greed. Knowing it’s useless and hard to sell, you should discard it immediately. But you don’t. Because you think you might use that steel someday.”

“...”

“Power is not something you can hold like extra steel and forget. Its mere existence controls me. Do you understand?”

“Alliance Lord.”

“I don’t need any extra that dulls me. Power must fit perfectly in my hand like a fine sword. Wielding a sword mixed with impurities and a dull blade is a clown’s act. Those that are bloated and move on their own must be beheaded or burned to ashes.”

A playful yet sly smile suddenly appeared on Jang Ilso’s lips.

“In the process, it serves as a good lesson to others, so you get the highest price. Isn’t that right?”

Ho Ga-Myung finally nodded.

The enemy is overwhelmingly strong. Therefore, Ho Ga-Myung tried to grow the Evil Sect more. But Jang Ilso seemed to think differently.

Then they must follow Jang Ilso’s will. He is the one who moves this power.

“Ga-Myung.”

“Yes, Alliance lord.”

“The wars we will face from now on will be different from before. We will have to face multiple enemies in many places, not just one.”

“...”

“If those fools move as they please each time, we will lose even battles we could have won.”

Ho Ga-Myung nodded.

He knew that well too.

“So first, we must make them as dogs, not humans. Dogs that bark when told and wag their tails when told. Those who refuse to become dogs should be allowed to die as humans. That is true mercy. Understand?”

“...Can they really become Alliance Lord’s dogs?”

A fierce aura darkened Jang Ilso’s face.

“Of course. If those who don’t obey are all killed, and they learn that disobeying me is a fate worse than death at the enemy’s hands, humanity will disappear.”

“...”

“It will take a long time. A tedious process must be endured. But once all that is finished...”

Jang Ilso’s eyes stared at the ceiling. No, it would be more accurate to say he looked beyond the ceiling at something else.

“Only then will I earn the right to hold the world in my hands.”

Ho Ga-Myung’s hand trembled slightly. Had Jang Ilso ever spoken such words before?

“If so, I will simply follow.”

Then it must be done. Even if it means staining all of the Southern Land with blood, not just the Evil Sect.

“Yes.”

Burning desire filled Jang Ilso’s eyes. Ho Ga-Myung’s heart flared hotly as well.

Then Jang Ilso suddenly seemed to remember something and said, “Ah.”

“Before that, go bring some alcohol.”

“...”

“I need to change the helper...”

“...”

A sigh escaped from Ho Ga-Myung’s lips.

Deep dawn.

Most of the pavilions were dark by this time, but the quarters of Ho Ga-Myung, the military general of the Four Sects Alliance, were still brightly lit.

Ho Ga-Myung, sitting at a large desk looking over documents, rubbed his dry eyes and leaned back in his chair.

‘It’s not easy.’

He was also a martial artist. Ordinary matters rarely fatigued him. But nearly ten days of continuous overnight work was enough to wear even him out.

Still, the road ahead was long, and resting was out of the question.

“...Sometimes you ask for too much.”

He muttered quietly in complaint.

He fully understood Jang Ilso’s intentions. And now he perfectly understood how reasonable those intentions were.

The problem was that it was Ho Ga-Myung’s role to realize in reality the grand goal that Jang Ilso casually tossed out while drinking, saying, ‘This should work.’

Easier said than done.

This time, nearly a thousand people were executed, causing a massive personnel gap within the Pirates. The small fry didn’t matter much, but several executed were close to the core of each sect, which was a big problem.

They had to minimize the administrative void left by their disappearance and appoint new people to suitable positions. At the same time, they had to identify and efficiently reorganize all the inefficient and lax operations within each sect.

Not only that. The factions divided among the Black Ghost Fort, the Ten Thousand People Clan, and the Pirates had to be unified and coordinated.

This was a brutal task incomparable to creating a new sect.

In the past, those who saw Ho Ga-Myung’s workload during the Ten Thousand People Clan era were amazed. But now, he had to streamline the Four Sects Alliance, which were several times larger than the Ten Thousand People Clan, while also dealing with the aftermath of Jang Ilso’s eccentric actions.

Who could do this? Even competent ministers in the imperial palace would have fled within days.

Yet Ho Ga-Myung endured all these tasks silently without complaint.

If Chung Myung knew this, he might have forgotten the past and tearfully begged Ho Ga-Myung to work together.

Frankly, Ho Ga-Myung was handling everything that Chung Myung, Hyun Young, Hyun Sang, Un Am, and even the Eunha Merchant Guild from Mount Hua would have done.

Given the situation, even as Jang Ilso gave a casual gesture and fell into a deep sleep at this deep dawn, Ho Ga-Myung had no choice but to remain alone handling the work.

The Four Sects Alliance could not exist without Jang Ilso, and could not operate without Ho Ga-Myung.

‘Let’s see.’

Ho Ga-Myung reached out and pulled the report that had just arrived from Lower District Sect in front of him.

Even while reorganizing the system, he must not miss information. Information is the most precious thing. And the information sent to Jang Ilso is even more important.

Receiving, sorting, and forwarding only the core of such information that cannot be entrusted to others to Jang Ilso was also one of Ho Ga-Myung’s duties.

“Beast Palace and Ice Palace...”

Moreover, the report just received concerns none other than the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Recently, information about the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s movements is classified as top secret even within the Four Sects Alliance.

“What on earth are they trying to do by calling all these guys together?”

Ho Ga-Myung frowned and muttered.

This is not an efficient decision at all. There’s no war about to break out. But calling so many people to the Yangtze River costs a fortune just to feed and house them.

Moreover, gathering people with different temperaments inevitably causes conflicts and accidents. The Four Sects Alliance are currently struggling with this problem and shedding much blood.

Yet they provoke disputes by doing unnecessary things? Anyone would see this as foolish.

“Damn it...”

A hollow sigh escaped from Ho Ga-Myung’s lips.

Still, the reason he couldn’t dismiss this as foolishness was one: the person behind this was Chung Myung.

‘Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.’

Though unwilling to admit it, the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was beyond Ho Ga-Myung’s comprehension.

Of course, Ho Ga-Myung knew he was not an outstanding strategist. To ordinary people, he might seem excellent, but on a battlefield twisted by monsters, even he was just an ordinary, maybe even dull, human.

That much, the battlefield where they fought was a realm no ordinary person could enter.

Ho Ga-Myung habitually pressed his temples.

‘I never really resented that fact.’

Because his strengths lay elsewhere.

His value was not in strategy, but in administration, management, and the execution to realize Jang Ilso’s words. Though many existed in the world, only Ho Ga-Myung could satisfy Jang Ilso with his work.

Ho Ga-Myung took pride in this fact.

But only at times like this... he felt frustrated by his inability to see the complex battles behind the scenes among them. Yes, only at times like this.

“...Annoying.”

Ho Ga-Myung muttered softly and moved the report he was holding to the left. The reports on the left were those he couldn’t handle himself and had to forward to Jang Ilso.

Recently, especially after the calamity in Hangzhou, reports related to the Heavenly Friends Alliance were often moved to the left.

‘That much... it must have been impressive.’

Ho Ga-Myung’s eyes darkened.

Frankly, it was not Jang Ilso or the Four Sects Alliance who resolved the Hangzhou calamity and killed the high priest of that heretical sect, but the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

The figure he showed that day and the strategy he executed left an indelible scar in Ho Ga-Myung’s mind.

Ho Ga-Myung was a cold man, and that applied to himself as well. He never overestimated himself. He admitted he could not handle Chung Myung with his intellect.

‘Still, I don’t understand.’

The only reason Ho Ga-Myung silently obeyed Jang Ilso’s unreasonable order to execute those showing signs of resistance was one.

There was no time to accept and reform them all now. War was not far off. So they chose to cut out the wound before it festered.

What about the Heavenly Friends Alliance? What Chung Myung is doing now is like inflicting more wounds on himself all over his body. What on earth is he thinking?

‘Could it be he’s planning a pre-emptive strike before the problem grows?’

Ho Ga-Myung chuckled. Even as a passing thought, it was nonsense.

“There is a reason. There must be a reason for it. But it’s a reason I find hard to guess...”

Ho Ga-Myung chuckled softly.

He was human after all. Humans cannot understand why or how monsters fight each other. They can only guess superficially.

The fight between Jang Ilso and Chung Myung was that kind of thing to Ho Ga-Myung. He only wished not to be a burden to Jang Ilso and hoped Jang Ilso would achieve his great purpose.

“I’ve been lost in thought for too long.”

Without hesitation, Ho Ga-Myung tossed the report aside and grabbed a new one. To finish the tasks before dawn, even idle thoughts were a luxury.

But despite his urgency, his thoughts did not easily turn to the new report.

‘Mount Hua’s Sword Saint...’

Lately, thinking of him made his chest feel tight.

That Mount Hua’s Sword Saint... though he didn’t want to admit it, was surprisingly similar to Jang Ilso. Completely different yet so alike. That meant the Sword Saint was truly formidable.

‘He must be confident he can handle it.’

A sigh escaped Ho Ga-Myung’s lips.

Anyway, their actions must have a purpose, and that purpose likely stems from the Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s confidence.

‘But in the end, we will be the ones who are right, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.’

Ho Ga-Myung glanced toward the north of the river, bit his lip slightly, and then refocused on the documents.

There was no time to rest if he wanted to realize the will of Jang Ilso and himself.

In the quarters of Chung Myung, whom Ho Ga-Myung tried to interpret cautiously.

Similarly, Chung Myung sat at a large desk, rubbing his haggard face and tearing at his hair.

At that moment, the door burst open urgently.

“Chung Myung!”

Chung Myung, with half his soul drained, looked at the person who rushed in. Seeing Baek Cheon’s face, he muttered in a dying voice.

“What... again...”

“There’s another fight!”

“...”

“The Tang family and Namgung are fighting!”

“...Why?”

No, what kind of combination is that?

“I don’t know! They suddenly started fighting while eating.”

“...Try to stop them moderately.”

“They drew swords?”

“...”

“Do something! The building might get destroyed.”

“Well...”

Just as Chung Myung was about to speak, Jo Gul pushed past Baek Cheon and poked his head inside.

“Chung Myung! The Green Forest and our guys are fighting.”

“...Then stop them.”

“No, the sasuks are fighting too, so they won’t listen to me! ...Oh? Baek Cheon sasuk! Why are you here? You should be stopping the other sasuk, not here.”

“You do it! I have to stop Namgung and Tang family now!”

“No, those are other people! Our guys are beating the Green Forest like rats!”

“If our guys are beating them, it doesn’t matter. They’re not the ones getting hit.”

“Huh?”

Jo Gul muttered absentmindedly, realizing his mistake.

“Now that I think about it, yeah? Why did I run here then?”

“...”

Half of Chung Myung’s remaining soul drained away again.

Why is that okay? You crazy bastards.

But his ordeal was not over yet.

“Sahyuuuuuung!”

Chung Myung looked blankly at Tang Soso, who poked her face beside Jo Gul, and muttered.

“...Why? Are the Ice Palace and Beast Palace fighting?”

“No! Sahyung, that’s not it!”

“Then what?”

“The beasts brought from the Beast Sect escaped the warehouse and attacked the villages!”

“...Why are you just watching then? You have to catch them!”

“We don’t have enough hands! The other sahyung are fighting the Green Forest bandits now.”

“What about the Beast Palace?”

“They’re fighting the Ice Palace now.”

“Huh?”

Chung Myung stared blankly at Tang Soso.

“You said they weren’t fighting just now...?”

“I meant it’s none of my business. Whether they fight or not, I don’t care.”

“...”

“They were so busy fighting that they didn’t feed them on time, so some escaped. It seems hard to control with just the remaining people. Can I kill a couple as an example?”

Melted Chung Myung slumped slowly into his chair.

“Hey, what do we do about this?”

“The fights keep breaking out.”

“Hey. Maybe it was a mistake to let them rest? Since there’s no official sparring, they have leftover energy and fight among themselves.”

Chung Myung laughed hollowly at the nagging voices and stared blankly at the ceiling, muttering.

“You know.”

“Yeah. What should we do?”

“All...”

“All?”

“I wish they all died.”

“...”

“You bastards...”

A tear rolled down Chung Myung’s cheek.

Harmony? What a joke.

Oh dear, Chung Mun sahyung.

This is hell, this place. Oh dear.

“Nooooo!”

A loud voice shouted from the temporary sect leader’s residence. Those passing by clicked their tongues and shook their heads.

“It’s starting again.”

“Anyway, that Chung Myung guy never has a quiet day.”

“That’s a talent too. A talent.”

Laughter mixed with concern briefly poured toward the quarters, but that was all. No one was surprised. This was a daily occurrence at Mount Hua, and wherever Mount Hua stayed.

“Does this even make sense! Huh?”

One who was about to quicken his steps with a bitter smile at the outburst suddenly stopped and tilted his head.

“Wait. Is that really Chung Myung?”

“Huh? What do you mean? If not Chung Myung, then who can it be?”

“The voice sounds different though?”

“Voice?”

Hearing that, they looked back toward Hyun Jong’s quarters. Without even needing to listen closely, a tremendous shout erupted.

“I’m so pissed off! Huh? I am!”

“......”

For a moment, everyone’s faces turned strange.

“Right?”

“Yeah. This, this isn’t Chung Myung’s voice?”

“Then who is it?”

“Isn’t that the sect leader’s voice?”

“Huh?”

Everyone was startled and stared blankly toward the quarters.

“Say something, say something!”

“Ahem.”

“Hmm.”

“Ugh.”

Those gathered before Hyun Jong subtly averted their gaze.

Tang Gunak, Meng So, Im So-Byeong, Namgung Do-Wei, and even a representative who was sitting in place of Seol So-baek for some reason, dared not meet Hyun Jong’s eyes.

“When the kids hit and fight each other and cause chaos!”

“......”

“The sect leaders should stop it! Are you just watching?”

“...Uh, um... Alliance Leader.”

Meng So gave an embarrassed smile.

“That’s all something Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is supposed to handle...”

“Well said!”

“Huh?”

“Can’t you see over there? There?”

Hyun Jong, eyes half-rolled back, pointed fiercely to the side. Where he pointed, Chung Myung, now pale like a corpse, was slumped against the wall.

‘What, what is that... Has he starved himself for ten days?’

‘How did the shadows under his eyes reach down to his chin?’

‘Isn’t he going to die like that?’

“Is that even a human figure? That guy came laughing, walking on two feet after beating up the high priest! But look at how he looks now!”

“......”

“They’ve made someone else’s precious son look like that? These people, I swear!”

“P-please calm down, Sect Leader.”

Hyun Young, sweating nervously, stopped Hyun Jong who was rolling up his sleeves.

“Calm down? Do I look like I can calm down?”

Baek Cheon opened his mouth wide at this bizarre situation.

‘I am living to the day to see this. To think I’d see this kind of scene.’

Hyun Jong taking Chung Myung’s side and getting angry at others, while Hyun Young tries to calm him down—how could this make sense unless the world was turned upside down?

Hyun Jong pushed away the clinging Hyun Young and glared fiercely at Tang Gunak.

“Say something, Tang family lord!”

“K-khem.”

“I understand using poison in duels! But! They used poison not in a duel but because of a quarrel? Are they trying to pick a fight with Mount Hua now?”

“Th-that part I cannot comment on. But that poison wasn’t the absolute poison and I already revoked...”

“Ah, so you think beating someone up and then treating them later makes it okay?”

“That’s not what I meant, but...”

Tang Gunak, seemingly at a loss for words, averted his gaze. Surely he didn’t expect poison to be used during a fight in the diner, not a duel.

If this had happened with another sect, it wouldn’t be surprising if it ended in a fight to the death. But since Mount Hua was the victim, it was overlooked to this extent.

“And, Beast Palace Lord!”

“Yes, Alliance Leader.”

“How on earth did you manage those beasts to let them rampage into villages like this!”

“Ahahaha!”

Meng So laughed heartily as if it was nothing.

“That’s why they’re called beasts, aren’t they? If they knew how to be controlled, they’d be humans.”

But Hyun Jong had no intention of letting that joke slide. With bloodshot eyes, he openly glared at Meng So.

“Are you laughing right now?”

Meng So lowered his gaze slightly.

“Well said! If you know beasts are like that, you should manage them! What were the Palace people doing just watching the beasts run wild?”

“I have no comment on that...”

“Those bastards went into villages, ate all the food people had, and devoured the livestock. Chung Myung even got his eardrum damaged while protesting! His eardrum!”

“He looks fine though...”

“What?”

“Ah, nothing.”

Meng So shrugged his shoulders, uncharacteristically small for his large frame. They say gentle people are scarier when angry, and this was exactly that.

“And!”

Hyun Jong’s fiery gaze now fixed on Im So-Byeong. But at that moment, Im So-Byeong took the initiative.

“Wait a moment!”

“...Hmm?”

He confidently opened his fan wide.

“It seems you’re blaming us, but if you look closely, aren’t we the ones wronged?”

“Did you just say you’re wronged?”

“Yes! This time, those Tang guys started the quarrel first. Even though we’re lowly Evil Sect bastards, isn’t it their fault for provoking and spitting first? Should we just take the hits?”

“Well...”

Hyun Jong hesitated as if at a loss for words, and Im So-Byeong seized the opportunity to press on.

“Alliance Leader, aren’t we the ones who decided to wash our hands clean and live properly? Then you should embrace us with kindness, not keep discriminating against us and Tang guys...”

“That’s not it!”

“...Yes?”

“Officials came and went!”

“...Officials?”

Im So-Byeong tilted his head as if puzzled by the sudden remark. His confidently spread fan drooped slightly.

Hyun Jong openly showed his displeasure and continued speaking.

“Yes. Recently, there was a brawl at a gambling den in the back alleys of river mouth. The gamblers beat up all the staff and even robbed the safe before fleeing.”

“...Ah, no!”

Im So-Byeong shouted with a face full of injustice.

“What does that have to do with us?”

“...They say the ones involved wore green clothes with a tree symbol on the right chest.”

“Wow, they wore that? Crazy bastards...”

Bang!

Hyun Jong slammed the table with all his might.

“They came to help the commoners and then gamble? Gamble? And fine, gambling is one thing! Why rob a perfectly fine gambling den? Why!”

Im So-Byeong gave an embarrassed smile.

“Well... we didn’t rob innocent commoners. If it’s the gambling den operators, they’re like leeches sucking the blood of the commoners, so robbing them a bit...”

“Then you should have shared the money! That money all came from the commoners! What happened to that money?”

“...I’ll look into it.”

“Bah!”

Hyun Jong raised his hand, and Im So-Byeong ducked as if something was flying at him, then sheepishly raised his head.

Hyun Jong had hit his own chest. The pounding hand was full of frustration as if his insides were burning.

“Ice Palace!”

“S-sorry.”

Han Yi-Myung, already half intimidated, slowly backed away as soon as the word ‘Ice Palace’ was mentioned.

“Why isn’t the Palace Lord coming?”

“...There are some circumstances he has...”

“Ugh.”

Hyun Jong sighed deeply and glared at Han Yi-Myung again.

“Let’s say other places are like that. You asked not to fight unless during duels, but how can even Ice Palace be fighting? You said you’d listen, but I heard Ice Palace attacked Beast Palace first! What’s going on here?”

“Th-that has a story behind it.”

“What story?”

Han Yi-Myung answered, sweating profusely.

“Actually, our Palace Lord did his best to prevent private fights as requested by taoist Chung Myung. Yesterday, upon hearing a quarrel between Beast Palace and the Ice Palace, he rushed to mediate.”

“The Palace Lord tried to stop it?”

“Yes! You know our Palace Lord wouldn’t break Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s request. Of course, he tried to stop it!”

“...Then what happened? I clearly heard Ice Palace attacked Beast Palace first.”

“Well... our Palace Lord told both sides to stop, saying ‘Taoist Chung Myung said not to fight.’ But one reckless Beast Palace member said in front of the Palace Lord, ‘I don’t care what that Taoist said,’ so...”

“...So?”

Han Yi-Myung squeezed his eyes shut.

“Well... isn’t it obvious? Our Palace Lord, enraged by the ‘Taoist’ remark, beat that Beast Palace member without question...”

Hearing that, Five Swords applauded.

“Kuh. Our So-Baek has really grown.”

“He even beat a Beast Palace member. Wow, his skills must have improved a lot...”

“Shut up, you guys!”

Hyun Jong’s roar made Five Swords shrink his shoulders. Taking a deep breath, Hyun Jong barely suppressed his anger and asked.

“So that’s why he didn’t come here?”

“Ah, no. He went wild and got beaten up badly, so he’s bedridden now.”

“B-beat the Palace Lord?”

Hyun Jong blinked and looked at Meng So, who awkwardly rolled his eyes and scratched his nose bridge.

“He was so young, we didn’t realize he was the Palace Lord...”

“Didn’t you see him before today?”

“Well... I only saw him briefly...”

They really didn’t recognize him well. Those crazy Beast Palace guys.

“So? Anyway, every sect fought without exception?”

“...Sorry.”

“We have no excuse to say.”

“Meanwhile, the beasts raided villages, and the back alleys where beasts didn’t go were robbed by bandits?”

“Ahem.”

“Ehem...”

“Even if the Four Sects Alliance’s main base was here, this chaos wouldn’t happen! Hey, you...”

“Hey! Sect Leader! Sect Leader!”

“Decorum! You must keep your dignity!”

Hyun Sang and Hyun Young hurriedly covered Hyun Jong’s mouth from both sides. It was extremely rude, but they couldn’t let the Heavenly Friends Alliance leader say those words.

While stopping Hyun Jong, Hyun Young spoke sharply instead.

“How much chaos must have been caused for Chung Myung to lose his mind! I’ve never seen him bedridden before! Do you even have any sense or not!”

Everyone subtly turned their heads to look at Chung Myung.

Seeing him look like he’d given up on everything, a subtle guilt arose, and everyone grimaced.

“Can’t you apologize quickly?”

“Ahem. Sorry, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Sorry, Taoist.”

“...I’m sorry.”

Hearing the apologies, Chung Myung smiled broadly. His face looked like he was dying, and the bright smile gave off a creepy feeling, but a smile was still a smile...

“Just go and die, you bastards!”

“Ah! Chung Myung!”

“Hold on, Chung Myung!”

“They are sect leaders! You crazy bastard!”

Chung Myung suddenly rolled his eyes and started rampaging, and Five Swords immediately rushed in to pin him down.

“Let go! Sect leader? Sect leaders should go freeze and die! What kind of sect leaders are those!”

“C-calm down!”

Chung Myung even foamed at the mouth and convulsed.

“I won’t do it! I won’t, you bastards! Heavenly Friends Alliance or whatever, I’ll do it all myself! I won’t do it!”

The desperate scream of one crushed by the disaster he brought upon himself echoed mournfully... truly mournfully throughout the manor.
